2. tolporuḷ āyviyal
ARCHAEOLOGY
āyviyalil putiya oḷi
tolporuḷ āyviyal eṉpatu makkaḷiṉ paḻimic ceytikaḷippaṟṟiya aṟiviyal āyvucārnta ariyatuṟi eṉap poruḷtarum. muṟkālattil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷiyum, uṇavu viḷittaliṉ taṉmikaḷiyum, āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkum vaḻikaḷiyum, vīṭu, araṇmaṉi māḷiki, araṇ kaḷañciyam kaṭṭum muṟikaḷiyum, nāṭu nakaram amikkum tiṟaṉiyum, uyirtuṟantāriṉ uṭalkaḷi aṭakkam ceyyum vaḻimuṟikaḷiyum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi virivāka āyvu ceytu varukiṟatu. iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi ulaka meṅkum paravi virivaṭintuḷḷatu.
mēlum aḵtu aṟiviyal pāṅkāka āḻntu āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟatu. ittuṟi, ātiyil nilatti akaḻntu āyvu ceyyum muṟiyi (Excavation) aṭippaṭiyāyk koṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu teḷivu.
appāl, attuṟiyil atikat tolivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷi lum kāṭukaḷilum malikaḷilum kukikaḷilum paḻamporuḷkaḷ putintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉavā? eṉpatik kāṇa āyvuppayaṇam (Exploration) naṭattutal eṉṉum virivāṉa muṟi eḻuntatu. ip poḻutu nīṇṭa neṭunīrppayaṇam ceytu, āyvukkappal (Research ship), nīrmūḻki (Submarine) mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ aṭittaḷam vari ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa mali, ceṭi, koṭi, pāci, pavaḷam, vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiyum paḻaṅkālattil kaṭalāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa nāṭu, nakaram, vīṭu, araṇ, karuvikaḷ, ammi, ural tiriki pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum kaṇṭupiṭittu āyum kaṭal āyvu (Oceanography) eṉṉum putiya āyvu muṟiyum arumpi vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ (Historians) cevivaḻi marapuric ceyti kaḷiyum, ēṭu, cempuppaṭṭayam, kalveṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiyum āyntu muṟkāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷiyum āyntu nūlākkam ceytuḷḷaṉar. māṉiṭa iyal aṟiñarkaḷ (Anthropologist) paṇṭik kāla makkaḷiṉ uṭal amippu, elumpukaḷiṉ amippu, vaṇṇappolivu, tōṟṟam, vāḻkkimuṟi, paṇ pāṭṭu iyal, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, camutāya amippu (Social Structure) uṇavu, uṭi, aṇikalaṉ, toḻil, ēṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi eppaṭippaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu paṇṭikkāla makkaḷiṉ kiviṉip poruḷkaḷ (Handicrafts) eppaṭippaṭṭaṉa? avaṟṟi avarkaḷ eppaṭi uruvākkiṉar? evvāṟu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar? avaṟṟāl nām aṟiyakkūṭiyateṉṉa? eṉpavaṟṟiyellām teḷivāy āyntu uṇmiyi ulakiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
1. tolporuḷ āyvu, 2. varalāṟṟiyal, 3. māṉiṭa iyal (maṉita uṭal, uḷam iraṇṭum cārnta muḻu varalāṟṟu āyvut tuṟi) ākiya mūṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mika neruṅkiya toṭar puṭiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; ivi oṉṟukkoṉṟu uṟutuṇi yāy nilavukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu paṇṭikkāla makkaḷiṉ pāṅkāṉa varalāṟṟit tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉ nērāṉa tuṇi yiṉṟic cīrākak kāṇamuṭiyātu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. muṟkāla ilakkiyam, paṇṭikkāla nāṭu, nakaram, ciṟṟūr, iṉam, vilaṅku, paṟavi, makkaḷ, karuvi taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷi nāṭutalē muṟiyākum eṉpati nām maṟantuviṭak kūṭātu.
iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvu iyal aṟiñarkaḷ, varalāṟ ṟiyal vallunarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum allatu payilum nūlkaḷi nuṇukiyaṟiyap putiya muṟiyil oḷikāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl avi putiya polivum, poṉṟāc cāṉṟum peṟṟup pukaḻuṭaṉ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ poṟik kappaṭṭu, putikkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷiyum, ceppēṭukaḷi yum paṭikkalaṉkaḷiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum piṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum nakaraṅkaḷiyum vīṭukaḷi yum araṇkaḷiyum akaḻntu āyntu aṟintu varalāṟṟaṟiñar kaḷukkup pala putiya ceytikaḷi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kallil allatu cemputtakaṭṭil allatu kaḷimaṇ takaṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum ēṭukaḷilum variyappaṭṭa kāṇaṟkaritāyk karutappeṟum karuvūlaṅkaḷiyum akaḻntu eṭuttut tuppuravu ceytu avaṟṟil poṟikkappaṭṭa puriyāp putirākat tikaḻum poṉṟā eḻuttukkaḷi āyvu ceyyum aṟiñarkaḷ (Epigraphist) mūlam paṭittaṟintu varalāṟṟāciriyar kaḷukku uṇmiyāṉa ceytikaḷi urikkiṉṟaṉar.
eḻuttukkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭa toṭakka kālattil makkaḷ kaṇṭa varivaṭivam ōviya eḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. intap paṭa eḻuttup pala vakippaṭum. (1) pāpilōṉiya cumēriya aciriya makkaḷ eḻutiya āppu eḻuttukkaḷ (Cuneiform writings) (2) ekiptil uḷḷa makkaḷ kaṇṭacol eḻuttiṟku īṭāka eḻunta, poruḷ vaṭivap paṭa eḻuttukkaḷ (Hieroglyphs) (3) cintuveḷit tirāviṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ payiṉṟa paṭa eḻuttukkaḷ (Logograph, Logosyllophic or pictographic writings) eṉappaṭum. inta ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ, viḷaṅkāp putirāka iruntaṉa. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar (Archeologist) paṭa eḻuttukkaḷip paṭikkum paṉmoḻip pulavarkaḷiṭam inta eḻuttukkaḷi oppuvittup poruḷ viḷakkam kēṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ avaṟṟi alaci āyntu kaṇakkiṭum nuṇpoṟiyiṉ (Computer) tuṇikoṇṭu pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ payiṉṟa, paṭa eḻuttukkaḷi, paṉṉāḷ payiṉṟu āyntu, maṟikuṟi eḻuttumūlattiṉ poruḷviḷakkam (Decipher) kaṇṭaṉar. itaṉāl, akaḻ āyvil tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ uṟutip paṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uruvākkiya paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, kali, camayam, kaṭavuṭ paṟṟu, āṭi aṇikalaṉ, kiviṉip poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ teḷivāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar karuttukkaḷ araṇ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. varalāṟṟaṟiñar kaḷukkut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉar aṟiviyal vaḻikāṭṭi yāka viḷaṅkiṉar. intiya varalāṟu muḻumi peṟṟu viḷaṅka, cintu veḷi akaḻ āyvum ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvum peruntuṇi yāka iruntaṉa. iruḷcūḻnta paṇṭiya intiya varalāṟṟiṟku ivviru varalāṟukaḷum nalla oḷiviḷakkāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ulakil ituvari eṅkum cintuveḷiyip pōṉṟu ki.mu. 4000 āṇṭiṟku muṉṉar eḻunta pala nakaraṅkaḷi akaḻntu kaṇṭatē illi.
virivāṉa ākkappaṇi
ulakil uḷḷa ellā nāṭukaḷum, tattam nāṭukaḷil tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟiyi niṟuvic ceyalāṟṟi varumpaṭi cey tuḷḷaṉa. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟi mutalil airōppāvilē arumpiyatu. appāl āciyāvilum, amerikkāvilum ēṉiya kaṇṭaṅkaḷilum malarntuḷḷatu.
iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar palvēṟu nāṭu kaḷilum palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum āyvu naṭatti varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvari, tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar naṭattiya ciṟappāṉa akaḻāyvukaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭuvaṉa ekiptu, ellam, upitiyā, cumēriyā, kirīṭ, peru, mekcikō, cintuveḷi, ātittanallūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa akaḻ āyvukaḷē yākum. ivaṟṟil vaṭa intiyāvil uḷḷa cintuveḷi yilum teṉ intiyā vilum naṭipeṟṟa akaḻāyvukaḷ mika veṟṟi vāynta - perumik kuriya akaḻāyvukaḷākum. ivi, ulakilē mikat toṉmiyāṉa tol kuṭi makkaḷiṉ uyariya nākarikatti uṇarttuvatāka eṇṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa paṇpāṭu cintuveḷi, upitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ, mekcikō, peru mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tuḷirtteḻunta āriya nākarikaṅkaḷukkellām aṭippaṭiyāy amintuḷḷatu. ēṉ? - mūlakkaruvāka amin tirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. itu mikat toṉmiyāṉa makkaḷ irumpu ūḻiyil (Iron age) arumpiya irumpup paṇpāṭu (Iron Culture) ākum. inta ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭik kaṇṭa makkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ cintu veḷi cumēriyā ekiptu kirīṭ mutalāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟi ariya periya paṇpāṭukaḷukku aṭikōliyavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar, muṟkālattil tam āyvukkuriya iṭam īteṉak kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, mikunta iṭarp paṭṭuk kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ cevivaḻi marapuric ceytikaḷiyum ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷiyum vittu āyvukkuriya iṭam īteṉa muṭivuceytu vantaṉar. itil avarkaḷ pala ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷi aṭintaṉar; eṇṇaṟṟa tōlvikaḷiyum eytiṉar. iviyaṉṟi vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟku aṭippaṭiyiṭa, nilattit tōṇṭum poḻutum payir ceyya nilatti uḻum poḻutum, kuḷam kuṭṭi, kālvāy mutaliyavaṟṟit tōṇṭumpoḻutum nilattiṉ aṭiyil paḻaṅkācukaḷ, cilikaḷ, tātup paṭimaṅkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, tāḻikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar. malikkuki kaḷil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar.
ivaṟṟiyellām vittut tolporuḷ āyviṟkuriya iṭam īteṉak kaṇṭaṉar. muṟkālaṅkaḷil ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷi aṟintu aṅkuc ceṉṟu akaḻ āyvu ceyvataṟ kuriya iṭaṅkaḷi aṟintu, aṅku akaḻntu nilattiṉuḷ paḻam poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavā, illiyā? eṉṟu āyvukaḷ ceytuvaruvar. aṅkup paḻamporuḷ iruppatum uṇṭu; illā mal ēmāṟit tirumpuvatum uṇṭu. iṉṟu, appaṭic ceyvatilli.
aṟiviyal muṟi vaḷarcci eytiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu miṉ cātaṉaṅkaḷ mūlam nilattiṉ mēṟpakutiyiṉ mītu niṉṟu koṇṭē, uḷḷē paḻamporuḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavā, illiyā? eṉṟu eḷitil kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭalkaḷilkūṭak kappalkaḷ mītu iruntu koṇṭu nīriṉ aṭiyil āḻamāṉa iṭattil irukkum paḻam poruḷkaḷiyum, pāṟikaḷ uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, ceṭi koṭikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum paṭampiṭittu varukiṉṟaṉar. mākaṭalil nīriṉuḷḷirukkum nilattip paṭam eṭuttu āyum iyal (Oceanography) pōṉṟa pala ariya nuṇṇiyal vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṉṟu maṉitaṉ taṉ āṟṟali vīṇākac celavu ceyyāmal payaṉtarum muṟiyil maṉita āṟṟalic celaviṭa vaḻikōlap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
akaḻ āyvut tuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭōr, nilattit tōṇṭum poḻutu nilattiṉ aṭiyil irukkum poruḷkaḷō vīṭō, ciliyō, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷō, kūlapporuḷkaḷō eviyāka iruppiṉum avi ciṟitum cētamuṟātu tōṇṭi eṭuppar. maṇṇuḷḷirukkum tuṇiyō maṇiyō, tātup poruḷō (Metals) cāmpalō, pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ putiyuṇṭu uḷḷiruntu makkip pōyiruntālum avaṟṟi aṇuvaḷavum cētamuṟāmal uṉṉippāka uṟṟuṇarntu akaḻntu eṭukkiṉṟaṉar.
ceyaṟkaruvi (Artifact)
tolporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉar akaḻntu kaṇṭavi, nakaram veḷippuṟak kōṭṭi (Fort), nakarattiṟku aṇmiyil uḷḷa araṇ (Citadel), araṇmaṉi, māḷiki vīṭu, kūlakkaḷañciyam, niṉivucciṉṉam, kōyil, toḻiṟcāli, kallaṟi, putikuḻi, piṇappāṉi (tāḻi), maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, tātu poruḷkaḷ - ulōkap poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟaṉavāka irukkalām; avi ciṟiyaṉa vākavum periyaṉavākavum irukkalām. allatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ, viḷakku, kiṇṇam, keṇṭi, katti, arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, tūṇṭil, curaṇṭi, karaṇṭi, ūci, muḷvāṅki, kātu kuṭivāṉ, iṭukki, paḻaṅkācu, tuṇi, kūlapporuḷ, umi pōṉṟaṉavākavum irukkalām. makkippōṉa nellum, iṟṟuppōṉa pullum, karaṭu muraṭāṉa kaṟ karuviyum tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉarukku iṉṟiyamiyāta karuvūlamākak karutappaṭum. ivaṟṟi avarkaḷ ceyaṟkaruvikaḷ (Artifacts) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolporuḷ āyvuvallunarkaḷ ceyaṟ karuvikaḷ mūlam, ivaṟṟip paṭittup payaṉpaṭuttiya muṟkāla mutumakkaḷiṉ kittiṟaṉ, aṟivuttiṟaṉ, pōrttiṟaṉ, āṇmi, āṟṟal, vāḻkki muṟi, vaḻipaṭu teyvam, camayam, camūka uṟavu, caṭaṅku muṟi, ūṇ, uṭi, paṇpu, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟi yellām aṟikiṉṟārkaḷ.
puvi aṭukkiyal (Stratigraphy)
tolporuḷ āyviyalār nilatti akaḻntu āyvu ceyyat toṭaṅkum poḻutu, catura vaṭivil ceṅkuttāka nilattit tōṇṭi uḷḷirukkum eḷiya poruḷiyum viṭṭuviṭāmal kippaṟṟik kuḻiyil kiṭikkum poruḷ enta aṭukku variciyil ettaṉi yaṭiyiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kuṟittu vittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷit tōṇṭumpoḻutu maṉita varalāṟṟik kuṟippiṭum ūḻik kālattiṟkuriya palavakik kaṟkaruvi kaḷum, tātup poruḷkaḷum, ēṭukaḷum, eḻuttukkaḷum kiṭik kiṉṟaṉa.
eṉavē, avaṟṟi varicippaṭuttit tokuttuvik kiṉṟaṉar. nilattit tōṇṭumpoḻutu uṭpuṟam, nilattiluḷḷa maṇ aṭukkaṭukkāy (Layers) kāṇappaṭum. tōṇṭum poḻutu ovvōr aṭukkilum oru ciṟu kucciyi aṭittu ataṉ aṭukku eṇkaḷiyum, āḻattiṉ aṭiyi allatu muḻattiyum kāṭṭum oru vācakatti ōr aṭṭiyil eḻutit toṅka viṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. appaṭi aṭivari tōṇṭi eṭutta piṉ, ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiyum, ataṉ āḻattiyum pativu ceytukoḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. piṉṉar ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiyum pirittu vakippaṭutti maṭ pāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷ, tātupporuḷkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, elumpukaḷ, pāci pavaḷaṅkaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ eṉṟu vakuttut tokuttu vakippaṭutti vikkiṉṟārkaḷ. appāl ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukkuk kālam kaṇikkiṉ ṟārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam puvi aṭukkiyal eṉṉum āyvu muṟi urup peṟṟuḷḷatu. akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa poruḷkaḷi aṟiviyal ārāycci mūlam (Carbon 14 test), poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālatti aḷaviṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭivam amippu aḻaku nili cāyam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam kālam kaṇippatum uṇṭu.
putiya āyvu muṟi
amerikka aṟiñar āṇṭrū-iṭaklacu maravaḷiya muṟi eṉṟa oru putiya āyvu muṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. oru kaṭṭiṭattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa marattil kāṇappaṭum vaḷiyaṅkaḷi vittu akkaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa kālattik kaṇippatākum. itu kaṭṭiṭattiṉ kālattit tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kaṇṭu piṭikkum putu muṟiyākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.
maṟṟoru muṟi paṇṭik kālattiluḷḷa kallaṟikaḷilum vīṭukaḷilumiruntu akaḻntu kaṇṭa kūlap poruḷkaḷ, kāykaṟikaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntu pala ceytikaḷic cēkarikkum muṟiyākum.
piṟitorumuṟi, paṇṭik kālattut tātup poruḷkaḷi iracāyaṉa muṟiyil pakuttu, aḵtu evvāṟu tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu aṟitalākum. iṉṉoru muṟi uyir nūl aṟiñarkaḷ paṇṭik kālac citivukaḷiṉiṉṟu kiṭikkum elumpukaḷ taliyōṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmūlam, kālam, iṉam, pirivu pōṉṟavaṟṟi aṟiviyal muṟiyil kaṇṭupiṭittalākum.1
āḻnta āyviyal
iṉṟiya makkaḷ paṇṭiya makkaḷiviṭap pala tuṟikaḷil ārvaṅkāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatu mey. ciṟappāka nammuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, moḻi, uṇavu, uṭi, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, karuvikaḷ, kippaṇikaḷ, vāḻkki muṟi, paṇpuppāṅku, nākarika nayam mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟiyap peritum nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭik kāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷiyum avarkaḷuṭiya karuvikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum aṟiya iṉṟiya aṟiviyal, nalla vaḻi vakuttuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ ceyta kippaṇikaḷik kāṇa ariya paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṟatu. iṉṟu ulakiluḷḷa aṉittu nāṭukaḷilum tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟikaḷ niṟuvap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
inta āyvuttuṟi muṉṉar nām vāḻum nilatti maṭṭum akaḻntu āyntu vantatu. appāl mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ aṭiyil mūḻkik kiṭakkum kōṇṭ vāṉā, lemūriya kaṇṭaṅkaḷiyum, nāvalan tīvu pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum avaṟṟil uḷḷa pāṟikaḷ uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum āyntatu. kappalkaḷ, nīrmūḻkik kalaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉ āḻkaṭaliṉ aṭit taḷaṅkaḷiyellām kaṇṭu āyntu varukiṉṟatu. eṉ matippiṟkum aṉpiṟkum uriya cōviyat nāṭṭu aṟiñar  a. kōntrottōv mūṉṟu mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tamiḻarkaḷiṉ mūtātikaḷ muṉṉāḷil nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amittut teṉ maturiyil mutaṟ caṅkam niṟuvi muṭi puṉintu, aracu kaṭṭilil amarntu ceṅkōl tāṅki, erutuk koṭi ēṟṟiya katikaḷi yellām mey eṉṟu ulakiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭa muyaṟci ceytuḷḷār eṉṟu terikiṟatu. āḻkaṭal aṟivā rāycci toṭakka niliyil iruntu varukiṟatu. āṉāl, atu virivil anta ariya uṇmikaḷi ulakiṟku uṇarttum eṉṟu tam nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2 inta intumāk kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷa āyvuttuṟi vicrānti eṉṉum ciṟappāṉa kappal mūlam uruciya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu 7 allatu 8 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ 6 laṭcam kal tolivup payaṇam ceytu āyvu naṭatti uruciya moḻiyil 11kku mēṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉi mūṉṟu māk kaṭaliṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil 1974 il kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷār. ataṟkup piṉ inta āḻkaṭal āyvu muṟi evvaḷavō vaḷarcci aṭintuḷḷatu. avaṟṟiṉ vivaram namakku iṉṟuvari kiṭikkavilli.
āṉāl inta āyvu muṟi intiyāvilum ataṉ aṇṭi nāṭukaḷilum vaḷaravilli. araca pīṭattil amarntiruppavarkaḷ varalāṟṟāyviṉ, mutaṉmiyi uṇarntavarkaḷāka illi. talivarkaḷil cilar nāṭṭil vaṟumi, vēliyillāt tiṇ ṭāṭṭam, vēli niṟuttam, māṇavarkaḷ kiḷarcci mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟi varalāṟṟu āyviṟkup paṇam celaviṭak kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ittakiya mara maṇṭikaḷ peruki varum pōkūḻ (turatiruṣṭa) niliyil paḻaṅkāla nāṭṭiṉ pāṅki aṟintu ataṉ vīḻcciyi uṇarntu putiya nāṭṭi vaḷamārnta iḷamip polivu ceṟiyum intiyāvi uruvākka nāṭum iḷiñar ulakam tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟi cīrpeṟa ilaṅka namatu nāṭṭit tūṇṭa vēṇṭum. pārāḷumaṉṟattiṟkum caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷukkum palkalip paṭṭam peṟṟa paṇpāḷarkaḷi uṟuppiṉarkaḷākat tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum. aṟivu paṭitta putiya nākarika intiyāvi uruvākka muyala vēṇṭum.
niṟka, paṇṭikkāla makkaḷuṭiya vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷi nām naṉkaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa āval pāpilōṉiyāvil ōr aracaṉukkuk ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil arumpiyatām. uṭaṉē taṉ nāṭṭil akaḻ āyvu naṭatta āṇi aḷittāṉ eṉṟu an nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. ki.mu. 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuviṉ kallaṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka an nāṭṭu makkaḷ nāṭiṉar. urōmaṉ nāṭṭu maṉṉar maṉṉaṉ mā kāṉcuṭāṇṭiṉ (Constantine the Great) eṉpavarukkum itil akkaṟi arumpiyatu. uṭaṉē avar oru tūtukkuḻuvi, pālattīṉattiṟku aṉuppik kallaṟi irukkum iṭattik kaṇṭupiṭikka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytāṉ eṉṟu oru varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. 1801ām āṇṭu elcciṉ pirapu ēteṉcu nakaril uḷḷa oru kirēkka kiṟittavak kōyilil irunta paḻiya ciṟpaṅkaḷit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭittu iṅkilāntiṟku eṭuttuc ceṉṟār.
iviyellām paḻaṅkālattilē pāpilōṉiyarkaḷukkum, urōmarkaḷukkum āṅkilēyarkaḷukkum paḻam poruḷkaḷit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum; paḻam ciṟpaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu piṭittu avaṟṟip pātukāttu vikkavēṇṭum; varalāṟṟu aṟivi vaḷarkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iruntatik kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām. aḵtiṉṟip paḻaṅkālattilē aṅkut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi muḷittu eḻak kāraṇamāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇalām; iyampalām; eḻutalām.
intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvu
intiyā paḻamperum nāṭu. teṉintiyāvilē makkaḷiṉam tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vaḷampeṟṟu uyarntatu eṉṟu ulakil aṟiñar palar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉip pērāciriyar erṉacuṭ ekkal (Ernest Heaecrel) eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.3 ati makliṉ eṉṉum aṟiñarum ātarittuḷḷār.4 kirām ulliyam pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa aṟiviyal pulavarkaḷ araṇ ceytuḷḷaṉar.5
tamiḻnāṭṭil tolpaḻaṅkal ūḻitoṭṭu (Eolithic age) paḻaṅkal ūḻi (Paleolithic age), iṭikkal ūḻi (Mesolithic age), putukkal ūḻi (Neolithic age), tātupporuḷ ūḻi (Metal age), varalāṟṟu ūḻi (Historic age) pōṉṟa ellā ūḻikaḷum maṉita iṉam tōṉṟi, karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttat terinta paṇṭik kālantoṭṭu iṉṟuvari ellāk kālaṅkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntu vantuḷḷaṉa. iti mikat teḷivākac ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟup pērāciriyarum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟip pērāciri yarumāka irunta aṟiñar tiru. vi.em. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar, em.ē. avarkaḷ tam nūlil virivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār.6 ati aṟiñarkaḷ palarum virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
tamiḻmakkaḷ - ēṉ tirāviṭa iṉam, viḷakkamākak kūṟuvata ṉāl - maṉita kulattiṉ mūtātiyarkaḷ tamiḻakattil tōṉṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭik kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, eṉap pirittu vāḻntuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu tamiḻpperum ilakkaṇanūlāka viḷaṅkum tolkāppiyam pōṉṟa paḻamperum nūlkaḷ navil kiṉṟaṉa. kuṟiñci nilamakkaḷ (villarkaḷ) maliyil vāḻntu vēṭṭuvat toḻilik kaṇṭaṉar. villip paṭikkalamākak koṇṭaṉar. mulli nilamakkaḷ (āyarkaḷ) tiṉi, kēḻvaraku mutaliya puṉceyt tāṉiyaṅkaḷip payiriṭappayiṉṟaṉar. āṭu māṭukaḷip paḻakkip pālkaṟakkavum tayir, mōr, veṇṇey pōṉṟaṉa ceyyavum paḻakikkoṇṭaṉar.
marutanilamakkaḷ nīr niṟinta kuḷakkarikaḷilum āṟṟaṅkari kaḷilum avaṟṟi aṭuttuḷḷa camaveḷikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi erutukaḷip paḻakkiṉar. kaḷimaṇ nilaṅkaḷik kaṟkaḷāl kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi naṉcey nilamākkip payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. piṉṉar ērik kaṇṭu uḻutupayiriṭṭu nelliyum eḷḷiyum ēṉiya kūlap poruḷkaḷiyum payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. in nilattilē makkaḷiṉ naṉmuyaṟciyāl pala toḻilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi vaṉital, nūlnūṟṟal, tuṇineytal, irumpukkaruvikaḷiyum marapporuḷkaḷiyum ceytal, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷi vārttal, kalli, maravi, caṭṭi, tūṇ mutaliyavaṟṟi uruvākkal, eḷvittu, tēṅkāyk koppari, āmaṇakku vittukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ney vaṭittal, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷi eṭuttup puṭamiṭṭup pacum poṉṉākki aṇikalaṉkaḷic camittal, tātup poruḷkaḷit takaṭukaḷākki avaṟṟil eḻut tāṇi koṇṭu eḻututal, vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭutal, kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ amittal, kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭutal kōpuraṅkaḷ, kōṭṭikaḷ eḻupputal mutaliya toḻilkaḷ vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa. āṭutal, pāṭutal, ōviyan tīṭṭutal, pala vaṇṇac cāyaṅkaḷi uruvākkutal, kavitikaḷ iyaṟṟal, kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ eḻututal, camaya aṟivip perukkutal pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ceyalkaḷi āṟṟivantaṉar. uḻavut toḻili vaḷarttaṉar; vaṇikam naṭattiṉar. maṟṟum paṟpala kittoḻilkaḷip pēṇi vaḷarttaṉar.
innila makkaḷ kāṭu kaṭantu, kaṭal kaṭantu, nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu pukuntu kuṭiyēṟa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. kaṭal vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭu veṟṟiyīṭṭiṉar. piṟa nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi avvan nāṭukaḷit tāyakamākak koṇṭaṉar. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa malikaḷilē taṅkaḷiṉ teyvamākiya mantiramalivācalik kaṇṭaṉar. civaneṟiyi nili nāṭṭiṉar. taṅkaḷiṉ uyariya paṇpāṭṭiyum aṟaneṟiyiyum oḻukkattiyum nākarikattiyum parappiṉar. in nilattiṟkuriya uḻukulattōr tāṅkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya nilaṅkaḷilellām payirt toḻili vaḷarttaṉar. marutanilam toḻil vaḷarccikku oru miyamāka amintatu. celvap perukkiṟku uyarnta ūṟṟāka uyarnteḻuntatu. inta vaḷamārnta nilattil mutaṉmutalākap parattiyar (Concubine) tōṉṟum vāyppu uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli.
neytalnilap paratavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭal vaṇikam vaḷarat tuṇiniṉṟaṉar; mīṉpiṭittaṉar; uppu viḷittaṉar; caṅku aṟutta ṉar; tōṇi camittaṉar; kappal kaṭṭiṉar; āḻkaṭal kaṭantu kappalkaḷic celuttum ariya kaliyit tamakkē uriyateṉak koṇṭu pēṇi vaḷarttaṉar. kaṭal āḻattiyum nīrōṭṭaṅkaḷi yum alikaḷiṉ pōkkukaḷiyum aṟintaṉar; kaṭalil vāḻum periya timiṅkilaṅkaḷiyum, koṭiya cuṟāmīṉkaḷiyum, piṭikka vaḻivakuttaṉar; kaṭalil cippikaḷiyum muttukkaḷiyum pavaḷaṅ kaḷiyum, caṅkukaḷiyum kaṇṭu eṭuttaṉar; vāṉanūṟ pulami peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻar tirikaṭalōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu eṉṟa poṉmoḻiyi naṉmoḻiyākak koṇṭu vaḷarat tamiḻnāṭṭu neytal nilap paratavarkaḷ tuṇiniṉṟaṉar eṉṟāl aḵtu oru ciṟitum mikiyākātu.
tamiḻakattu neytalnila makkaḷiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟit tamiḻar allatu tirāviṭar eṉpōr ekiptu, upitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, kirīṭ, teṉamerikkā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭu kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi irukkamuṭiyātu eṉpatu eḷitil viṭṭuviṭakkūṭiya ceyti aṉṟu.
intiyāvil ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭilum piṟa tirāviṭa nāṭukaḷilum paḻaṅkālattiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ mūtātiyar viṭṭucceṉṟa eccamiccaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākak kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiyellām innāṭṭik kavara eṇṇic cila aṉṉiya nāṭṭu aracukaḷāl poṉṉiyum poruḷiyum koṭuttu aṉuppappaṭṭa kiṟittava camayak kurumārkaḷ cilar ceṉṟa nūṟ ṟāṇṭilē uṇarntaṉar. intiya nāṭṭiṉ paḻamperum nākarikaṅ kaḷiyum camayac ciṟappiyum iṟivaḻipāṭṭiyum moḻic ciṟappiyum naṉkāyntu aṭikkaṭi ayalnāṭṭiluḷḷa aracukaḷuk kum, makkaḷukkum uṇarttivantaṉar. eṉavē airōppāvil toṭakkaniliyil irunta tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi māṇavarkaḷ. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ arumpi appāl aḻintu pōṉa intiyanāṭṭiṉ nākarikattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum nakar amipput tiṟaṉiyum, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭum muṟiyiyum, kappal kaṭṭum kaliyiyum, maruttuvam ceyyum māṇpiyum, moḻi amikkum nuṇukkattiyum, eḻuttākkum pāṅkiyum akaḻntu āya ārvamuṟṟaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi mēṉāṭṭiluḷḷa tol poruḷ āyvu vallunarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟip paḻaṅkācu cēkarippavar kaḷum, paḻiya cilikaḷ cempuppaṭimaṅkaḷ, paḻiya ōlic cuvaṭikaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ cēkarip pōrkaḷumāka aṟiñarkaḷ palar mēlināṭu kaḷiṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟkuḷ ki.pi. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvilē pukuntu pala ariya paḻamporuḷkaḷic cēkarittut tam nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōy āyntaṉar. ap poruḷkaḷit taṅkaḷ paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭampeṟacceytaṉar. iṉṟum avi pāricu, perliṉ, mācukō, ilaṇṭaṉ, ileyṭaṉ, niyūyārk, pōcuṭaṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccālikaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppatik kāṇalām.
tolporuḷ āyvu iyaliṉ toṭakkam
intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvu iyalik kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭci ki.pi. 1860ām āṇṭiṟkuc caṟṟumuṉ toṭaṅku vittatu. āṉāl 1850ām āṇṭiṟku muṉpē tirunelvēli māvaṭṭak kiṟittuva capiyil nallāyar (Bishop) ākap paṇi yāṟṟiya muṉivar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ (The Rt. Rev. Dr. Robert Caldwel, D.D., LL.D. Bishop of Tinnevelly) iṭiyaṉkuṭiyiliruntu koṇṭu tamiḻppulavarkaḷiṭam tamiḻkaṟṟup pulami eytiṉār. avar paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ talinakarāyum iṭiccaṅkam nilaviya mūtūrāyum varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉa māyum viḷaṅkiya koṟkikkum kāyalukkum ceṉṟu tol poruḷ āyvuceytuḷḷār. avar aṅku āyvu ceyyum poḻutu makkaḷ avarit taṭuttaṉar. paḻaṅkālap pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷum paḻaṅkāla vaṇikarkaḷum ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷi maṇṇil putittu vittuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟi iṉṟum pala pūtaṅkaḷ kāttu varukiṉṟaṉa. nīṅkaḷ nilatti akaḻntāl ac celvaṅkaḷik kavara varuvatāka eṇṇip pūtaṅkaḷ uṅkaḷik koṉṟu viṭum. muṉṉar māvaṭṭa atikāriyiṉ icivupeṟṟu iṅku vanta atikāri oruvar koṟkiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyya vantu iravil tam kūṭārattil paṭuttiruntār. tiṭīreṉṟu tam uṭampil ēṟpaṭṭa atircciyik kaṇṭu viḻittup pārkka, avar uṭal pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyiluḷḷa tam illattil kaṭṭiliṉ mēl iruppati aṟintu tikiluṟṟār. ataṟkuppiṉ koṟkiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyyum eṇṇattik kiviṭṭuviṭṭār eṉṟu puṉikati pōṉṟa ceytiyik kūṟiṉar. nīṅkaḷ iṅku akaḻntu pārkka muṟpaṭṭāl pūtaṅkaḷ uṅkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟu, eṅkaḷiyum, eṅkaḷ ūriyum aḻittuviṭum! eṉṟu eccarittaṉar.
avarkaḷiṉ parivurikaḷik kēṭṭu viyantu, aiyā, nāṉ poṉṉiyō, maṇiyiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷiyō akaḻntu eṭuttuc cella nāṭi iṅku varavilli eṉṟu avarkaḷukku amiti kūṟi māvaṭṭa atikāriyiṉ āṇiyōṭum avaruṭiya paṇiyāḷōṭum vantirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟurittu akaḻntu āyvukaḷ ceytuḷḷār. itu paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ avar eḻutiya tirunelvēli araciyal potuvaralāṟu7 eṉṟa nūlilum, koṟkiyilum kāyalilum naṭattiya āyvup payaṇam8 eṉṟa kaṭṭuriyilum naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1861ām āṇṭu kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭciyāḷariṉ (Rulers of the East India company) poṟi vallunarāṉa alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām (Alexander Cunningham) avarkaḷi oru mēṟpārvi yāḷarāka amartti intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyit toṭaṅkumāṟu āṇiyiṭṭatu. kumpiṉi aracu avar atit toṭaṅki vikkumāṟum ceytatu. appāl 1870ām āṇṭil avarē intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉ iyakkunarāka amarttap peṟṟār.9
1861ām āṇṭil tāṉ intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟikkum naṉku aṭikōlap peṟṟatu. mutalāvatu iyakkunar taḷapati (Director General) ākat tiru. alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām 1870ām āṇṭil amarttap peṟṟār eṉṟu ataṉ varalāṟu kūṟu kiṟatu. alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām nāṭṭiṉ nālāpakkaṅkaḷukkum ceṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvu ceyya muṟpaṭṭār. avar vaṅkam, pañcāp, cintu mānilaṅkaḷuk kellām ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkē kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷic cēkarittu vantār. āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa marapuvaḻic ceytikaḷiyum ilakkiyac ceytikaḷiyum tiraṭṭi aṟikkikaḷ pala veḷiyiṭṭu vantār. ivarukku vaṭa moḻip payiṟciyumuṇṭu.
1881ām āṇṭil teṉṉintiyat tolporuḷ āyvup poṟuppu (Archeological Servey of South India) ivariṭam oppuvikkappaṭṭatu. appoḻutu cēmcu parkecu (James Burgess) eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāvilum mēṟku intiyāvilum iv vāyvut tuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭiruntār. anta āṇṭilē eyc.eyc. kōl (H.H. Cole) paḻamporuḷkaḷiṉ pātukāppāḷarāka amarttap peṟṟār. 1883ām āṇṭil ip patavi muṭivu eytiyatāl tol āyvup paṇi mānila aracukaḷiṭam mīṇṭum oppaṭikkap paṭṭatu.10
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil naṭuvaṇ aracu tolporuḷ pātukāppu mutaliyavaṟṟi mīṇṭum taṉ poṟuppil koṇṭuvantatu. naṭuvaṇ aracu aṉittu intiyat tolporuḷ tuṟiyic cemmippaṭutti, virivākkiyatu. vayavar cāṇ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) 1876-1958 eṉṉum pēraṟiñari 1902 ām āṇṭu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi iyakkunar taḷapati 
(Director General of Archeologic Department) āka amarttiyatu. avar ēṟakkuṟiya irupattintu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟi 1928ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟār; ōyvu peṟṟu, mokañcatārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum (Mohenjo Daro and Indus civilization) eṉṟa ulakappukaḻpeṟṟa nūli eḻuti mūṉṟu tokutikaḷāka veḷi yiṭṭār; taṭcacīlam, cāñci mutaliya puttarkaḷiṉ puṉita iṭaṅkaḷi akaḻntāyntu oru ciṟu nūl veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār; 1931 ām āṇṭu avar ellāp patavikaḷiṉiṉṟum vilaki ōyvu peṟṟu avaratu tāy nāṭākiya iṅkilāntu ceṉṟu 1958ām āṇṭu uyir tuṟantār.
mārcal muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattiya cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu ulakilē oppaṟṟa periya akaḻ āyvu eṉṟu pārāṭṭappeṟṟu, ivarukku īṭillāp perumiyi īṭṭiyatu. ivartam mokañca tārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa nūlil cintuveḷi nākarikam, civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu vanta tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭimakkaḷiṉ uḻavut toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭu eṉṟu āyntu muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷār. innūl mānilam aḻiyum vari avarukku maṅkāp pukaḻi īṭṭit taravallatu.
cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu
cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu veṟṟi īṭṭiyatu. tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉ mītu makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭam īrkkappeṟṟatu. intiya naṭuvaṇ aracu cintuveḷi nākarikattip peṟṟa aracu eṉṟu peru mitam koṇṭatu. aracu tārāḷamākap paṇam nalkit tiṅkaḷ veḷi yīṭukaḷum, tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi āṇṭu aṟikkikaḷum, nūlkaḷum veḷiyiṭat tūṇṭiyatu. mīṇṭum pala iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻ āyvu naṭatta āṇiyiṭṭatu.
āṅkila āṭci, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkak kālattilē paṇṭiya nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ pātukāppuc caṭṭam oṉṟi niṟuviyuḷḷatu. ataṉ payaṉākap pala ciṟappu vāynta paḻaṅkāla niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷukkuk kaṭattik koṇṭu pōkaviṭātu taṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. eṉṟālum keṭṭa neñcattiṉar cilarmūlam nammuṭiya aimpoṉ paṭimaṅkaḷ cilikaḷ, tūṇkaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, kācukaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, cep pēṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉa kavarntu pōkappaṭṭati nām maṟikkavō maṟakkavō muṭiyātu. itaṟku nam araciṉarum kōyil pūcārikaḷum poṟuppāḷar āvar.
niṟka, 1926 ām āṇṭu tolporuḷ āyvup payaṇam 
(Exploration) naṭattuvataṟkeṉṟum, akaḻ āyvukku (Excavation) ceyvataṟkeṉṟum oru taṉippirivu uruvākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 1935 ām āṇṭiltāṉ intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṉ kīḻttol poruḷ āyvu, naṭuvaṇ araciṉ poṟuppil koṇṭu varap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 1944ām āṇṭil muṉivar ār.i. mārṭimar uyilar tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉ iyakkunar taḷapatiyāy vantatum, tēṅkik kiṭanta tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟikkup put tuṇarcci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu, āriyar allāta makkaḷ kaṭṭi eḻuppiya nākarikam eṉpati uyilar araṇ ceytār. arappā, mokañcatārō nakarkaḷiṉ kāvaṟ kōṭṭi kaḷi (citadels) akaḻntu kaṇṭu āriyarkaḷiṉ irukkuvētam kūṟum tācar, tacayūkkaḷiṉ araṇkaḷ iviyēeṉṟu ulakaṟiya urittār; mokañcatārō eṉṟu avar eḻutiya ciṟu nūlil anta nakar araṇiṉ (citadel) paṭattiyum irukkuvēta culōkaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭic cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam aṉṟu eṉṉum pēcciṟku muṟṟuppuḷḷi vittār. ivarē arikkaṉ mēṭu akaḻ āyvi naṭattit, tamiḻar 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ naṭattiya kaṭal vaṇikattiṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi, intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟi naṭattum paṇṭiya intiyā (Ancient India) eṉṟu intiyat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉar veḷiyiṭṭu varum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil oru periya kaṭṭuri varintu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum yavaṉa nāṭṭu vaṇikattiṟku meyccāṉṟu īteṉa eṭuttak kāṭṭiṉār.
mēlum uyilar, intiyattolporuḷ āyvuttuṟikku nalla maṟuvāḻvu aḷittār. āṅkāṅkē paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālikaḷ eḻac ceytār. putiya muṟiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyya araciṉ aluvalarkaḷiyum taṉiyārkaḷiyum payiṟṟuvittut tolporuḷ āyvu virivaṭiya vaḻikōliṉār. muṉṉar araciṉ potup paṇittuṟiyē paṇṭiya niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷip paḻutu pārttal, kāppāṟṟutal pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceytu vantatu. ati avar tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyē nēraṭiyākac ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭiṉār.
intiyā urimi eytiyatum 1950ām āṇṭil tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟi putiya araciyal amippiṉ paṭi naṭuvaṇ araciṉ poṟuppilum, mānila araciṉ poṟuppilum oruṅkē varakkūṭiya tuṟikaḷil oṉṟāka amintatu. intap putiya neṟi muṟikaḷiṉpaṭi mānila aracu ovvoṉṟum taṉakkeṉṟu taṉit taṉiyē tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉi niṟuvik koṇṭatu.
1958ām āṇṭil paḻiya niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, tol poruḷ kaḷaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pātukāppiṟkāka oru putiya caṭṭam eḻuntatu. nāṭṭiṉ paḻam poruḷkaḷ (Antiquities) varalāṟ ṟiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum nākarikattiṟkum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭi yaṉavāy iruppatāl avaṟṟi ayalnāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyvatik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum atikāratti aracu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṭam oppaṭittatu. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟi yiṉarē iṉṟu tol poruḷ kaḷaṅkaḷi āytal, akaḻtal, paḻam poruḷkaḷ paḻutupaṭā vaṇṇam vētiyal muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu pātukāttal, āyvu muṭivukaḷit tokuttu aḻaku poliya acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭutal pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceytu varu kiṉṟaṉar.
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi taṉ ceyalmuṟi eḷimik kāka intiyāvi 9 tolporuḷ vaṭṭaṅ kaḷākap pirittuḷḷatu. ataṉ talimi aluvalakam pututilliyil uḷḷatu. aṅkut tolporuḷ āyvut tērccikkeṉṟu tolporuḷiyal āyvuppaḷḷi oṉṟu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. mēlum tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟiyil intiyap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ ūkka muṭaṉ māṇavarkaḷukkup payiṟciyaḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa.
kāḷipaṅkaṉ
intiyā urimi peṟṟu eḻuntapiṉ naṭanta āyvukaḷil oṉṟu irācattāṉil pikkāṉir māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa kāḷipaṅkaṉ eṉṉum iṭattil naṭipeṟṟa akaḻāyvākum. iḵtu arappā mokañcatārō pōṉṟa nakaramākum. itu cintu veḷip paṇ pāṭṭiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka ki.mu. 2045 mutal ki.mu. 2075ām āṇṭu kaḷukkuḷ eḻunta nakaramākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. arappāvip pōṉṟu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, kaḻivu nīrōṭikaḷum, kiṇaṟukaḷum akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. arappāvip pōl kuṭiyiruppu iṭaṅkaḷum poṉ cempu maṇikkōvikaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
cintu veḷiyil kiṭitta muttirikaḷpōl iṅkum pala muttirikaḷ atē eḻuttukkaḷiyuṭiyaṉavāyk kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilar itaṉ kālam ki.mu. 1500āka matippiṭukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ neruppit teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭirukka lām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuc cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyap paṭṭa civaliṅkaṅkaḷ palavum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta akaḻ āyvil kiṭitta uṭinta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ pala uḷa.
kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam
itu cōḻarkaḷiṉ tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam. itaṉip pūmpukār eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu cīrkāḻikku aṇmiyil kāviri kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭattil uḷḷatu. iṅku intiya naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyic cērnta tiru. ē.vi. irāmaṉ ēṟakkuṟiya 15 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ akaḻ āyvu naṭatti ṉār. iṅkup pala paḻiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, cōḻar kācukaḷum, urōma nāṇayaṅkaḷum, caṅkukaḷum, vili uyarnta maṇikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. itaṉ kālam ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭāka matikkap paṭukiṟatu. iṅku oru kaṭṭiṭamum akaḻntu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
iṅku ōr aḻakiya puttar kōyilum kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum ceṅkaṟcuvarkaḷ mītu aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. iviyaṉṟi vēṟu pala kaṭṭiṭac citivukaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku irācarācaṉ kālattiya kācukaḷum kaṟuppu civappu vaṇṇan tīṭṭappaṭṭa uṭinta maṇpāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷ palavum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭitta maṇ pommikaḷ caturak kācukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu iṅkuc citintuḷḷa kaṭṭi ṭaṅkaḷ 9 allatu 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntaviyeṉac cilarāl eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu akaḻ āyvu
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭukku aṇmiyil uḷḷa pālāṟṟiṉ kariyi aṭuttuḷḷa kōrttaliyāṟṟup paṭuki intiya naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi vallunar tiru. vi.ṭi. kiruṭṭiṇamūrtti avarkaḷ talimiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭatu. aṅkup paḻaṅkal ūḻiyic cērnta makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa kallālāṉa kikkōṭāri, kattikaḷ, kuḻavikaḷ pōṉṟavi yākum. iviyaṉṟi uritteṭukkum kittoḻilkaḷum, tiṇṇiya mutukuḷḷa vāḷkaḷ ceyyum kittoḻilkaḷum, ciṟukaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa niṉivucciṉṉaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭukku aṇmiyil uḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷil paḻaṅkal ūḻiyil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ uṇavu camitta aṭuppukaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup pala uṭinta maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum cāmpalkaḷum arikuṟi yāy erintu miñciya marakkaṭṭi kaḷum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṟiviyal muṟiyil āyvu ceyyappaṭṭu (Carbon 14 Test) ivi paḻaṅkal ūḻiyic cārnta makkaḷ (Paleolithicmen) viṭṭuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu.
irāmanātapuram, nelli māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil akaḻāyvu
irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tirumaṅkalam pōṉṟa nakarkaḷukku aṇmiyil putiya kal ūḻiyic cērnta makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷ pala kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa uvari, meyññāṉapuram, nācarattu, cāyarpuram, putūr pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil putiya kal ūḻi yil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷ palavaṟṟi naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉar kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷaṉar.
eṉavē, tamiḻnāṭṭil cumār aimpatāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē makkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. tamiḻmakkaḷ paḻaṅkal ūḻiyiliruntu māḻi (ulōka) ūḻimūlam varalāṟṟuk kālattiyum kaṇṭa tolkuṭiyic cērnta mutu makkaḷ eṉpatu aiyantiripaṟak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷālum varalāṟṟuttuṟi vallunarkaḷālum tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟi vallunarkaḷālum uṟuti ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
koṟki akaḻ āyvu
koṟki, pāṇṭiyanāṭṭiṉ talinakar, tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉam, iṭiccaṅkam nilaviya mūtūr, it tuṟi mukattiṉ mūlamē tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉar, cōṉakar, ekiptiyar, cumēriyar pōṉṟa veḷināṭṭiṉaruṭaṉ kaṭal vaṇikatti naṭattiṉar. ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikarkaḷ poṟkācukaḷiyum, poṟpāḷaṅkaḷiyum aḷḷi aḷḷik koṭuttuviṭṭuk koṟki muttukkaḷiyum cantaṉak kaṭṭikaḷ, ēlam, kirāmpu, eḵku āyutaṅkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum peṟṟut tam kappalil ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. nam kappalkaḷum ayalnāṭukaḷukkac ceṉṟu mīṇṭaṉa.
iṉṟu koṟkiyilirunta kaṭal cumār 4 kal toliviṟkup piṉvāṅkip pōyviṭṭatu. eṉavē, pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tam tali nakari maturikku māṟṟiṉar. ēṟattāḻa 2,000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ koṟki nilikulintu aḻintu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉāl avvūr iṉṟu ētumaṟṟa ciṟṟūrāyk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.
ataṉi namatu tamiḻ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi iyakkunar muṉivar nākacāmi avarkaḷum avaruṭiya utaviyāḷar tiru. macītu avarkaḷum akaḻntu cila maṭpāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷi yum, cila tātupporuḷkaḷiyum kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar. mēlum koṟkip paṇpāṭu ki.mu. 785-kku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiviyal pāṅkāka āyntu muṭivukaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. koṟkiyi mīṇṭum virivāka āyvataṟku namatu aracu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyi muṭukkiviṭumāṟu nammakkaḷ araciṉi vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.
tirukkōyil kaṭṭiṭak kaṇkāṇippu niṟuvaṉam
tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi, kaṭṭiṭakkali nuṇukkam ivaṟṟiṉ āyvukkeṉa intiya naṭuvaṇaracu kōyil kaṇ kāṇippu niṟuvaṉam (Temple Survey Project) eṉṟa amippiṉi uruvākkiyuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu, vaṭa intiya āyvukkeṉṟu pōpālil amintuḷḷatu. maṟṟoṉṟu ceṉṉiyil teṉ ṉintiyakkōyil kaṭṭiṭattuṟic ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiṉ āyvuk keṉṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟkeṉṟu iyakkunar oruvarum niḻaṟpaṭam eṭuppavarkaḷum, eḻuttarkaḷum paṇiyāṭkaḷum iyaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. inta niṟuvaṉam ceṉṉiyil uḷḷa tamiḻaka araciṉ talimic ceyalakattiṟku aṇmiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. aṅkuc ceṉṟu avarkaḷi ōraḷavu nāṉ payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ.
kalveṭṭu āyvuttuṟi
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟikku utaviyāka 1883 ām āṇṭu paḻiya paṭṭayaṅkaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittuppaṭiceytu viḷakki iṉṟiya varivaṭivil eḻuti nūlvaṭivil ākkam ceytu veḷiyiṭuvataṟkeṉṟu kal veṭṭu āyvuttuṟi oṉṟu (Epigraphic Department) toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. kalveṭṭu, paṭṭayaṅkaḷ āyvuttuṟit talivarākac cāṇ peyitpul piḷīṭ eṉṉum āṅkilēyari aracu amartti yatu. avar nāṭeṅkum tēṭi kiṭikkum kalveṭṭukaḷiyum, cempuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷiyum tiraṭṭiṉār; palavaṟṟip paṭiyeṭut tār; avaṟṟi nūlvaṭivamākki varalāṟṟuk kāla variciyil tokuppataṟkut tiṭṭamiṭṭup paṇiceytār.
kalveṭṭu, cempuppaṭṭaya āyvi oru taṉi aluvalakam kavaṉittu varukiṟatu. iḵtu āṅkila āṭciyiṉ kālattil utaka maṇṭalattil iyaṅki vantatu. intat tuṟiyiṉ talivarāka āṅkilēyarkaḷē iruntu vantaṉar. ippoḻutu ittuṟi intiyarkaḷiṉ talimiyil iyaṅki varukiṟatu. ippoḻutu itaṉ aluvalakam micūrukku māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl, ivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭuvarum paṇikaḷi empōṉṟa varkaḷē aṟiyamuṭiya villi. ivarkaḷ paṭitta paṭṭayaṅkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷivantatākat teriyavilli. namatu aracu ataṟku āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum. tolporuḷ vētiyiyal ārāyccit talimi aluvalakam tērāṭūṉilum, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa āyvu aluvalakam nākapuriyilum paḻam poruḷ kāṭcittuṟi āḷumi kalkattāvilum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nalla varalāṟṟi aṟiya iṉṉum virivākak kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷivaravēṇṭum. kiṭṭat taṭṭa nūṟāyiram kalveṭṭu kaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṟatu. kalveṭṭu āyvuttuṟi iṉṟu āmi vēkattil iyaṅkivarukiṟatu. inta vēkattil aḵtu iyaṅkivantāl tamiḻar kaḷiṉ niṟivāṉa varalāṟṟik kāṇa iṉṉum 100 āṇṭukaḷāvatu cellum eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. namatu aracu ittakiya oru tuṟiyi micūril naṭatti varukiṟatu eṉpatu aṟiñar palarukkukkūṭa teriyātu.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
II
1.  kalik kaḷañciyam, tokuti VI, mutaṟpatippu, 1959 (ceṉṉi) tolporuḷ iyal, pakkam 224.
2. The Riddles of Three Oceans :
		“Oceanographers may be right when they say that this portion of  the Indian Ocean is an ancient area in transition and has not yet  completed its development. That is, the Seychelles micro Continent  is not a region which has subsided but, on the contrary a portion of the ocean floor which has not yet risen to the surface. Neither a positive nor negative answer can yet be given to this question. We shall have to wait and see what is revealed by further geophysical and Oceanographic studies of the Indian Ocean; now only in their initial stage.
		Oceanographers, geologist and geophysicists are devoting their closest attention to the north western section of Indian Ocean, which has the most complex relief and where the earth’s crust is still in motion, as evidenced by volcanic eruptions and earthquakes. Many of them believe that this part of the Ocean has developed differently from all the other sections. The granite massifs of East Africa, the Arabian Peninsula and the Indian subcontinnt continue out into the Indian Ocean” (The Riddles of Three Oceans - Alexander Kondratov (Moscow) 1974, pp. 174-175.
3.	“Indian Ocean founded a continent which extended from Sunda Island along the coast of the Asia to the east coast of Asia to the east coast of Africa. This large continent is of great importance from being the possible cradle of the human race” - “Anthropology”.
	“These are a number of circumstances (specially chronological facts) which suggest that the primeval home of man was a continent now sunk below the surface of the Indian Ocean which extended along the South of Asia as it is at present (and probably in direct connection with it) towards the east as far as further India and the Sunda Islands towards the west as far as the Madagascar and the south shores of Africa.
	This large continent of former times Selater an English man has called Lemuria from the monkey like animals which inhabited it and it is at the same time of great importance from being the probable cradle of the human race which in all likelihood here that developed out of anthropoid apes” - Prof. Ernest Haeckel.
4.	Investigations in relation to race show it is by no means impossible that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of Northern and Mediteranean races proceeded to the globe which they now inhabit. Human remins and traces have been found on the east coast of an age which is indeterminate but quite beyond the ordinary calculations of History. Antiquarian research is only now beginning to find means of supplementing the deficiency cused by the absence of materials constructed or collected by usual historical methods. These results are specially to be regarded as without doubt, the people who have many ages occupied this portion of the Penisula are a people influencing the whole world not perhaps by moral and intellectual attributes to a great extent by a superior physical qualities” - Dr.E.Maclean.
5.	“India was an amazing breeding ground for the evolution of life forms. Man himself may have struggled upwards out of the anthropoid within the limits of India” - The World We Live In - Graham Williams Vol.1. p. 1114.
6.	Origin and Spread of the Tamils.
	(a)	“Bruce Foote examined, among others the most important Prehistoric burial places covering over hundred and fourteen acres of land at Adichanallur on the South Bank of the Tambraparni. (Thennporuni river) in the Tinnevelly District. These graves at Adichanallur are to be dated in te early Iron Age which succeeded the Neolithic Age in South India” - Origin And Spread of the Tamils by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar, M.A. (Madras) 1971, p. 21.
	(b)	“We have a continuity of culture from Paleolithic to Neolithic, from Neolithic to Megalithic and Megalithic to Iron age in South India. It cannot be that the Primitive indigenous culture was buried by the invading Dravidian. To escape out of the difficulty, an ingenious theory of Proto - Dravidian has been propounded. The Proto-Dravidian is as unsound as the theory of a Dravidian invasion. My firm conviction is that the ancient Tamils were inheritors of the Lithic Cultures of South India” - Ibid. p. 15.
	(c)	“The late Professor P.T. Srinivasa Aiyangar was a warm champion of the theory of cultural and even racial continuity of the people of South India from the earliest times. He sought to explin” that the Old Stone Age in South India ended very gradually and shaded off very imperceptibly into the neolithic culture and that throughout South India, there was no geological or other indication of catastrophic phenomena when the Paleolithic Age ended and the next age began. The substitution of traprock for quartzite, the acquisition of the skill to polish the tools made with trap-rock till they became extra-ordinarily smooth to the touch, the domestication of the wild dog, and the cultivation of the wild rice led to the peaceful evolution of the epoch of new stone tools from that of the older rough implements, the settled life of the neolithians from the nomad life of the paleolithians” - Journal of the Bihar and Orissa Research Society Vol. xxiv. pp. 39-40.
	(d)	Thus according to this view, the Dravidian race was indigenous to the Country; and the Tamil and other allied peoples were indigenous; and their languages were evolved where they are now spoken. A careful study of South Indian Pre-historic antiquities in Situ cannot lead to any other conclusion than that the passage of culture from stage to stage in ancient times was not a catastrophic change such as indicates the struggle or alien intruders with the pre-existing population but a peaceful course of evolution. An inspection of the map of neollthic India is enough to prove that the Country was thickly populated by people of one homogenous from of culture and that the people ought to have been autochthonous, as the Tamil people have always climed to be in the tradition recorded in their ancient literature. In the most ancient layers of the Tamil language can be discovered not only ample traces of neolithic culture, but also the birth of the Iron age culture that succeeded it” - Journal of th Bihar and Orissa Research Society vol. xxiv. pp. 41-42.
7.	A Political and General History of Tinnevelly - Dr. R. Caldwell (Bishop of Tinnevelly) Published by the Madras Govt. 1881.
8.	Exploration at Korki and Kayal by the Rt. Rev. Robert Caldwell. D.D., LLD. (Bishop of Tinnevelly) 1877.
9.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adicha-nallur and Perumbir - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras, 1915.
10.	Vide Reports of th Archeological Survey of Southern Circle for 1890-1900 to 1903-1904 and of the Archeological Survey of India for 1902 -1903 and 1903-1904.

intap pakuti ākkattiṟku utaviya nūlkaḷ

3. ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ akaḻāyvu
intiyāvilē naṭipeṟṟa akaḻāyvukaḷil ātitta nallūrp paṟampil naṭipeṟṟa akaḻāyvu, mutal akaḻāyvākum. inta akaḻāyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ tām ulakilē mikat toṉmi vāynta poruḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟṟikkup pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻumpiya putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil eḻunta irumpu ūḻiyil, tamiḻmakkaḷ ātitta nallūr irumpup paṇpāṭṭik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi vallunar pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ātittanallūr
ātitta nallūrp paṟampu, intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil uḷḷa tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil tirunelvēlic cantippup pukivaṇṭi niliyattiṉiṉṟu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi vaḻiyāyt tiruccentūr cellum cāliyil, pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyiliruntu kiḻakkē 11.6 kilō mīṭṭar tolivil taṇporuni āṟṟiṉ (tāmpiravaruṇi āṟṟiṉ) teṉkariyil uḷḷatu. intap paṟampiṉ varampu 114 kuṟukkattukku (ēkkarukku) mēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. inta aḷavu itaṉ aṇmiyil uḷḷa āticcanallūr eṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭu varum ciṟṟūri uḷḷaṭakkiyē kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ātitta nallūrp paṟampu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum oru maṇmēṭu taṇporuni āṟṟiṉ teṉ kariyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkit teṉvaṭalāka nīṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ vaṭapakuti uyarntum, teṉpakuti tāḻntum uḷḷatu. iṉṟum,kuṉṟeṉa nimirntu niṟkum im mēṭṭu nilam (paṟampu) kaḷḷiyum, muḷḷiyum, kallum, muḷḷum, caralum, paralum, veḷḷuṭiyum karuvuṭiyum (karuvēlum), putarum patarum aṭarntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭāta pāṅkaṟṟa nilamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. inta iṭattip paṇṭiya tamiḻarkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalip putikkum īmakkāṭākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. (paṭam 1aip pārkka). cintuveḷiyil akaḻntu kaṇṭa perunakar oṉṟiṟku mokañcatārō eṉṟu peyar. mokañcatārō eṉpatu intic col. ataṉ poruḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ nakaram eṉpatākum. intap peyar annakarukku ēṟṟatāka eṉṉāl karuta muṭiyavilli. āṉāl, ataṟku ñāyiṟṟu nallūr (ātitta nallūr) eṉṟa peyaraḷittuviṭṭu, ātitta nallūril akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku mokañcatārō eṉṟu peyariṭṭu aḻittāl mikap poruttamāka irukkum. ēṉeṉil itu muṟṟilum īmakkāṭē. iṅku āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ cavak kuḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārō appaṭippaṭṭa īmakkāṭaṉṟu. makkaḷ vāḻnta mānakaramākum. maṟintupōṉa māperum nākarikamāṉa cintu veḷi nākarikam, upitiya nākarikam, ellam nākarikam, cumēriya nākarikam pōṉṟa paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭiyāy amin tiruntu aḻintupōṉatu ātitta nallūr nākarikam.
mutal akaḻāyvu 
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi piḷḷip paruvattil irukkum poḻutē - aḵtāvatu 1876 ām āṇṭilē cermaṉ nāṭṭil uḷḷa perliṉ nakaril vāḻnta muṉivar cākōr avarkaḷ 
(Dr. Jagore) ātittanallūrkku vantu intap paṟampil uḷḷa putar kaḷik kaḷintu nilatti akaḻntu aṅkup putiyuṇṭu kiṭanta paḻam poruḷkaḷi eṭukkat tiṭṭamiṭṭār.
avaratu tiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟa muṉintu avar perliṉ nakariviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭuk kappalil ēṟi intiyāviṟku vantu puki vaṇṭiyilum, māṭṭu vaṇṭiyilum nīṇṭanāḷ payaṇam ceytu, intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil uḷḷa kumarimuṉikku vaṭakkil ēṟattāḻa 100 ki.mīṭṭar tolivil uḷḷa ātitta nallūri aṭintār. avar nērāka ātittanallūrukku varavilli. avar tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri tiru. icuṭṭu varṭṭu (Mr. Stuart the acting collector of Tinnevelly District) māvaṭṭap poṟiyiyal vallunar (District engineer) ākiyavarkaḷ piṉtoṭara, ātitta nallūrp paṟampi aṭintu aṅkuḷḷa kallum maṇṇum kalanta kaḷar nilatti akaḻa muṟpaṭṭār. iḵtu intiyāvil tuḷirttuk kiḷittuḷḷa akaḻ āyviṉ toṭakka viḻāvāka eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu. ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil etirpārttirāta veṟṟi yiṉi muṉivar cākōr eytiṉār. aṅkup pala tāḻikaḷum, elumpukaḷum, poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷum, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷam, irumpup poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta paḻamporuḷkaḷik kaṇṭu muṉivar cākōr kaḷippuṟṟār. kāṇaṟkariya karuvūlaṅkaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttatākak karuti aṉittiyum mikap pātukāppākap peṭṭiyil aṭittup perliṉ nakarukkak koṇṭu pōṉār. appāl avvariya paḻam poruḷkaḷ aṉittiyum aṅkuḷḷa paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil (Berlin Museum for Volkrkunde) iṭam peṟac ceytār.1 itaṟkumēl intap poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya ceyti etuvum namakku eṭṭavilli.
iraṇṭāvatu akaḻāyvu
itit toṭarntu 1903-1904 ām āṇṭukaḷiṉ kuḷir kālattil pirañcu nāṭṭic cērnta pārīcu nakaril uḷḷa em. ulūyicu ilāppikyū (M. Louis Lapicque) avarkaḷāl ātitta nallūrp paṟampil mīṇṭum akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭu muṉpāl paḻamporuḷkaḷ pala akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭu aṉittum pāricu, paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭampeṟac ceyyap peṟṟaṉa. aṟiñar ilāppikyū avarkaḷ iṅkuk kaṇṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷ aṉittiyum āyntatiṉ viḷivāka apporuḷkaḷ aṉit tiyum tirāviṭarkaḷ āti muṉṉōrkaḷukkuc contamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟa muṭiviṟku vantār.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku evvakit toṭarpum aṟṟa cermaṉkārar tamiḻakattil nuḻintu ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻāyvu naṭatti vili matikka muṭiyāta karuvūlamāṉa paḻamporuḷkaḷip pakaṟ koḷḷi aṭippatippōl kavarntu ceṉṟār. itik kaṇṭu ulakam kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi āṭciyi (East India company Rule) ēḷaṉamākac cirittatu. atik kaṇṭu piriṭṭīcuk kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi āṭcikkuc coraṇi ēṟpaṭṭatu. 1899 mutal 1906 ām āṇṭuvari ātitta nallūrp paṟampil mīṇṭumakaḻāyvu naṭattumāṟu aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷukku āṇi piṟappittatu. avar ap pakutikaḷi uṉṉippāka uṟṟu uṇarntu akaḻntu ērāḷamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷi eṭuttār. muṉṉar akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiyotta paṭikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa putikuḻikaḷ (Burial grounds) māperum paḻimi vāyntavi eṉṟu muṭipu ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattiṟkup piṉṉarp payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaviyāy irukka vēṇṭum. ivi pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa mūlatti uṭiyaṉa eṉṟum irīyā eṇṇiṉār. ï‹W m¿P® mby¡rhªj® ïßah mt®fŸ mfœªJ f©l Mâ¤ješÿ®¥ gH«bghUŸfŸ mid¤J« br‹id¥ gH«bghUŸ fh£á¢ rhiyÆš xU gh§fhd ïl¤âš tF¤J¤ bjhF¤J¢ Óuhf it¡f¥ bg‰ WŸsd.*
muṉivar cākōrum, aṟiñar em. ulūyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷum ātittanallūrppaṟampil akaḻntu eṭuttuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷ eṉṉeṉṉa eṉṟu evarum aṟiyār. avarkaḷil evarum taṅkaḷ paḻam poruṭkāṭciccāliyil iṭam peṟac ceytirukkum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷukku viḷakkamāṉa paṭṭiyal etuvum veḷiyiṭṭiruppatāka namakku evvakic ceytiyum ituvari kiṭikkavilli. āṉāl, intiya nāṭṭiṉ aṉṟiya aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka (trustees) irunta piriṭṭīcuk kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyiṉ pērāḷarāka iruntavar, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri tiru. icuṭṭūvarṭ eṉpavar āvar. avar 1876 ām āṇṭil, muṉivar cākōr akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭutta ceyti kaḷip paṟṟik kumpiṉiyiṉ mēlatikārikaḷukku oru kaṭitam eḻuti yuḷḷār. antak kaṭitattuṭaṉ aṉṟiya piriṭṭīcukkumpiṉi āṭci yiṉ tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyil 1902-03, 1903-1905 ām āṇṭu kaḷiṉ intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi aḷavāyvu ceytavarkaḷum 1899-1900ām āṇṭukaḷ mutal 1903-1904 āṇṭukaḷ vari tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉ, teṟku vaṭṭārac cuṟṟāyvu ceytavarkaḷum aḷitta aṟikkikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi kūṟum ceytikaḷ :
nāṅkaḷ 25 allatu 30 caturakkecam (1 - caturakkecam 3 aṭi nīḷamum 3 aṭi akalamum aṭaṅkiyatu) parappaḷavuḷḷa mēleḻunta vāriyākak kallum caraḷum niṟinta pāṟimītuḷḷa oru pakutiyi yum atē mūlakkūṟukaḷiyum kal pāḷaṅkaḷiyum paṭikakkal niṟinta paṟampiṉ maṟṟum oru pakutiyiyum, akaḻ āyvu ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉōm. aṅku, nāṅkaḷ 25kku mēl 30 eṇ variyuḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi (tāḻikaḷi)k kaṇṭupiṭittōm. avaṟṟiṉ uruva aḷavu 3-9 aṭi mutal 3-6 aṭivari uyaramum ēṟattāḻa pattu aṅkulam vāyakalamum aḷavuḷḷaṉavāka iruntaṉa. pala iṉṟu kaṭikaḷil viṟkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiviṭa uṟutiyum nākarikamum uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. inta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil maṇkaṭṭiyum caraḷkaḷum aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟōṭu paḻamporuḷ ārvalar (Antiquarian) maṉitavaki vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum toṭarpukaḷiyum, paṇpāṭukaḷiyum paṟṟi āyvu ceypavar, paḻam poruḷkaḷ tiraṭṭiccērppavar (antiquarians, the ethrologist and collectors of Antiquities) mutal aṟiviyal vallunar variyuḷḷavarkku mikak kavarcci yūṭṭum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ atil aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta palvēṟu vakiyāṉa parumaṉil uḷḷa aṭukkaḷip pāṇṭaṅkaḷum maṇṇāl ākkappaṭṭaviyāka iruntaṉa. avi aimpatu vakikku mēṟpaṭṭaviyākum. mēlum aṅku irumpuppaṭik kalaṉkaḷ (Iron weapons) tuṇik karuvikaḷ (Implements) ciṟappākak kattikaḷ allatu kuṭṭiyāṉa veṭṭu vāyp pakutiyiyuṭiya vāḷkaḷ (Short sword-blades) kik kōṭarikaḷ (Hatches) ākiyaviyum periya aḷavil elumpukaḷum, taliyōṭukaḷum (maṇṭiyōṭukaḷum) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iṉṟu mika mutaṉmiyāṉa ceyti yākum. aḵtāvatu, iraṇṭu ciṟumaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ oru periya maṭpāṇṭattiṉuḷ vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpatākum. periya maṭpāṇṭattuḷ kiṭṭattaṭṭa ellā elumpukaḷum aṭaṅki yiruntaṉa, (ivaṟṟil eṉṉa poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa) eṉṟu kūṟuvatil piḻi etuvum eḻa muṭiyātu. āṉāl, tirunelvēlic cāmi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ularnta kūlapporuḷum arici eṉap paṭum kūlapporuḷum mēlē uḷḷa veḷip pakutiyil iruntaṉa. intak kūlap poruḷkaḷ tāmākavē ukki maṟintupōyiṉa. āṉāl mēlē tūvappaṭṭa - perumpālum maṇal cattuḷḷavi - eñci iruntaṉa.
eṅkaḷuṭiya mutaṉmiyāṉa akaḻ āyvu toṭaṅkap paṭṭa iṭattiṉiṉṟu kuṟintatu 270 allatu 360 mīṭṭar toli vil uḷḷa palaviṭaṅkaḷil mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa cāvu viṉikkuriya putikalaṉkaḷ (tāḻikaḷ urns) paṟampiluḷḷa caraḷik kaṟkaḷiṉ mēṟpākattil uṭinta ōṭṭut tuṇṭukaḷākap pala citaṟuṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. avi ellā vaḻikaḷilum akaḻāyvi meyppikkak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl, oru ceytiyil maṭṭum avi piṇaṅkaḷip putikkum paḻiya iṭukāṭṭiṉip paṟṟik kuṟippāka eṭuttukkāṭṭum iṭamāka iruntatu. allatu aṭinilak kallaṟiyil (cata comb) antak kālattil piṇattip putittu vikkum perum vaḻakku nilaviyatu eṉpatu iṉṟiya intiyā viṟkup perumpālum teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟalām. makkaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭṭip periya kuvaḷi allatu maṭpāṇṭat tiṉuḷḷē vittu ataṉuḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku ciṟiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷ maṟṟōr ulaki aṭiyumpoḻutu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum poruṭṭu veḷip paṭiyāyt teriyumāṟu ēṟpāṉa uṇavum, irumpālāṉa paṭik kalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intap poruḷkaḷ aṉittiyum akaḻntu eṭuttuc ceṉṟa muṉivar cākōr avaṟṟip perliṉ paḻam poruṭkāṭciccāliyil (Museum) iṭampeṟac ceytuḷḷār. intap poruḷkaḷil oṉṟum ceṉṉip paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟavilli. ivaṟṟiyellām māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷar tiru. icuṭṭuvarṭ aracukku eḻutiya anta maṭalil naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉpatu ceṉṉi araciṉ potup paṇittuṟi naṭavaṭikkikaḷ (27-3-1876 iṉ 329 ām eṇṇuḷḷa cāṉṟu) eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aṅku akaḻāyvu naṭipeṟṟapiṉṉar nilam paḷḷamum mēṭumāyk kiṭantatu. caraḷikaḷ aṅkum iṅkum kuvintu kiṭantaṉa. maṇ eṅkum citaṟuṇṭu kiṭantatu. elumpukaḷ aṭaṅkiyirunta mutu makkaḷ tāḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷ iṅkoṉṟum aṅkoṉṟumāy nērākavum, cāyntum, uṭintum kiṭantaṉa. akaḻvataṟku eṉṟu vanta vēliyāṭkaḷ kuruṭṭunampikki koṇṭu pēy picācu eṉṟu kūṟi naṭuṅki aṅkup pukuntu paṇi ceyya añci niṉṟaṉar. mēṟkoṇṭu akaḻntu āya muṭiyavilli. mēṟpākattil kiṭakkum cila tāḻikaḷi āyvu ceyyavō, cermaṉ aṟiñar viṭṭucceṉṟa paḻamporuḷkaḷi eṭuttukkoṇṭu varavō veḷippaṭuttavō muṭiyātiruntatu. ērāḷamāṉa paḻiya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷ atika uḻippiṉṟi eṭuttuk koṇṭu varum niliyil kiṭantum avaṟṟiṉ arukē evarum tuṇintu cellavilli. mūṭanampikkiyil mūḻkik kiṭanta vēli yāṭkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷil elumpukaḷum kuṟippākat taliyōṭukaḷum koṭiya pēykaḷākat tōṉṟiṉa.
iṟuti āyvu
ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ mēltaḷattil kiṭanta ariya poruḷkaḷi eṭuttakkoṇṭu varavum, toṭarntu akaḻntu āyvu naṭattavum, aracu eṇṇiyatu. āṉāl, aṉṟuḷḷa cūḻnili yil anta eṇṇam kiviṭap paṭṭatu. appāl akaḻ āyvu ceyyavēṇṭā eṉṟu aracu āṇivantatu. paṟampu evvakik kaliyum akaḻvum ceyyappaṭātu viṭappaṭṭatu. akaḻ āyvu ceyyum aṟiñarkaḷukku utavi etuvum kiṭikkāta niliyil anta eṇṇam kiviṭappaṭṭatu. mēlum toṭarntu āyvu ceyyap paṭavilli. itaṉ payaṉāy ērāḷamāṉa vili uyarnta paḻam poruḷkaḷ tēṭuvāraṟṟa niliyil kiṭantu aḻintoḻintaṉa.
1899 mutal 1900 āṇṭu vari ceṉṉi mānila araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyiṉ matippukkuriya tuṇik kaṇkāṇip pāḷarāka irunta tiru.a.irīyā avarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalākap paṟampiṉ mēṟṟaḷattip pārviyiṭṭār. appoḻutu aṅkuḷḷa cila pakutikaḷ akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpati uṇarntār. 1903 mutal 1904 vari ovvōr āṇṭum cila tiṅkaḷ avarāl mēṟkoṇṭu akaḻāyvukaḷ naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷil tiraṭṭap paṭṭavi ceṉṉi araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyil vakuttut tokuttu iṭampeṟac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. irīyā avar kaḷiṉ perum muyaṟciyāl kiṭṭattaṭṭa avarukku muṉṉar akaḻ āyvu ceyta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukku oppāka ērāḷamāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉ maṟupaṭivam (duplicate) kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi ātitta nallūr akaḻāyvup poruḷkaḷukkut tarappaṭṭa paṭṭiyalil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ceṉṉi araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyiṉ kaṇkāṇippāḷar tiru.cē.ār. eṇṭarcaṉ 1914 ām āṇṭu ātitta nallūrp perumpāyūrkaḷi ṉiṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
1899ām āṇṭu mutal 1905ām āṇṭu vari teṟku vaṭṭārat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟik kaṇkāṇippāḷarāka iruntu ōyvu peṟṟavarum, ceṉṉi araciṉ paḻamporuṭ kāṭcic cāliyiṉ matippukkuriya tuṇik kaṇkāṇippāḷaru māṉa tiru. alekcāntar irīyā ev.e.ē. (kāṭ) avarkaḷ ceṉṉi mānilak kiḻakkintiyakkumpiṉi āṭciyiṉ āṇippaṭi āṇṭutōṟum ōriru tiṅkaḷ ātitta nallūrp paṟampic ciṟitu ciṟitāka akaḻāyvu naṭatti, ērāḷamāṉa ariya paḻam poruḷ kaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttu avaṟṟi vakuttut tokuttu, ceṉṉip paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyiṉ oru periya maṇṭapattil iṭampeṟac ceytār. 1913ām āṇṭu akṭōpart tiṅkaḷil avar tam paṇiyiṉiṉṟu ōyvu peṟumuṉ akaḻāyvup poruḷ kaḷukku oru nalla paṭṭiyalum uruvākkik koṭuttuḷḷār. ap paṭṭiyal aḻakiya 13 paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉum ceṉṉip paḻamporuṭ kāṭcic cāliyiṉ kaṇkāṇippāḷarāka irunta tiru.cē.ār eṇṭarcaṉ avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉuriyuṭaṉum ātitta nallūrilum perumpāyūrilumuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal (Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir) eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ 1915 ām āṇṭu ceṉṉi aracu paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccāli oruciṟu veḷiyīṭ ṭiṉi3 veḷiyiṭṭatu. ataṟkāka nāṉum tamiḻakamum aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷukkum tiru.cē.ār. eṇṭarcaṉ avarkaḷukkum naṉṟiyiyum vaṇakkattiyum eṉṟum celuttak kaṭamip paṭṭuḷḷōm. avaratu nūliṉ paṭi oṉṟu eṉṉiṭam kiṭittirā viṭil ippoḻutu uṅkaḷ kiyilirukkum innūl veḷi vantirukka muṭiyātu.
ulakam ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiyō tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya irumpu ūḻiyil nilaviya irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta nākarikac ciṟappiyō aṟintirukka vāyppu eḻāmal pōyirukkum. aṟiñar irīyā aḷitta ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal 1902-03, 1903-04 āṇṭukaḷukkuriya intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉ āṇṭaṟikkiyākavum 1908-09 āṇṭil perumpāyūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ aṟikkiyākavum veḷivantuḷḷatu. aṟupat tintu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṟikkiyākavum, nūl vaṭivamākavum ākkap peṟṟa ivvēṭu 50 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē viṟpaṉiyāki viṭṭatu.
makkaḷ kikku vanta nūlkaḷ pala, kāla veḷḷattāl paḻiyaṉavāki iṟṟukkeṭṭu maṟintu pōyviṭṭaṉa. eṉavē iṉṟiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukkum, tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉarukkum maṉita iṉanūl aṟiñarkaḷukkum (Anthropologists) ātitta nallūr tamiḻar nākarikatti aṟiyum vāyppu illātu pōyviṭṭatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukku ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvum, cintuveḷi akaḻāyvum intumāk kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷam vari cōviyat mākkaṭal āyvāḷarkaḷ (Oceanographers) kaṭalil 75,000,000 caturak kilō mīṭṭar parappu allatu ulakamāk kaṭal kaḷil aintil oru pākaṅkoṇṭa intumākkaṭali vityārcu (Vitiyarz) eṉṟa uyariya aṟiviyal pāṅkāṉa ariya kaṭalāyvuk kappal mūlam pallāṇṭu toṭarntu pala ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa kal tolivu 1. cuṟṟulāc ceṉṟu perum poruḷ celaviṭṭu naṭattiya cuṟṟuc celaviṉ (expedition) muṭipukaḷum, tamiḻmakkaḷukku iṉṟiyami yātaṉavākum.
cōviyat kaṭalāyvu muṭipukaḷ namakku iṉṉum kiṭikka villi. ātittanallūr akaḻāyviṉ ciṟappi aṟintirāta cilar vāyk koḻuppuk koṇṭu tamiḻarkaḷukku 2000-m āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya vāḻvu illi eṉṟum āriya kulappirivil ivarkaḷ cūttirar eṉṟu karutappaṭuvar eṉṟum kūṟi vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ 50 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉiruntu atāvatu paḻaṅkallūḻikku muṉ iruntu vāḻntu, putiya kallūḻi irumpu ūḻi ākiyavaṟṟik kaṭantu, iṉṟiya varalāṟṟuk kālam vari vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmi, aṟiviyal muṟiyil nilināṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattiṉ mūtātiyarkaḷ ilemūriyāk kaṇṭattil tōṉṟit tamiḻakam, nāvalantīvu oḷināṭu, mūnāṭu eṉṟellām kūṟum maṟintu pōṉa nāṭukaḷil vāḻntavarkaḷ eṉṟu karuti annāṭu kaḷip paṟṟiyum cōviyat aṟiñarkaḷum, piṉlāntup pēraṟiñar kaḷum, piriṭṭicu moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷum piṟarum mutal iṭi, kaṭic caṅkaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum avaṟṟil veḷi vanta nūlkaḷip paṟṟiyum anta nilattip paṟṟiyum iṉṟu āya muṟpaṭṭuḷ ḷārkaḷ.
cintuveḷi akaḻāyvu tirāviṭa nākarikam ki.mu. 4000 ām āṇṭukaḷukku muntiyatu eṉṟa uṇmiyi nilināṭṭiyatu. tirāviṭarkaḷ ekiptu cumēriyā ellam, kirīṭ, meccikkō peru nāṭṭiṉarkaḷōṭu pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē toṭar puṭiya makkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmiyi nilināṭṭiyatu. ātitta nallūr akaḻāyvu maṉita iṉattiṉ mūtātiyarkaḷāṉa tamiḻar kaḷiṉ (tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ) āti muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ toṭṭil teṉṉintiyā eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu. cintuveḷi nākarikam iruḷārnta intiya varalāṟṟiṟku oru viṭiveḷḷiyākat tikaḻntatu. āṉāl, intiya varalāṟṟiṟku ātittanallūr nākarikam karumēkaṅkaḷ aṭarnta kār iruḷiṉ naṭuvē ātittaṉ taṉ poṟkatirkaḷi vīci iruḷiṉi akaṟṟi oḷitarum iḷam ñāyiṟu ākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟu iṉṟu vāḻum nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam vāynta aṟiñarkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
paṟampu amippu
ip paṟampil akaḻ āyvu ceyvataṟkuriya iṭam īṇṭuḷḷa ciṟṟūriyum uḷḷaṭakki, 114 ēkkar mēṟparappuḷḷa taricu nilamākum. intap paṟampu nilam tirunelvēlic cantippiṉiṉṟu ēṟattāḻa 20 kilōmīṭṭar tolivil tirunelvēlic cantippu tiruccentūr araca pāṭṭiyil uḷḷatu. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ip paṟampiṉ mēṟṟaḷam payirt toḻil ceyvataṟkut takutiyaṟṟa, payaṉaṟṟa kaḷar nilam. innilam paṇṭutoṭṭu iṭu kāṭākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. inta iṭukāṭṭiṟku aṇmiyil oru ciṟṟūrum uḷḷatu. inta ūrukku aṇmiyil teṉpuṟamākac cila piṇakkuḻikaḷum uḷa. itu niṉivuk keṭṭāta kālantoṭṭu nilavi vantuḷḷatu. teṉ ticiyāṉatu maraṇattiṉ teyvamāṉa emaṉ ticiyeṉa eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. ūrukkut teṟkē eṅkum iṭukāṭukaḷum cuṭukāṭukaḷum iṭam peṟṟiruppatu iyalpu.
paṟampiṉ naṭuvil mūṉṟaṭi mēṟparappil caraḷik kaṟkaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa. ataṉ aṭippakutiyil taṉikkūṟākac citinta paṭikakkal pāṟi irukkiṟatu. piṇaṅkaḷit tāḻikaḷil vittup putippataṟkāka akaḻntu ovvoru piṇattiṟkum taṉittaṉiyāka uṭkuṭivu ceyyappaṭṭirukkum puḻivu pāṟikaḷil uḷa.
inta iṭukāṭṭil vāyciṟuttum uṭal peruttum nīṇṭa vaṭivam vāynta periya tāḻikaḷ pala akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tāḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi āḻattil putikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mēl maṭṭattiliruntu 3 aṭi mutal 12 aṭivari āḻamum uḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟiṉmītu oṉṟākavum putikkappaṭṭu irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇavum iṭantarukiṟatu. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya puti kuḻikaḷil itutāṉ mikap paranta iṭukāṭākum. paṇṭiya ceṉṉi mānilattil (iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭil) pala pakutikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa puti kuḻikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṅkum ātittanallūr pōṉṟa periya parappuḷḷa iṭukāṭukaḷum, atika eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa tāḻikaḷum (puti kalaṉkaḷum) kāṇap paṭavilli.
īmattāḻikaḷum avaṟṟiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷum
īmattāḻikaḷum avaṟṟiṉuḷḷē elumpukaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu pōṉṟa tātupporuḷkaḷā lāṉa ēṉaṅkaḷum, kūlapporuḷkaḷum tuṇimaṇikaḷum vittup putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟippaṟṟi virivākap piṉvarum pakutikaḷil viḷakkam tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷil vittup putikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷ eṉṟum avi avarkaḷukku viruppamāṉaviyeṉṟum iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ maṟu ulaka vāḻkkiyil payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷumāṟu avarkaḷuṭaṉ vittup putikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷil, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷum, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷum vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷāṉa erumi, āṭu, māṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ veṇkala uruvaṅkaḷum kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷāṉa puli, yāṉi, maṟi, māṉ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷum iruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum makkaḷ aṇiyum vaḷi, mōtiram, kāppu, kaṭakam, kaṭukkaṉ pōṉṟaviyum uḷa. paṟavi uruvaṅkaḷum, veṇkala aritaṭṭu (sieves) kiṇṇa vaṭivilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kiṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟamuḷḷa pakuti meṉmiyāṉa aṭiyil poruttappaṭṭa takaṭu kaṉattatu. kiṇṇat til tuḷiyiṭutal (perforation) palvēṟu vakiyil uḷa. pāṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ mītu irumpuk karuviyāl aḻuttappaṭṭa puḷḷi vaṭivaṅkaḷ aṇiaṇiyāy aḻakuṟa amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ciṟappāka aṭip pākattic cuṟṟi orē naṭuviṭamuḷḷa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum, ari vaṭṭaṅ kaḷum tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil caṭṭattic cuṟṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu poruntumāṟum poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mēlum pala maṉita elumpukaḷum maṇṭiyōṭukaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil oru taliyōṭu ciṟappāka nilattiṉiṉṟu muḻuvatum nīṅkiya tāḻiyiṉ aṭippakutiyiṉiṉṟu ataṉ aṭippaṭi uruvam ciṟitum kuṉṟātu cīraḻiyātu nallanili yil eṭukkappaṭṭatu. it tāḻiyil periya aḷavil elumpukaḷ aṭaṅki iruntaṉa. ciṟiya elumpukaḷ citintum cīrkeṭṭum pōyiruntaṉa. ki elumpukaḷ taliyōṭṭiṟkuk kīḻē kūṭutalāka iruntaṉa. mutukelumpum, vilāvelumpum ēṉiya ciṟiya elumpu kaḷum uṭampiṟkup piṉṉāl irukkum eṉṟu evarum etir pārppar. āṉāl, tāḻiyil elumpukkūṭu uṭkārntirukkum niliyil, keñcip piṟar ātaravit tēṭum niliyil vikkappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa elumpuk kūṭukaḷil muḻu elumpukaḷum aṭaṅki iruppatu cilavaṟṟiltāṉ kāṇappeṟṟaṉa. ivi kiṭṭattaṭṭa mūṉṟaṭik kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷa periya tāḻikaḷiltām peritum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
muṉivar tarcuṭṭaṉ (Dr. Thurston) ātittanallūr tali yōṭukaḷil ceṉṉip paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccāliyiṉ cēkarippil uḷḷa, aḵtāvatu nalla niliyil uḷḷa - āṟu tali yōṭukaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷi namakku aḷittuḷḷār. avaṟṟiṉ aḷavukaḷ aṭiyil varuvaṉavākum :
			taliyōṭṭiṉ nīḷat

intat taliyōṭukaḷil iraṇṭu tāṭi elumpukaḷ nīṇṭaṉavāka iruppatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil uḷḷavaṟṟil āntirar taliyōṭukaḷilum tamiḻar jliyōṭukaḷilummvaravarkaḷuṭiyakrapukTWkaḷna‹kupuyppaṭukiṉṟaṉa.kY« tiru. tarcuṭṭaṉ iṉṟiya tamiḻakattil vāḻum nīṇṭa taliyōṭṭiyuṭiyavarkaḷil (Dolichocephalic) vakuppil eñciyirukkum mika nīṇṭa taliyōṭṭiyuṭiyavarkaḷ (Hyper dolichocephalic) vakuppu iṉaṅkaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa aḷavu vēṟṟumikaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu uḷḷaṉaveṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷār :

3.	vēḷāḷaṉ	40	67.9, 69.6
iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭampic cuṭukāṭṭiliṭṭuc cuṭṭapiṉ anta elumpukaḷit tērnteṭukkum intiya muṟiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu ātittanallūril cāvu viṉikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutalām. eṉṟālum aṅkup piṇaṅkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ illi. všyh Éjkhd f©L ão¥òfS« m§F vY«òfŸ òij¡f¥ g£ld v‹w c©ikia¤jh‹ cz®¤âíŸsd.*
cila elumpukaḷil pañcāṭiyiṉ pativaṭiyāḷam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tātupporuḷkaḷiṉ turuvōṭu iṇintu nīṇṭa nāḷāka oruppaṭṭiruntatiṉ cāyal naṉku terikiṟatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷil arici, tiṉi, cāmi pōṉṟa kūlap poruḷkaḷ tāḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tuṇiyip pōlaṉṟi ataṟku māṟāka ōr aṅkula nīḷattil tuṇṭutuṇṭāka aprēkattiṉ uṭinta tuṇṭukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cila poruḷkaḷ kallālāṉa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṅkiya cenniṟam vāynta maṇippācikaḷum veṇkalappācikaḷum kaḻuttu mālikaḷumākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. putikuḻikaḷukku veḷiyē cantaṉakkaṭṭiyiṉ aravikkallum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟāka ventu uṟutiyākavum aḻakākavum kāṇappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷuḷḷē kallāl ceyyappeṟṟa cila pāttiraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇṇi melliyatākat taṭṭi aḻakiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷāka vaṉintu cūḷiyil iṭṭu vēkavikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi civappu, kaṟuppu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil kaviṉuṟak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu vaṇṇaṅ kaḷum oṉṟu cērntu orumittu oḷirkiṉṟaṉa. orucila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ vaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyum, cuṇṇam tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉa vāyum polivuṟṟiruntaṉa. ivi ciṟiya aḷavil aḻaku ceyyap paṭṭiruntaṉa. aṭuttaṭuttuḷḷa cila puḷḷikaḷ caṭṭattic cuṟṟi mūliviṭṭattōṭu niralpaṭat tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tāḻikaḷ kaṉam vāyntaṉavāyum cevvaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyum karaṭu muraṭākavum ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
ātittanallūrp paṟampu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟikku oru vaṟṟāta karuvūla ūṟṟāka nilavukiṟatu. aḵtu eṭukka eṭukkak kuṟiyāta viliyuyarnta paḻamporuḷkaḷit taṉṉakattē aṭakki vittuk koṇṭirukkum kaḷañciyamākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. iṅku īmakkāṭṭil namatu mūtātiyarkaḷāl cēmittu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷa paḻam poruḷkaḷ pala uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ cila maṭṭumē akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭu ayalnāṭukaḷilum intiyāvilu muḷḷa paḻam poruḷ kāṭciccālikaḷil kavarcciyāṉa kāraṇap poruḷākap paṇpuruvam vāynta pāl poruḷāka matikkappeṟṟuk kavarcci yāṉa uyariya iṭaṅkaḷil vikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkup periya aḷavil mūṉṟu akaḻāyvukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyilum cilar akaḻāyvu naṭattip pala ariya poruḷkaḷi eṭuttut tiraṭṭi vittuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅku naṭipeṟṟa ovvōr akaḻāyvum putiya oḷiyiṉi vaḻaṅki eviyēṉum cila putiya poruḷkaḷi aḷittu uḷḷaṉa. āyvu naṭattiya aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē pala putiya karuttukkaḷi arumpac ceytuḷḷaṉa. atōṭu itip pōṉṟa payaṉuḷḷa akaḻāyvu ituvari tōṉṟavilli. intiyā vil varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ciṉṉaṅkaḷi aṭakki yirunta pala pāṅkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, etiyum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷukku oppuk kūṟa muṭiyātu. iḵtaṉṟi ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ akaṉṟa periya nilapparappu veḷiyil mikutiyāṉa aḷavu iṉṉum toṭap paṭāmalē iruntu varukiṟatu. kiḻakkiluḷḷa mēṭṭunilam aḵtā vatu ātittanallūr kirāma ellikkuṭpaṭṭa iṭam periya aḷavil kaṉṉi nilamāka iṉṉum iruppatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. inta iṭatti ōr aṭi āḻam akaḻntu pārttāl mēṭṭu nilattiliruntu mēṟparappil maḻi nīrāl arittuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭa caraḷ taḷam kāṇappaṭu kiṟatu. ataṉkīḻ āṟu aṭi āḻattiṟkuc cāmpal, elumpukaḷ, vilaṅ kiṉ kompukaḷ, uṭinta ōṭṭut tuṇṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kalanta maṇ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. curukkamākak kūṟiṉāl kirāmattil uḷḷa paḻaṅkālak kaḻivup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collalām.
akaḻāyviṟ kaṇṭa ariya poruḷkaḷ
akaḻāyvup poruḷkaḷil tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa elumpukaḷ taliyōṭukaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiviṭac ciṟappāṉa poruḷkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷ eṉalām. tātup poruḷkaḷil poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliyavi ciṟappāṉavi. poṉ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ taliyil kaṭṭappaṭum paṭṭattiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu. veṇkalam pāttiraṅkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavi ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭatu. irumpu poṉṉālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷiyum vēṟuvakiyāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum ceyvataṟku uriya karuvikaḷic ceyyap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. taccuvēli, kollu vēli, kaṉṉār vēli, kalvēli, poṟkollar vēli mutaliyavaṟṟukku iṉṟiyamiyāta uḷi, cuttiyal, paṟṟuk koraṭu, piṭittu arāvum aram, iḻippuḷi mutaliya karuvikaḷ ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭatu. uḻavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum koḻu, arivāḷ, paṉṉarivāḷ, kaṭappāṟi, maṇveṭṭi mutaliya karuvikaḷ ceyyavum, vīṭṭu vēlikku uriya arivāḷmaṇi tēṅkāyt tiruki, katti, veṭṭarivāḷ, karaṇṭi, curaṇṭi, akappi pōṉṟavi ceyyavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. palvēṟu vakiyāṉa paṭik kalaṉkaḷum irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavi kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṅkuk kiṭikkappeṟṟa ēṉaṅkaḷukkum palaki mūṭikaḷ pōṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irumpuk karuvikaḷukku marattiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭa kippiṭikaḷ poruttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷiṉ uḷḷē yum, veḷiyēyum ērāḷamāṉa eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil cempō veṇkalamō kiṭiyātu. vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu cempum, veṇkalamum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu iṅkup pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷi nāṭṭiṉ uyarnta iṉattārkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭutti vantuḷḷaṉar. atu tamiḻ nāṭṭil kiṭitta tātupporuḷākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ kālaṭi eṭuttu vippataṟku muṉpē, poṉ ceyyum taṭṭārkaḷum, maravēli ceyyum taccarkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṉiyum maṭkōvarkaḷum, nūlāṭikaḷ neyyum kikkōḷarkaḷum, vittukkaḷic cekkiliṭṭu āṭṭi ney eṭukkum eṇṇey vaṇikarkaḷum, makkaḷuṭiya uṭalukku uṟum piṇikaḷi akaṟṟum maruttuvarkaḷumākap palvēṟu toḻilāḷarkaḷ iruntu vantaṉar. palvēṟu toḻilāḷarkaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷ ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭikkap peṟṟamiyiṉik koṇṭu toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻnāṭṭil pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ iruntu vantaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām.5

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
III
1.	Proceedings of the Madras Government No. 329 of 27th March 1876.
2.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur by A. Rea (Madras) 1915. Preface p. iii-v.
	(a)	“We commenced excavation in the side of a hill consisting chiefly of quarts gravel with holders of the same material and resting on gneiss rock and within a superficial area of twenty five or thirty square yards, we discovered from twenty to thirty baked ear thern pots varying in size from three feet nine by three feet six down to ten inches either way, of very various shapes and of shapes in most cases more elegant and of a better manufacture than any one sees at the present day in the bazaars.
	“These pots when examined, were found to contin besides earth, gravel and stones a variety of objects of great interest” to the antiquarian the ethnologist and to Science generally.
	“Upwards of fifty kind of baked earthernware utensil of all sizes and shapes, a considerable number of iron weapons and implements chiefly knives or short sword-blades and hatchets, and a great quantity of bones and skulls were discovered. In one very interesting case two small pots were found within a large one, together with the bones as in most cases of a nearly complete skeleton, contining what it was impossible to mistake for anything else but the outer coats of grins itself had disappeared, but outer coating-probably of silica-had remined.
	“Several places at considerable distances, one at least 300 or 400 yards a from out principal excavation, were found to contin similar sepulchral urns, and the surface of the quarts hill above was strewn over with broken pieces of pottery of a similar character. Our excavation in all probability only touched one point of an extensive ancient cemetery or catacomb in which the dead of an age, the habits of which are probably quite unknown in India at the present day, are disposed of in this singular manner, that is to say, by placing the skeleton or the body inside a large earthenware vase or pot together with two, three or four small pots together with two, three or four small pots apparently contining food for the dead and weapons and implements of iron ready for use when he reached another world”.
	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras. 1915 - Introduction.
3.	Ibid. Preface 1.
4.	Ibid. Introduction p. 1-5.
5.	A History of India by James H. Gense S.J. (Madras) 1965. 
pp. 16-17.
	“Aryans were the first invaders of India. They came originally from the treeless grass lands or stepps of South Russia. They were peaceful farmers, the word” “Aryan” meaning “tiller of the earth”.
	(1)	The Punjab. In the hymns of the Rig Veda the names of about twenty-five rivers are mentioned and these many rivers all belong to the system of Indus. They include the five great eastern branches of the Indus and a number of its north-western tributaries. We may therefore conclude that the earliest Aryan invaders made their homes partly to the west and parttl to the east of the Indus.
	(2)	Hindustan Proper. As years went on the decendants of the first immigrants probably increased by fresh arrivals travelled gradually eastwards and occupied the basins of the Jamuna and the Ganges or Hindustan proper ....
	(3)	TheVindhya Range line. In the Course of time the Aryans spread both southwards and eastwards conquered the whole country lying between the Himalayas and the Vindhya Range and reduced the Dasyus to slavery.
	The fresh advance is mentioned in the Laws of Manu. According to these the wide territories contined between the Himalayas and the Vindhyas Range are called Aryavarta or the country of the Aryans. In the land of Aryavarta there was one part spot or rather province, that was privileged above all the others. This was Brahmavarta, the Holy land, situated due north of modern Delhi and to the south of the upper course of the Sutlej between the river Saraswathi and Drishadvati. It was in this region that the Rig Veda is believed to have been completed. Tehre is a couplet in the Mahabharata to the following effect : ‘Those who dwell in the Kurukshetra to the south of the Saraswathi and the north of the Drishadvati dwell in Heaven!’
	(4)	Aryan Penetration of the Deccan. The last stage in the Aryan conquest was a movement southwards. To the south lay the country which they called Dakshina of which the English world Deccan is sid to be a corruption. The Aryan penetration of the Deccan was chiefly confined to the spread of Aryan ideas and institutions among the Dravidians of the South. But this peaceful penetration of theSouth, does not belong to the Vedic Period, it took place many centuries afterwards.
