4. poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ
(GOLD DIADEMS)
ātittanallūrp paṟampil iṟutiyāka aṟiñar alekcāntar irīyā avarkaḷ naṭattiya tolporuḷ āyvil kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ pala. āṉāl, iṅku mutaṉmutalākak kuṟippiṭat takka poruḷkaḷ poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷēyākum. intap poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷ ātitta nallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ elumpu kaḷukkum maṇṇiṟkum ūṭē oḷi kuṉṟātu miṉṉeṉat tikaḻ vati aṟintu eṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṟiñar irīyā akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ mottam irupattu mūṉṟu poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭam kaṭṭal
ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil arum piya ariya poruḷ kaḷip paṟṟi aṟiñar irīyā avarkaḷ kūṟum poḻutu tāḻikaḷuḷ poṉṉālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷum veṇ kalattālāṉa caṭṭikaḷum viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum irumpā lāṉa paṭikkalaṅkaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷum ciṟu tāḻikaḷum piṟavum iruntaṉa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. poṉṉā lāṉa poruḷ paṭṭaṅkaḷ maṭṭumēyākum. avi iṟantārkku iyaṟṟum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟāṉa paṭṭam kaṭṭum cey muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. uyir nīttār neṟṟiyil ip paṭṭattik kaṭṭuvatu tamiḻar marapu. ippaṭṭam peritum muṭṭi vaṭivilē uḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa caturavaṭiviṉālāṉa orucila paṭṭaṅ kaḷum ōrōr iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭṭattiṉ iṟutiyil irupuṟamum kayiṟu allatu kampi kaṭṭuvataṟkuc ciṟiya aḷavil tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭṭam melliya poṉ takaṭṭil aḻaku poliya mukkōṇa vaṭivil kaṭuku pōṉṟa puḷḷikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ellām maṭittuc curukki vikkak kūṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. ivi iṟantārk kuriya caṭaṅkukaḷukkut takka kuṟiyīṭāka aminta poruḷkaḷākum eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu irīyā avarkaḷ viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār.
paṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ amippu
paṭṭaṅkaḷ potuvāka muṭṭi vaṭivil irukkum. ivi melliya poṉ takaṭṭil ceyyappaṭṭu iru nuṉikaḷilum tuḷiyiṭṭu nūl allatu melliya kampiyāl neṟṟiyil kaṭṭap paṭum. iḵtu iṟantārkku iyaṟṟum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟākum. iṟantārkku neṟṟiyil poṉ paṭṭam kaṭṭappaṭuvatu perum pālum civa vēḷāḷar marapiṉar iṉṟum ceytuvarum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟākum.
poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ cila tuḷiyiṭappaṭāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. poṉ paṭṭam mika melliyatāka iruppatāl ciṟiya irumpuk kampikaḷik koṇṭu eḷitākat tuḷiyiṭṭu appāl antak kampikaḷik koṇṭē paṭṭamākak kaṭṭi neṟṟiyil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ. ippoṟpaṭṭam cila iṭaṅkaḷil nīḷamāṉatākavum, aḻakiya mukkōṇa vaṭivākavum irukkiṟatu. āṉāl, muṉpuṟam puḷḷikaḷ kaṭukuvaṭivil aṇiaṇiyākat turuttik koṇṭirukkum. muṟkālattil paṭṭaṅkaḷ māṟṟuyarnta poṉṉilē ceytu kaṭṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa. iṉṟu poṉvili peritum uyarntupōṉatāl paṭṭam māṟṟukkuṟinta poṉṉil kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. palar paṭṭam kaṭṭum vaḻakkattiyē viṭṭum varukiṉṟaṉar. potuvākap poṉṉiṉ vili uyarvāl tamiḻarkaḷ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vaḻakkil ucciyiliruntu uḷḷaṅkāl vari uṟuppukaḷ aṉittilum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintu varum marapu vaḻakkaṅkaḷik kūṭa ovvoṉṟāka viṭṭuvarukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
mukkōṇa vaṭivil aṇikalaṉkaḷ pōl aḻakuṭaṉ tayārikkappeṟṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷ ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ piṉpuṟam muttu muttāy aḻakiya vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa ciṟu puḷḷikaḷ mēl eḻumpi niṟpaṉapōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa (uruvam 15 mutal 20 vari pārkka). mūṉṟu ciṟiya paṭṭaṅkaḷ tāḻnta ulōkattāl ceyyappaṭṭaviyākum (uruvam 18 mutal 20 variyuḷḷavi pārkka) muṭṭi vaṭivilāṉa neṭṭiyāṉa neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ 2.3/1611 7/811 mutal 6-5/811 2.5/32 variyuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoru ciṟiya paṭṭam oṭukkamāṉa kōṭukaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟatāy 15/16 nīḷamum 1/411 akalamum uḷḷatu. eṇ 1 aṭiyāḷa miṭappaṭṭa neṟṟippaṭṭattil iru variciyākat tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa puḷḷikaḷ uḷḷa iṇivāṉa kōṭukaḷ iraṇṭu nuṉikaḷilum toṭarntu cellukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭukaḷ naṭuvē ūṭuruvic ceṅkuttākac cellukiṉṟaṉa. ivi pōṉṟa iṇivāṉa kōṭukaḷ iru pakkaṅkaḷiṉ mītum cāyvāṉa kiḷi yāka etirppuṟam uḷḷaṉa. avi takaṭṭiṉ iṭivari mēṭṭi ottuḷḷaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṇkaḷ 2-10 paṭṭaṅkaḷi luḷḷa puḷḷikaḷ iru iṇivāṉa varikaḷ uḷḷa amippu iṟutivari vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavē ceṉṟuḷḷatu. atōṭu mūṉṟu iṇivāṉa kōṭukaḷ naṭuvē ceṅkuttākac cellukiṉṟaṉa.
eṇ 20 uḷḷa paṭṭattil pōyilikkāy vaṭivil kal nīrttuḷi pōṉṟu taṅkat takaṭṭil takatakaveṉṟu miṉṉumpaṭi cetukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉi aṟiñar irīyā aḻakupeṟa viḷakkikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. irīyā avarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭutta taṅkat takaṭukaḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ neṟṟiyil kaṭṭum paṭṭam eṉpati jē.ār. heṇṭarcaṉ. ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
cintuveḷip poṉpaṭṭam
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭilum iṟantupōṉavarkaḷiṉ cavac caṭaṅkil paṭṭaṅkaṭṭuvatum peruvaḻakkāka iruntatu eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷiyil naṭipeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa cuṇṇāmpuc cili namatu kavaṉatti īrppatāyuḷḷatu. atu tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ cāyalil uḷḷatu. aḵtu ōr aracar allatu purōkitar uruvampōl uḷḷatu. antac cili mārpiṟkuk kīḻē uṭintupōṉa tākat terikiṟatu. ataṉ mēl caṭṭikaḷ illi. ōraḻakāṉa pūvēlippāṭu uḷḷa pōrvi valap puṟamuḷḷa iṭuppi liruntu iṭappuṟattōḷmītu pōrttap paṭṭu mutukuppuṟamākat toṅkaviṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mukattil tāṭi mayir oṭṭa veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. akaṉṟa mukamum taṭṭiyāṉa taṭitta mūkkum periya utaṭum paranta neṟṟiyum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. neṟṟiyil oru melliya nāṭā vil paṭṭattik kōttup piṉpuṟam muṭiccuppōṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eñciya nāṭāviṉ ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷ piṉpuṟam aḻakuṟat toṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
itaṉic cintuveḷiyil akaḻ āyvu naṭattiya pēraṟiñarkaḷ car. cāṇ mārcal, car. mārṭṭiyar uyilar, ār.ṭi. pāṉarji, muṉivar i. cē. eyc. em. ecu. mekkē.vāṭcu pōṉṟa aṟiñarka ḷellārum eṭuttukkāṭṭit tirāviṭarkaḷ paṭṭam kaṭṭum marapu vaḻakkam uṭiyavarkaḷ eṉpataṟkum cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉpataṟkum ciliyiṉ neṟṟiyil uḷḷa paṭṭamum, ciliyiṉ muka amippum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭu kaḷākum eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar.3 cintuveḷiyiṉiṉṟu ātitta nallūrvari tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu nilaviyuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku ātitta nallūril akaḻntukaṇṭa poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷum cintuveḷiyil kiṭitta tirāviṭa aracaṉiṉ neṟṟi naṭuvil kāṇum paṭṭamum aḻiyāc cāṉṟukaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉalām.
aṟiñar kilparṭ cilēṭṭar āṅkilattil eḻutiya intiya nākarikattil tirāviṭap paṇpu eṉṟa cīriya nūlil ātitta nallūriṉ akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa neṟṟip poṉ paṭṭam tirāviṭa makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya ekiptu naṭunilak kaṭaṟpakuti yil kirīṭṭu, piṉiciyā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām vaḻakkil iruntatu eṉpati virivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atu varumāṟu :
mēlum ātittanallūrc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum mika melliyatāka aṭittut tuvikkappaṭṭa poṉ neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi perumpālum kurumārkaḷiṉ neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷāka amintiruntaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu. avi muṟṟilum micēṉāvilum kirīṭṭilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭavaṟṟip pōṉ ṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi tavira oru veṇkala uruvam ekimō iṉattavarukku uriya āṭi aṇinta ōr arampiyiṉ aḻakuruvi lum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intat taṅka neṟṟip paṭṭam oru puṟamum aitarāpāttuk kaṟkuviyalkaḷil uḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ uḷveṭṭuk kīṟṟukkaḷ maṟṟoru puṟamumāka niṉṟu āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar kaḷukkum, nōcācuvit (Knossus) talinakarākak koṇṭu vāḻnta kirīṭṭiṉ kaṭalōṭi nākarikattiṉarukkum toṭarpiruntirukka vaḻi yuṇṭu eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. pīṉiciyarkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭē miṉōvarkaḷ naṭunilakkaṭal tīraṅkaḷilum orukāl aṭlāṇṭit tīraṅkaḷilum ceṉṟu paranta toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. atēpōlac ceṅkaṭal, pāracīka vaḷikuṭā, intumākkaṭal ākiyavaṟṟiyum muṉintukāṇpatil muṉṉōṭikaḷāyirun tārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟac cāṉṟukaḷ illi. itaṟku māṟāka mīṉō varkaḷ mēṟku nōkkiyum pīṉiciyar kiḻakku mēṟku nōkkiyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa orēkālattilēyē paravat toṭaṅki iruntaṉar eṉṟu niṉikka iṭamuṇṭu.
eṉavē, cariyotta ivviru nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappukaḷukkum kāraṇamāṉa kappal kaṭṭum toḻilum kaṭal pōkkuvarattup paḻakkamum, miṉōvarkaḷiṉ putuvatu puṉiviṟkō, pīṉiciyariṉ putuvatu puṉiviṟkō uriyatu eṉṟu koḷvativiṭa ekipti yarukku uriyateṉṟu eṇṇuvaru ēṟṟamuṭiyatākum. aitarāpāttu kaṟkukip pāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, perumpālum pīṉiciyat toṭarpu, miṉōvat toṭarpu ākiya iraṇṭiṉ viḷivukaḷāka vum irukkalām. attuṭaṉ pīṉiciyak kurumārum itupōṉṟa neṟṟip paṭṭam aṇintiruntaṉar.
tirāviṭarkaḷ nēraṭiyākavō, cuṟṟumuṟiyākavō nōcācu (Knossus) nākarikattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākak kūṟalām. teyvaṅkaḷ civaṉippōl āṇpālāyiṉum cari, kāḷiyip pōl peṇpālāyiṉum cari, iru pālkaḷumē mikippaṭuttap paṭṭu nuṇṇiṭi uṭiyavarāka atuvum iṭiyē oṭukkuvataṟ kēṟṟa karuvi etuvum illātapaṭi iṭi oṭuṅkiyavarākavē amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. miṉōvakak kaliyil iṭi iṉṉum ciṟitu. āṇkaḷum, peṇkaḷum iṭiyil oṭṭiyāṇam aṇipa varkaḷāyk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. itu kuḻantip paruva mutal toṭarntu vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. iṭiyic ciṟukka vikkum vaḻakkam intiyāvil paravāviṭiṉum ap paḻakkatti aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭa kalimarapu maṭṭum intiyā vari vantu paraviṟṟu eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
tamiḻar paṇpāṭāṉa poṉpaṭṭam kaṭṭum paḻakkamum, oṭṭiyāṇam aṇiyum vaḻakkamum, peṇkaḷukku iṭi curuṅki iruttal aḻaku eṉṟa tamiḻar kaṇṭa aḻakuc ciṟappum kirīṭṭu, ekiptu, pīṉiciyā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷi lellām paraviyuḷḷatu eṉṟu naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.4
teṉ intiya makkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiṉiṉṟu - ciṟappāka ulōkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷip periya aḷavil aṇintuvarum vaḻakkam uṭiyavar eṉpatu namatu kavaṉattiṟku varukiṉṟatu. irumpukkālattil uḷḷa iṭukāṭukaḷil poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu pala paṭikkaruvikaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷum putikkappaṭṭu vantatuḷḷaṉa. aṟiviṟ keṭṭāta paḻaṅkālattiliruntu palvēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum palvēṟu aḷavil poṉaṇikalaṉkaḷiyum maṇikaḷ iḻitta poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum ceyvatil tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ kivaṇṇam vāyntavarkaḷ eṉpataṟku ipporuḷ cāṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mutalil poṉṉik kaṇṭa nāṭu
intiyāvilē, mutaṉmutalākat tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa ulōkap poruḷ poṉ ākum. atu vaṇika muṟiyil pala ūḻikkālaṅkaḷil eṅkum paraviyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu intiya araciṉ kaṭumiyāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷāl poṉ kiṭippatu aritākiviṭṭatu. viliyō naccuppōl ēṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟālum, tamiḻaka makaḷiriṉ poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyum marapuvaḻakkam kiviṭa muṭiyāta tāy irukkiṟatu. kāraṇam avarkaḷiṭiyē paṇṭu toṭṭu nilavi varum intap paḻakkam, camayattōṭum, kulattōṭum iṇikkap paṭṭatāka iruppatēyākum. itaṉi aṟiñar vi.ār. irāmacantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ tam nūlil virivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
intiyāvil - ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil tirumaṇattil peṇ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatum piḷḷip paruvattilē kātu kuttik kaṭukkaṉ aṇivippatum tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu āṇum peṇṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭu aḻakuṟa iruppatum ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷ kūṭa palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭiruppa tum, teyva uruvaṅkaḷukkut taliyiliruntu kālkaḷ vari poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṭṭiyiruppatum, teyvaṅkaḷum aracarkaḷum poṟṟērkaḷilum poṉ pallakkukaḷilum ulāvaruvatum maṉṉarkaḷ poṟkācukaḷ veḷiyiṭṭu varuvatum nām aṟiyum poḻutu paṇṭiya intiyāvil ērāḷamākap poṉ iruntatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. intiyāviṉiṉṟu poṉ ekiptu, mecapot tāmiyā, ellam, pālattīṉam mutaliyanāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭatākak kūṭat terikiṟatu.5
poṉviḷiyum pūmi
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil ērāḷamākap poṉ viḷintatu. paṇṭit tamiḻakattil poṉviḷinta kaḷattūr, poṉṉēri, poṉṉāṇi, poṉṉūr, poṉ amarāvati, poṉvayal, poṉ mukanallūr eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷilellām poṉ kiṭittuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻakattil mutaṉmutalākak kiṭikkap peṟṟa mañcaḷvaṇṇattil takatakaveṉa oḷiyūṭṭiya tātupporu ḷukkup poṉ eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. avarkaḷ aṉṟu poṉṉi māḻi (ulōkam) eṉṟa poruḷiltāṉ peyar cūṭṭiṉar. piṉṉark kiṭitta karuppu vaṇṇam vāynta tātup poruḷukkuk karumpoṉ eṉṟu peyar aḷittaṉar. atu piṉṉar, irumpāyiṟṟu, veṇmi niṟamāṉa tātupporuḷik kaṇṭatum ati veṇpoṉ eṉṟaṉar. atu iṉṟu veḷḷiyāyt tiripuṟṟatu. civappu niṟamāṉa tātup poruḷic cempoṉ eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. atu piṟkālattil cempu eṉṟum ceppu eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭatu.6
tamiḻakattil poṉ ērāḷamākak kiṭittatu. vaṇika muṟi yil yavaṉa nāṭṭiṉar poṉṉik kācukaḷākavum poṟpāḷaṅ kaḷākavum, poṟkaṭṭikaḷākavum ērāḷamākap peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻakat til āṅkila āṭci arumpu muṉ makkaḷ ucciyiliruntu uḷḷaṅ kālvari poṉ aṇikaḷiyum maṇikaḷiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar. poṉṉiḻi koṇṭa paṭṭāṭikaḷip puṉintu vantaṉar. poṉvaṭṭililum, poṉkumpāvilum uṇavu uṇṭum poṟ kiṇṇaṅkaḷilum, poṉ kalayaṅkaḷilum nīr aruntiyum vantaṉar. uṭal uramuṟa, mēṉi civakka, piṇi akalap poṉṉiṟ ceyta maruntiṉi (poṉpacupam) arunti vantaṉar. tirukkōyil vimāṉaṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa kuṭaṅkaḷum, vāyil kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa kuṭaṅkaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa. tilli yil uḷḷa āṭavallāṉiṉ uruvam poṉṉāl vārkkappeṟṟa tāy iruntatu. anta āṭavallāṉ āṭum tirucciṟṟampalam poṉ ōṭukaḷ vēyappeṟṟu poṉṉampalam eṉṟu iṉṟum pukaḻn tēttap peṟṟu varukiṟatu. palvēṟu kōyilkaḷil tiruvulāp paṭimikaḷ poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟavikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. tirukkōyilkaḷiṉ poṉ viḷakku vittu ney vārttu ēṟṟip pēṟu peṟṟavar nam nāṭṭil palar uṇṭu. iṟivaṉ tiruvuruvip poṟṟēril ēṟṟi ulā varacceytaṉar. teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷi muḻukkāṭṭa, poṟkuṭaṅkaḷum teyvaṅkaḷukku uṇavu paṭikkap poṟṟaṭṭum poṟkalaṅkaḷum palar nērttikkaṭaṉāka aḷitta taṟkuk kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷum, vaḷḷalkaḷum, pulavarkaḷukkum pāṇarkaḷukkum, pāṭiṉikaḷukkum, viṟaliyarkaḷukkum poṟ ṟāmarippūvum poṟkiḻiyum, poṟ kācukaḷum, poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷum variyātaḷittup pērum perumiyum īṭṭiṉar eṉpa taṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. araṇmaṉi kaḷilum, kōyilkaḷilumuḷḷa yāṉikaḷ viḻānāṭkaḷil (poṉ mukapaṭāmum), poṉ ōṭiyum, kompukaḷil kimpuri eṉṉum poṉ pūṇkaḷum, poṉ ampārikaḷum tāṅki iruntaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kōnakaraṅkaḷil poṉ vaṇikarkaḷiṉ kaṭit terukkaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅku, cātarūpam, kiḷicciṟi eṉṟa māṟṟuk kuṟinta iḷam poṉvakikaḷum āṭakam, cāmpūnatam eṉṟa māṟṟuyarnta cempoṉ vakikaḷum viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.
māṟṟuyarnta pacumpoṉṉit tamiḻar pattari māṟṟup pimpoṉ eṉpar. itu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟkum, maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉ camippataṟkum payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟatu. pacum poṉ kuḻivāṉatu; atu kuḻiyāmal irukkavum, vaḷiyāmal irukkavum ataṉōṭu, ciṟitu cempu kalantu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytaṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaṇikam naṭattip pērum perumiyum peṟṟavarkaḷ. mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē marakkalaṉkaḷ kaṭṭi ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, ilaṅki, maliyam, cāvakam pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam vaḷarttavarkaḷ, yavaṉa nāṭṭiṟku miḷaku, cantaṉam, muttu, ēlam, kirāmpu, melliya tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟi ēṟṟumati ceytu ataṟku īṭākap poṟkaṭṭikaḷi yum poṟkācukaḷiyum peṟṟup poṉṉi maliyeṉak kuvittavarkaḷ.7
aḻakiya peṇṇip poṟpāvi eṉṟu pukaḻntu pōṟṟiṉar. eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikaḷ aṇinta arampiyarip poṉ kāytta pūṅkoṭi eṉap pukaḻntaṉar. civaperumāṉip poṉṉār mēṉiyaṉē eṉṟu ēṟṟip pōṟṟiṉar.
putiya kaṟkālattiṉ naṭuvilē tamiḻnāṭu poṉṉiyum ataṉit tōṇṭiyeṭuppataṟku ēṟṟa irumpuk karuvikaḷiyum periya aḷavil peṟṟiruntatu. kaltēyarkaḷiṉ (Chaldean) kalveṭṭukaḷil ūr nakaril poṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatāka aṟikiṉṟōm. antap poṉ anta nāṭṭiluḷḷataṉṟu. ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. anta ayal nāṭukaḷ intiyāvum āppirikkāvumēyākum. teṉ intiyap poṉ, āppirikkāvil viṟkappaṭṭatu. āppirikkā antap poṉṉil tēviyāṉa aḷavu vittukkoṇṭu eñciya pakutiyi kaltēyāviṟku viṟṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl āppirikkāvip poṉ viḷiyum nāṭākak karutuvatu tavaṟu, eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm. 1927ām āṇṭu, aṟiñar liyōṉāṭu ulli (Leonard Willey), ūr nakaril naṭattiya akaḻ āyvil cupāttu araci (Queen Subath) putikkappaṭṭa kallaṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭittu aṅkup pala poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum poṉ taliyaṇikaḷ, poṉ nāṭā, poṉ mōtiram poṟṟakaṭṭāl pōrttappeṟṟa yāḻ (Harp) pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum kaṇṭupiṭittār. cālṭiyāvi aṭutta mecapottāmiyāvil muṟkālattil poṉ atikam kāṇap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl anta nāṭu intiyāviṟkup peritum kaṭamip paṭṭa nāṭākum eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.8
poṉṉi atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttiya paḻam perunāṭu ekiptu. aṅku iṟantapiṉ maṉṉar uṭalukkup poṉ aṅki cāttappaṭṭatu. pārō maṉṉarkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aracu kaṭṭilip peṟṟiruntaṉar. annāṭu intiyāviṉiṉṟum, mecapottāmiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum poṉṉip peṟṟatu. ekiptil maṉitap paṇpu paravuvataṟku intiyā āṟṟiya perum paṅkē kāraṇamākum. poṉṉip pīṉiciyā, kirī mutaliya piṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷ palvēṟu ceyalkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉa. cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷukkup poṉ tēvip paṭṭatu. eṉavē, cintuveḷi teṉ intiyāvōṭu uṟavu koṇṭatu. teṉ intiyā, taṉ iṉa uṟavuḷḷa cintuveḷi nāṭukaḷukku ērāḷamāṉa aḷavu poṉṉi ēṟṟumati ceytatu. teṉ intiyā viltāṉ mutal mutalākap poṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. teṉ intiyāvil ērāḷamāṉa irumpuk karuvikaḷ iruntatāl virivil periya aḷavil poṉ akaḻntu kuvikkap peṟṟatu.9 ataṉāl, eṉ naṇpar oruvar, āṅkila āṭciyiṉpōtu,
poṉviḷinta pūmieṉṉum intiyāvē - iṅkup
pucikkuṇa vaṟṟateṉṉa intiyāvē
eṉṟu mukārip paṇṇil oru pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
iṉṟu ulakiluḷḷa iculāmiyarkaḷum yūtarkaḷum kiṟittavar kaḷum puṉitamāṉa malikkōyilāṉa erucalēm tirukkōyilip paṟṟi naṉku aṟivar. atu cālamōṉ aracar avarkaḷāl iṟṟikku 2,978 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kaṭṭiyeḻuppap peṟṟatu. appuṉita māṉa tirukkōyili naṭuvākakkoṇṭu (kiplāvākak koṇṭu) oru kālattil ulakameṅkumuḷḷa iṟippaṟṟāḷarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu muculim aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.10 akkōyilik kaṭṭiya cālamōṉ aracar (cālamōṉñāṉi) intiyāviṟkup pīṉiciyak kappalkaḷi aṉuppi, cantaṉakkaṭṭi, yāṉikkompu, mayiliṟaku, kuraṅku, poṉ pōṉṟavaṟṟi vāṅkivaracceytu antat tirukkōyilic ciṟappuṟac ceytār. pīṉiciyak kappal intiyāvil uḷḷa uvarit tuṟimukattiṟku vantu apporuḷkaḷi vāṅkic ceṉṟuḷḷatu. antat tuṟimukattiṉ peyar opir (Ophir) eṉṟu pipiḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cilar opir āppirikkāvil uḷḷatu eṉṟum, cilar cōmālilāntil uḷḷatu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. opir eṉṟu pipiḷ kuṟippiṭum iṭam uvaritāṉ; inta uvarip paṭṭiṉam nelli māvaṭṭattic cērnta nāṅkunēri vaṭṭārat tiṉ kaṭaṟkariyil uḷḷa ūrēyākum. itaṉi nallāyar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. intiyāvil mutalāvatu kiṟittavak kaṇkāṇiyar (Bishop) āka vanta nallāyar vi.ecu. acariyā avarkaḷum ātarittuḷḷār. avarkaḷuṭiya uṭaṉ piṟantārāṉa vi.ecu. jhkR v«.V., avarkaḷ ceṉṉik kiṟittavak kallūri itaḻ oṉṟil ēṟattāḻa 60 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ opir eṉpatu uvarippaṭṭiṉantāṉ eṉṟu uṟuticeytu oru muttiri aṭittuḷḷār. āppirikkāvil mayiliṟakum, cantaṉamum illi eṉpati aṟiyāta cilar opir āppirikkāvil uḷḷatu eṉṟu colliviṭṭaṉar eṉa ariya āppuk kaṭāviyuḷḷār. ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭilkūṭa, poṉ kiṭittiruppatik koṇṭu poṉ mikutiyākat teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷatu eṉpati aṟiyalām.
irumpum poṉṉum
irumpu ūḻiyil irunta makkaḷāl, teṉintiyāvil mikappaḻaṅkālattil irumpiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākap poṉ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉiyāvil - ciṟappāka ūr nakaril poṉ mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatāka kaltēyāvil (Chaldean) kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷ veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. poṉṉip, paḻaṅkālak kaltēya makkaḷ vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭuvanta poruḷākak koṇṭaṉar eṉpataṟku atu cāṉṟu tarukiṟatu. mikattolivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷiliruntu ūrukku (UR¡F)¢ contamāṉa paḻaṅkālak kappalkaḷ poṉṉik koṇṭuvantaṉa eṉpatāka atil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intat tolivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ intiyāvum, āppirikkāvumēyākum.
mecapottāmiyāvil intap pimpoṉ ulōkam, mikap paḻaṅkālattil paravalāka mikunta aḷavil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirunta taṟku annāṭu intiyāviṟku mikavum kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiyap pacum poṉṉip paḻaṅkālattil peritum payaṉpaṭuttiya maṟṟoru nāṭu paḻam perum ekiptu ākum. poṉṉi nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoru pakutikkum oru nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoru nāṭṭukkum koṇṭu celvatu eḷitāka iruntatu. ekiptil intiyap poṉ pāyntu ceṉṟatu paṟṟi yārum viyap paṭivataṟkilli; anta nāṭṭil puṉitamāṉa maṉita nākarikam paravuvataṟku intiyarkaḷē peritum kāraṇamāka iruntaṉar. piṟkālattil pīṉiciyarkaḷum, kirēkkarkaḷum ēṉiya airōppiya nāṭṭiṉarkaḷum intat tātup poruḷip palvēṟu kāriyaṅ kaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. cintu veḷiyil kūṭap poṉṉiṟku mikutiyākat tēvi iruntatu eṉṟu nām kūṟiṉāl atu perum tavaṟu eṉṟu evarum eṇṇār. cintuveḷikkum teṉ intiyāviṟkum iṭiyē aṭikkaṭi naṭanta vaṇikap pōkku varattut toṭarpil teṉ intiyā antap pakutikkup peritum poṉṉi eṟṟumati ceytu irukka vēṇṭum.
poṉṉip paṟṟiya āyvil pukunta āciriyarkaḷ palarum teṉ intiyāvil poṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa tātupporuḷ eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ. intiyā oru kālattil poṉṉiṉ nilam eṉṟu pativu ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. teṉ intiyāvil oru cila māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ maṭṭuntāṉ poṉṉit tāṅkum taṉittaṉmi vāynta taḷamāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu uṇmiyākum. intiyāvil cila pakutikaḷil maṭṭum kiṭittatālum atu kiṭippataṟku aritāka irunta tālum poṉ viliyuyarnta tātupporuḷāka matikkappaṭṭatu.
iti naṭuvuniliyil niṉṟu ārāyntāl, paḻaṅkālatti liruntu intiyat tīvakattil poṉṉi nīrmit tōyppu (Washing Gold) ceyyum vaḻakkam iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākum. āṟṟu maṇaliṉum, poṉ uṭkoṇṭa pāṟi aṭukkukaḷilum (Gravels), vaṇṭal cārnta paṭukiyamivilum poṉṉi alaci eṭukkum muṟi iruntu vantatu. intat tātup poruḷi nīril alacum vaḻakkam, muṟkālattil cila variyaṟukkappaṭṭa pakutikaḷil poṉ cēkarikkappaṭṭatik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. āṉāl, itaṟku appāl teṉ intiyat tīvakattil ērāḷamākak koṭu varippāṟi (Schist) kaṉma īruyirakiyuṭaṉ poṉṉum kalanta kaṉimap poruḷum uḷḷa pāṟi eṉṉum iraṇṭum ērāḷamākak kalanta poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉip paṇpuṭiyatāy amintiruppati nām naṉkaṟivōm eṉṟālum, paḻaṅkal ūḻiyiliruntu iṉṟu vari intap poṉ eṉṉum tātupporuḷiṉ payaṉi naṉku aṟiyap paṭṭiruntālum paṇṭik kāla makkaḷ vāḻnta malikaḷilum kuṉṟukaḷilum paṟampukaḷilum uḷḷa poṉṉiṉ ulōkak karu (Gold Ores)it¡ kaṇṭupiṭippataṟkup pōtiya muṉṉēṟṟam maṉitaṉ iṉṟuvari peṟavilli.
makkaḷ piṟkālattil ataṉuṭiya taṉicciṟappil uyirt tuṭippuṭaṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. poṉṉip peṟuvataṟkum, poṉṉiṉ karuvik kāṇavum, ceyaṟki muṟiyil poṉṉic ceyyavum maṉitaṉ taṉ kālattiyum āṟṟaliyum, poruḷiyum atikamākac celaviṭṭuḷḷāṉ. nīla maliyil namvōluk koṭi amcāvil (Namvolukodi Amsham) uḷḷa tēvālam eṉṉum iṭattil ērāḷamāṉa mikap paḻamiyāṉa poṟcuraṅka vāyiṟ kuḻikaḷ (Mining Shafts) ākiyavaṟṟik kāṭṭuvataṟkuc cāṉṟākap pala uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟukku appāl periya kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷ 70-80 aṭi uyarattil nīṇṭu vaḷarntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tēvāla (Devala) eṉṉum iṭattic cuṟṟip palvēṟu vakiyil poṉṉik kaṇṭupiṭikkum paṇikaḷ naṭi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṉum cāṉṟi mēṟpōnta vāṟākap pārkkak kūṭātu. oru kālattil tamiḻ makkaḷ iṅkup poṉṉik kaṇṭu piṭippatil perum paṇiyāṟṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa. ippoḻutum kūṭa poṉṉi eṭukkum paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷ viyakkattakka muṟikaḷil takaval tēṭupavarkaḷāy iruntu varukiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum iṉṟiya makkaḷ vaṇṭal cārnta nīrmit tōyvil tam naṭavaṭikkikaḷil kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭuk kiṭantu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. paḻiya poṟ curaṅkaṅkaḷ paḻamic ciṉṉaṅkaḷākap pōṟṟappeṟum niliyil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cāṉṟuṟuti aḷikkum mēmpaṭṭa nili eyti yuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa kālak kaṇippu muṟi cārnta muṟiyil variyaṟuttu nilināṭṭuvataṟku muṭiyātirukkiṟatu.
muṟkālattil curaṅkam uḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷ poṉṉi akaḻn teṭuppatil atika ārvam kāṭṭi vantaṉa. inta ūrkaḷ paṇṭu taṉic ciṟappip peṟṟiruntaṉa. atāvatu ūrkaḷic cuṟṟi araṇkaḷ eḻuppap peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpatākum. inta viliyuyarnta tātup poruḷi akaḻntu eṭukka evvāṟu paṇṭik kāla maṉitaṉ poṟuppōṭu paṇiyāṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyi nām paḻiya aḻipāṭukaḷiliruntu tokukka muṭiyum. āṉāl, irumpu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭu, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu, potu makkaḷiṭiyē paravat toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarē poṟ curaṅkaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu poṉṉi eṭukkum paṇi perum aḷavil naṭantirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟuvatu nalla poruttamākum. māperum maraṅkaḷ tōṉṟi vāṉuṟa vaḷarntu niṟkum kāṭukaḷil uḷḷa paḻiya curaṅkaṅkaḷi nām āyvu ceytu kāṇumpōtu, paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ curaṅkaṅkaḷit tōṇṭi, poṉṉi eṭuppatil evvaḷavu viruppam uṭiyavar kaḷāy iruntaṉar eṉpati aṟiyalām. ac curaṅkaṅkaḷip pātu kāttu vanta avarkaḷ aṟiyalām. ac curaṅkaṅkaḷip pātukāttu vanta avarkaḷ ceyal poruḷātāranili mēṉmi yaṭivataṟku utaviyāka iruntatu.10
paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ teṉ intiyā poṉṉiṉ tāyakamākap pōṟṟuvataṟku maṟṟoru eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ēṉ nām pativu ceytuḷḷōm eṉpati uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ mikap piṟkālattavi ākum. nam ilakkiyaṅkaḷil koḷkik kōṭpāṭukaḷic ceyaluruppaṭuttukiṟatu pōla ilakkiyap paṇpiṉ marapiṉ matippu ēṟṟuk koḷḷāmal iruppatu mika varuttamāka irukkiṟatu. ilakkiyap paṇpuṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ teṉ intiyāviṉ tēcīya māḻiyākap poṉ payaṉpaṭuvatu eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉac cuṭṭik kāṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu. piṟkālattil cempum veḷḷiyum piṟa māḻikaḷum potu makkaḷiṭiyē tārāḷamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirunta niliyiṉ pōtum māḻit toḻil perum aḷavil vaḷarcciyuṟṟu poṉṉiṉ piṟkūṟu oru māḻi maṟṟoṉṟiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṇpa taṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tuppuravāṉa poṉ puṭattaṅkam allatu cokkat taṅkam eṉṟum, cempu kalanta poṉ nakit taṅkam allatu cavaraṉ taṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. poṉ eṉpatu oru kālattil potuvāka māḻi eṉṉum poruḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum puṉitamāṉa poṉ ellāt tātukkaḷukkum mēlāka matippilum, oḷiyilum, vaṇṇat tilum, taṉmiyilum uyarntatāy ippoḻutu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
poṉ ceyyum vitti
tamiḻakattil, poṉ ceyyum ōr ariya toḻilip palar aṟintiruntaṉar. cempiliruntum turucukaḷ akaṟṟap paṭṭāl atu poṉṉākum eṉṟu cilar kūṟivantaṉar. pātaracattip payaṉpaṭuttic cilar poṉ ceytu vantaṉar. cempu allatu pātaracattip poṉṉāka māṟṟum vitti iracavātat toḻil eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟatu. kaṉma īruyirakiyuṭaṉ cila camayam poṉ kalanta kaṉimap poruḷ cārnta paṭikkak kallil (Quartz) iruntu terinteṭutta tuṇṭukaḷi naṉṟākak kaḻuvip periya kuḻi ammiyil iṭṭu arittup poṭiyākki, antap poṭiyi mīṇṭum kaḻuvi, oru caṭṭiyil pōṭṭu vaṟuttu atiluḷḷa kantakac cattu akaṟṟap paṭṭatu. pātaracattiṉiṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟupoṭikaḷ poṉṉāka māṟum. oruṅkiṇinta pātaracamum poṉṉum cūṭākkappaṭṭa oru irumpuc caṭṭiyil pōṭappaṭukiṟatu. muṉ kūṟiyatu āviyāka māṟip pōkiṟatu. piṉṉuḷḷatu ataṉ puṉitaniliyil eñci niṟkiṟatu.
aṟiñar kirik (Grig) utakamaṇṭalattip paṟṟip pēcum poḻutu kuṟippiṭukiṟār; piṣap ṭavuṇ (Bishop Down), perm  hil (Ferm hill) eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkup piṉṉuḷḷa paḷḷattākkil poṉ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum ataṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum intap pakutiyilum nañcaṉkūṭu paḷḷattākkilum ataṉ aṇmiyilum poṉ tukaḷkaḷ maṇṇōṭu kalantiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭut taṇṇīril alacip poṉ tukaḷkaḷ vēṟākavum maṇ vēṟākavum pirittu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉ taṭaṅkaḷ āṟṟaṅkarikaḷiṉ neṭukilum uḷḷa maṇmēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḷḷattākkukaḷiṉ arukil uḷḷa nīr ōṭikaḷil kaṉma īruyirakiyuṭaṉ cilacamayam poṉṉum kalantuḷḷa kaṉimapporuḷ (Quartz) kuviyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi poṉ ceyyum paṇi paṇṭik kālattil kuṉṟukaḷil maṭṭum naṭipeṟavilli. vēy nāṭṭilum mēṟkattiya kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷilum naṭipeṟṟu vantatu eṉpatic cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
poṉṉum aṇikalaṉkaḷum
mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattiṉiṉṟu teṉ intiya makkaḷ ciṟappākat tamiḻarkaḷ ittātupporuḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu pūṇṭukoṇṭatu periya aḷavil itaṉōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpu eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya putikuḻikaḷil kūṭa, pala taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷōṭu poṉṉum miṉṉik koṇṭiruntatu terikiṟatu. niṉiviṟ keṭṭāta kālattiṉiṉṟu palavakiyāṉa uruvil poṟkollarkaḷ poṉṉi urukkit taṭṭi, atik kottiyum, aṟuttum eṇṇaṟṟa eḻil vaṭivil aṇikalaṉkaḷit tam kivaṇṇamum uḷappāṅkum oḷira uruvākki vantaṉar. ippoḻutum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ kaṇṭa tātupporuḷkaḷil poṉ mutaṉmutalākak kaṇṭa poruḷāy miḷirkiṉṟatu eṉpatu uṟutippaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻmakkaḷ kaṇṭa kaṭal vaṇikattiṉ payaṉāyk kāla vaḷarcciyil poṉ ulakameṅkum parantu nilaviyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu poṉ kiṭippataṟkariyatāy nāḷukkunāḷ vili ēṟikkoṇ ṭirukkum nilimikkut tamiḻnāṭṭumakaḷir, poṉmītu koṇṭa viruppam kuṉṟātu vaḷarntu koṇṭiruppatum oru kāraṇamāka irukkalām.
poṉ aṇiyum iṉattavarkaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭup poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceytuvantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷip pārttu intō āriyarkaḷ poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷip pūṇat talippaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ kātiṉ kīḻppakutiyil toḷiyiṭṭup pala poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar. pōrṉiyōvil uḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷippōl kātuvaṭittu atil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa vaḷiyaṅkaḷi aṇintu varuvati aṟintēṉ. āṉāl ēṟattāḻa 4,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē yūtap peṇkaḷ kātil toḷi yiṭṭup poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷip pūṇṭu vantatuṭaṉ kaḻuttilum kikaḷilum kālkaḷilum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. yūtarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇinta tōṭu, malitteyva vaḻipāṭṭiyum mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. tirukkōyilil civaṉ kāḷimītu amarntirukkum muṟiyil karuvaṟiyil tiruuruvam amittu vaḻipaṭṭum vantaṉar. teyvat tiṟku maṉivi makkaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu nampiṉar. teyvattiṟkup pāl paḻam appam paṭittu naṟumpuki kāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kōyil, tamiḻar kōyil amippip pōlak koṭimaram, palipīṭam, karuvaṟi mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭiruntatu.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
IV
1.	They are locally known as Pattams and the system of putting gold in some shape or other over the dead body is prevalent in orthodox Hindu Homes even nowadays. Dr. Sir. John Marshall makes the following note on these objects (Annual Report of the Archeological Survey of India 1902-03 foot note on page 105). The gold frontlets are of thin gold left so flimsy that they could not possibly have been used as jewellery in real life. They are mere imitations of the genuine articles substituted by the relation of the dead who no doubt kept the more substantial jewellery for themselves. For similar instances of this economical piety towards the dead. See Frazer Pausanias Vol.III. p. 107. Schliemanu Mycenal p. 156, etc., : E. Gardner new chaplers in Greek History E.P. Tyler Primitive Culture Vol. I. p. 439.
2.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras, 1915, (Introdution) pp. III-IV.
3.	Mohenjo Daro and Indus valley Civilization - Dr. Sir. John Marshall Vol. I (London) 1930.
	Excavation at Harappa - M.S. Watts Vol.I. p. 14.
4.	Dravidian Elements in Indian Civilizaiton - Dr. Gilbert Slater. M.A., D.Sc., 1920 (Madras).
5.	Pre-Historic South India by Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951.
	History and Monuments of Ur. of - C.J. Gad (London) 1929.
6.	tamiḻar mutaṉmutal payaṉpaṭuttiya tātu poṉṉāyiruntati ṉālēyē poṉ eṉṟa colliṟku ulōkam eṉṉum poru ḷum, piṟkālattil kiṭitta veḷḷi, cempu, irumpiṟku muṟiyē veṇpoṉ, cempoṉ, karumpoṉ eṉṟa peyar kaḷum tōṉṟiṉa - paḻantamiḻāṭci - ñā. tēvanēyaṉ. v«.V., (ceṉṉi) mutaṟpatippu. 1952. pak. 93.
7.	“Tamils traded with the Chaldeans and teak found in the ruins of Ur, the sea port and capital of Babylonia “grows”, says Ragozin in Vedic India p. 806, in Southern India close to the Malabar coast and no where-else”. They took Indigo and muslin to Egypt. King Solomon had Sandal wood apes and peacocks (thoki) from Ophir, “The navy of Tarshish brought gold, silver, ivory, apes and peacocks from India. Nineveh had gold from Tamil Country and Assyria tin. The Greeks took rice and pepper. Pearls were exported from Korki” - Tamil India - M.S.Purnalingam Pilli, B.A., L.I. (Madras) 1927. p.76.
	(a)	“If there was intercourse between the Indusvalley and Sumerla there must have been greater intercourse between these two districts and South India. Evidence of this is found in two facts mentioned by sayce. One is that Indian teak was found in the ruins of Ur (Mugheir) which was the capital of the sumerian kings in the iv millennium B. C. and the other is that the word Sindhu or muslin is mentioned in an ancient Babylonlan list of clothing.” -Sayce Hibbert Lectures pp. 136-138.
	(b)	“Not only was there Commercial intercourse between the Tamil Country and the Mesopotamian valley, but there is some evidence that the trade of South India extended to Egypt in the iii millennium B.C. Says W.H. Schoff,” thousands of years before the emergence of the Greeks from savagery ... Egypt and nations of Ancient India came into being and a Commercial System was developed for the interchange of products within those limits having its centre of exchange near the head of the Persian gulf. The people of that region the various Arab tribes and more especially those ancestors of the Phoenicians, the mysterious Red Men were the active carriers or intermediaries. The growth of civilization in India created an active merchant marine trading to the Euphrates and Africa and eastwards we know not whither.” Periplus. p.3.
8.	Pre-Historic South India - Prof. V.R.Ramachandra Diksitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 114.
	The Sumerians - C. Leonard Willy (Oxford) 4th impression May 1930.
9.	We need not enter into the discussion whether gold was a material of the sanskirt speaking people known as the Aryans or whether it was bequeathed by the old inhabitants of South India. Examining the various points of view both in favour and aginst, we are almost driven to the conclusion that gold was first discovered in south India though India as a whole had once been regarded as a land of gold. It is true even in the South there are only a few districts which can exclusively be sid to be gold - bearing districts. It became a precious metal simply be-cuse gold was scarce and avilable only in a few parts of the land.”
	Pre-Historic south India - Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 114.
10.	Ibid. pp. 115-117.

5. irumpup poruḷkaḷ
(IRON OBJECTS)
putiya kal ūḻiyil ulōkap poruḷkaḷ
ātittanallūr paṟampiluḷḷa iṭukāṭṭil aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷāl akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka tātup poruḷkaḷ mūṉṟu. avi, muṟiyē (1) poṉ (2) irumpu (3) veṇkalam eṉappaṭum. im mūṉṟil poṉ, tamiḻakattil mutaṉ mutalākak kiṭitta tātup poruḷāṉatāl, iṅku mutalil vittu āyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. poṉ nilattil periya aḷavil kiṭikkappaṭṭatālum, makkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭali aḻaku ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyvataṟkēṟṟa taṉmiyum meṉmiyum vaṇṇamum vāynta poruḷāka iruntatālum atu ulakil mutalāvatāka vittu eṇṇappaṭum poruḷāy uyarntatu. makkaḷ, poruḷ parimāṟṟuk karuviyāka, poṉṉik kaṭṭi nāṇaya mākavum, kācu nāṇayamākavum payaṉ paṭuttiyatāl poṉṉiṟku matippu eḻuntatu. āṉāl irumpip pōl, makkaḷ vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷākap poṉṉō, piṟa tātup poruḷ kaḷō iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkilli. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpik kaṇṭa makkaḷē maṉita nākarikattiṟku vittiṭṭa makkaḷāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpi naṉku payaṉpaṭuttat terinta makkaḷē ulakil ariya periya karuvi kaḷip paṭittu ulaki āḷum āṟṟal peṟṟavarkaḷāyum, ciṟanta poṟikaḷiyum nīrāvik kappalkaḷiyum uruvākki vaṇikat tuṟi yil vaḷarntum, poruḷātārat tuṟiyil uyarntum vāḻn tuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉip piṉṉar virivāy ārāyvōm.
inniliyil 100 āṇṭiṟku muṉ ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil irumpup poruḷkaḷ ērāḷamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟiya aṇu ūḻiyil kūṭa irumpiṉ ēṟṟam eḷ aḷavil kūṭak kuṉṟavilli. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl iṉṟu irumpiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta nili paṉmaṭaṅku uyarntuḷḷatu. irumpē iṉṟiya intiyāviṉ perumikkum pēṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāy amintuḷḷatu.
akaḻāyvil kiṭitta irumpup poruḷkaḷ
ātittanallūrp paṟampil aṟiñar alekcāntar irīyā avarkaḷ āṇṭutōṟum aṭuttaṭuttuc ceyta akaḻāyvil pala irumpuk karuvikaḷum cila irumput taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivaratu akaḻāyvil kiṭittirāta oru cila irumpup poruḷkaḷ perliṉ muṉivar cākōr avarkaḷiṭa mum pāricu aṟiñar em. uluyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷiṭamum kiṭittirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iruvaruṭiya akaḻāyvukaḷilum ettaṉi poruḷkaḷ, eṉṉeṉṉa poruḷkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu yārē kūṟa vallār. nkY« mt®fŸ f©blL¤j bghUŸfŸ x›bth‹¿‰F«, ïÇah f©blL¤j bghUŸfËš Ãfbuh¤j gofŸ.(Duplicates) uḷa eṉṟu evvāṟu tuṇintu kūṟa muṭiyum?
ātittanallūril akaḻāyvu naṭattiya mūvarkaḷiṉ cēkarippi lum, tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya ellā vakiyāṉa paṭikkalaṉ kaḷum (Armours) toḻilāḷarkaḷukku iṉṟi yamiyāta karuvikaḷum, (Tools), vīṭṭuppayaṉpāṭṭukku uriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum (Implements) kiṭittuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. mēlum tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyil uḷḷa īmak kāṭṭil ellā vakiyāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷum, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷum, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷum irukkum eṉṟu evarum etirpārkka muṭiyātu. avaṟṟip paṇṭikkāla aracaṉiṉ araṇmaṉiyilum, paṭikkalak kiṭaṅku kaḷilum kūṭa irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkilli.
aṟiñar irīyā akaḻntu kaṇṭa irumpup poruḷkaḷ mottam 391 eṉṟu avar namakkut tantuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ātittanallūr, perumpāyūr ākiyavaṟṟi ṉiṉṟu kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūlil kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, eṉṉuṭiya matippil tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paḻaṅkāla irumpup paṭik kalaṉkaḷum, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷum, vīṭṭup payaṉpāṭṭiṟkuriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷumāka ēṟattāḻa 1000 vakikaḷ irukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu. āṉāl, irīyā kaṇṭeṭutta irumpup poruḷkaḷ 66 eṉṟu terikiṟatu. 1ituvē nām viyakkattakka ceytiyākum. ulakil perumpālāṉa makkaḷ āṭi kaṭṭat teriyātu vilaṅkukaḷ pōl vāḻnta putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ ittuṇi irumpup poruḷkaḷi uruvākkip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟa ceyti, tamiḻulakam iṉṟum eṉṟum perumi aṭivataṟkuriya ceytiyākum.
irumpuk karuvikaḷ vakikaḷ
ātitta nallūr paṟampiṉ akaḻāyvil kiṭitta poruḷ kaḷil cilavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ :
1.	eṟivēl (Spear).
2.	īṭṭi (Lance).
3.	kuttuvāḷ allatu paṭṭākkatti (Dagger).
4.	ampumuṉi pōṉṟa vēl (Barbed javeline).
5.	iru puṟamum kūrmiyāṉa vāḷ (Double edged Sword).
6.	mūvili vēl (tiricūlam) (Trident).
7.	kūriya amputtali (Barbed arrow head).
8.	kikkōṭari (Hatchets).
9.	vāḷ (Sword).
10.	vaḷinta katti (Curved knife).
11.	iru vaḷivāṉa kokkittaṭi (Hooks with two curved rods).
12.	pali vāḷ (Sacrificed sword).
13.	amputtali (Arrow head).
14.	vēlāyutam (Javeline).
15.	kōṭarikaḷ (Axes).
16.	cūlāyutam (Sulams).
17.	kēṭayam (Shield).
18.	ariya āyutam (A Curious Weapon).
19.	ciṟiya uṭivāḷ (Small Dagger).
20.	ciṟiya īṭṭi (Small Lance).
21.	ceṅkōṇavāyuḷḷa īṭṭi (Lance with blade rectangular in section).
22.	kūmpuvāy īṭṭi (Lance with narrow blade).
23.	kūriya vāyuḷḷa īṭṭi (Lance with lapering blade and hollow butt).
24.	īṭṭip piṭiyuḷḷa kuḻivāṉa kuḻāy (Hallow tube handle of lance).
25.	kuḻivāṉa viḷimpuḷḷa kuttuvāḷ (Dagger with tapering point).
26.	akaṉṟa vāyp paracu (Hatch broadest at the cutting edge).
27.	nīṇṭa vaṭṭak kuḻivāṉa īṭṭi (lance with long round hollow shaft).
28.	kuḻivāṉa irumpuk kippiṭi īṭṭi (Hollow iron handles with rods through the centre).
29.	vaḷivukaḷuḷḷa vāḷ (Swords with various curved blades tapering with points).
30.	ciṟiya nuṉiyuḷḷa vāyuṭiya īṭṭi (Lance with lapering blades and hollow handles).
31.	katti (Knife).
32.	vaḷinta palavakik kattikaḷ (Reaping hook or curved knives of various forms.
aṟiñar alekcāntar iriyā (Alexander Rea) ātitta nallūrp paṟampil uḷḷa īmakkāṭṭi akaḻntu, palvēṟu paḻam poruḷkaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttatu oru periya viyappiṟkuriya ceyti aṉṟu. āṉāl, apporuḷkaḷi vakuttut tokuttup peyar kaṇṭupiṭittatu viyappiṉum viyappu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl aḵtu oru poḻutum mikiyāṉa ceyti eṉṟu evarum kūṟa muṭiyātu. atilum āṅkila nāṭṭic cārnta oruvar, teṉ intiyāviṟku vantu orucila āṇṭukaḷ taṅki, mikappaḻamiyāṉa kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ irumpiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷ kaḷukkum irumpup paṭikkalaṉkaḷukkum peyarkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar eṉpatu eḷitāṉa kāriyam aṉṟu. iṅkē piṟantu vaḷarntu tamiḻ naṉku payiṉṟa tamiḻppulavarkaḷāṟkūṭa avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷi eḷitil kūṟamuṭiyātu.
aṟiñar irīyā peru muyaṟci ceytu paḻaṅkāla irumpup poruḷkaḷukkup peyarkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. āṉāl, avar ellā irumpup poruḷkaḷukkum uriya peyarkaḷik kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷār eṉṟu colliviṭamuṭiyātu. eṉṟālum peritum muyaṉṟu palavaṟṟiṟkup peyar kāṇa muyaṟci ceytirukkiṉṟār. cilavaṟṟiṟkup peyar kāṇamuṭiyavilli. cila irumpu āyutaṅ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷ kaṇṭatum illi; kēḷvippaṭṭatum illi eṉṟālum iriyā avarkaḷ anta irumpup poruḷkaḷit tirumpat tirumpat turuvi āyntu apporuḷkaḷi nām uṇaru māṟu āṅkilattil naṉku viḷakkam tantuḷḷār. eṉṟālum iṉṟum nammāl antap poruḷkaḷukku uriya, cariyāṉa peyarkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyavilli. mēlum muṉivar cākōrum, pirāṉcu nāṭṭaṟiñar ulūyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷum kaṇ ṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷil nām peyar kūṟa muṭiyāta poruḷkaḷ evvaḷavu irukkum eṉṟu nammāl cintikkakkūṭa muṭiyavilli.
iṉavēṟupāṭu
ulakilēyuḷḷa palvēṟu vaṇṇamum, palvēṟu mukattōṟṟa mum palvēṟu paṇpāṭum palvēṟu moḻi amippum peṟṟup palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum tīvukaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷ oru tāy vayiṟṟil piṟantu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟip palvēṟu vaṇṇamum palvēṟu moḻi amippum palvēṟu paṇpāṭum peṟṟārkaḷā? kiṟittava camayak kaṟpaṉippaṭi orutāy vayiṟṟil kāyīṉ, āpol, cēt eṉṉum mūṉṟu āṇmakkaḷ tōṉṟi ulakil maṉiviyi eṅkiruntō peṟṟu makkaḷip peṟṟup palkip perukiṉārkaḷā, allatu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum orē kālattilō allatu muṉpiṉṉākavō cila āṇ peṇkaḷ tōṉṟik kalantu āṇ peṇ makkaḷip peṟṟu nāḷaṭivil ulakameṅkum paraviṉārkaḷā eṉṟu nammāl kūṟa muṭiyavilli. maṉita iṉattiṉ orumip pāṭṭiṟku ulakamakkaḷ aṉivarum orutāy vayiṟṟil piṟanta piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟu aṟavōrkaḷum aṟivōrkaḷum kūṟivaruvatiyum nām aṟivōm. āṉāl amerikkāvil vāḻum annāṭṭuk kuṭimakkaḷāṉa cevvintiyar kaḷum, aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiya āṅkilēya veḷḷiyarkaḷum, āppirikka nāṭṭil tōṉṟiya nīkrōvarkaḷum, aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi an nāṭṭiṉari veṉṟu koṉṟu aṭimippaṭuttic curaṇṭi vāḻum airōppiya pirañcu, piriṭṭicu nāṭukaḷiṉ veḷḷiyar kaḷum, mañcaḷ niṟa maṅkōliyarkaḷāṉa cīṉarkaḷum cappāṉiyar kaḷum, veḷḷi niṟa uruciyarkaḷum, intiyāvil tōṉṟi aṅkuvāḻum tirāviṭarkaḷum iṭikkālattil annāṭṭil pukuntu avarkaḷi veṉṟu koṉṟu aṭimippaṭuttiya āriyar kaḷum, cermaṉ nāṭṭu āriyarkaḷum, cupeyṉ (Spin) nāṭṭu mūr kaḷum cōrṭāṉ nāṭṭu arapikaḷum icarēyal nāṭṭu yūtarkaḷum orē tāy tantiyarkaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟanta makkaḷā eṉṟa aiyap pāṭukaḷukku aṟiviyal vallunarkaḷukkuḷ iṉṟu tiṭṭa vaṭṭa māṉa muṭivu kāṇa muṭiyavilli.
āṉāl, ulakil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum, palvēṟu tīvukaḷilum makkaḷiṉam taṭpa veppanili kaḷukkēṟpap palvēṟu vaṇṇamum uṭal amippum tōṟṟamum peṟṟuk kaṟkāla makkaḷāyum ulōka kāla makkaḷāyum varalāṟṟuk kāla makkaḷāyum vāḻntuvantuḷḷaṉar. cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē makkaḷ nāṭuviṭṭu nāṭu pukuntum, kaṇṭam viṭṭuk kaṇṭam pukuntum āṅkāṅkuḷḷa makkaḷōṭu kalantum irukkalām eṉṟam karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ulēkilē palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum vāḻnta makkaḷ orē kālattilē orē vakiyāṉa nākarikattip peṟṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyavilli. ulakilē palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum muṉ piṉṉākak kallūḻikaḷum ulōka ūḻikaḷum varalāṟṟuk kālamum arumpi yuḷḷaṉa. kal ūḻiyil, tol paḻaṅkal ūḻiyum, paḻaṅkallūḻiyum, putuk kallūḻiyum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avipōla maṉita nākarikat tiṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa ulōka ūḻiyilē cempum maṇṇum kalanta, kalappuc cempu ūḻi (Chalcolithic age) veṇkala ūḻi, irumpu ūḻi eṉṟu palavaki ulōka ūḻikaḷ malarntuḷḷaṉa. inta ulōka ūḻiyum kal ūḻiyum ovvoru nāṭṭilum orē kālattil tōṉṟavilli; caṟṟu muṉ piṉṉākavum tōṉṟa villi. orē kālattil oru nāṭṭil paḻam kal ūḻiyum, maṟṟoru nāṭṭil putukkallūḻiyum, iṉṉoru nāṭṭil ulōka ūḻiyum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. orē nāṭṭil vaṭa pakutiyil cempu ūḻiyum naṭup pakutiyil putuk kal ūḻiyum teṉ pakutiyil irumpu ūḻiyum eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.
ulakilē ulōka ūḻiyilē makkaḷ nākarikattiṟku aṭikōlat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. cempu ūḻiyiviṭa, veṇkala ūḻiyiviṭa irumpu ūḻiyiltāṉ makkaḷ naṉi ciṟanta nākarikatti aṭintuḷḷaṉar. cempu ūḻiyil periya nāṭukaḷiyum, nakaraṅkaḷiyum akaṉṟa terukkaḷiyum, māṭikaḷiyuṭiya vīṭukaḷiyum, neṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷiyum āṭi kaḷiyum, aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum, marakkalaṉkaḷiyum kappal kaṭṭum taḷaṅkaḷiyum, vilpaṭi, vēl paṭi, kālāḷ paṭi, tērppaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟip peṟṟup pēraraciyum niṟuviya makkaḷ irumpu āyutaṅkaḷip peṟṟa āṭumāṭu mēykkum nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟatu varalāṟu kaṇṭa uṇmiyākum. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpu ūḻiyi aṭinta makkaḷ naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟa makkaḷāyum vallami vāynta makkaḷāyum vāḻntuḷḷaṉar.
irumpiṉ tōṟṟamum ēṟṟamum
pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē teṉṉintiyāvil irumpu ūḻi malarntuḷḷatu - tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ muṉṉōṭi kaḷāṉa tamiḻarkaḷ irumpi naṉku payaṉpaṭutta muṉ vantaṉar. irumpu ōr uyarnta tātut taṉimam. ataṉ kuṟiyīṭu pe (Fe) ataṉ aṇu niṟi 55.84; itu tamiḻar pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap payaṉ paṭutti varum taṉimaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. itit tamiḻarkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē naṉku payaṉpaṭuttat terintuḷḷaṉar. ki.mu. āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē irumpu makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. mikat toṉmi yāṉa kālattilēyē irumpip piritteṭuttu atip pala poruḷkaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttuvatil tamiḻarkaḷ kitērnta varkaḷāy viṭṭaṉar.
kiṭikkum iṭam
irumpu taṉi niliyil mika aritākak kiṭittatu. iḵtu iyaṟkiyaṉṉi makkaḷukku aḷitta ariya naṉkoṭiyākum. itu viṇṇiṉiṉṟu viḻum kaṟkaḷilum erimalip pāṟikaḷilum ciṟuciṟu tuṇukkukaḷākak kiṭikkiṟatu. irumpuk kūṭṭukkaḷ pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṇilum atikamāka nīrnilikaḷi luḷḷa nīrilum tāvaraṅkaḷilum utirattilum irumpuk kūṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
perumpālum ellāk kaṉimaṅkaḷilum irumpu ciṟitu uḷḷatu. mukkiyamāka irumput tātukkaḷ āksiṭukaḷum kārpaṉēṭṭukaḷumākum. mākṉaṭiṭ (Fe3 O4) ke (ha) maṭiṭṭu (Fe2 O3) iliyōṉiṭṭu (2 Fe2 O3 3H2, O), ciṭariṭṭu, cālipiṭ pōṉṟavi mukkiyamāṉa irumput tātukkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
irumpu āksiṭu tātukkaḷik kariyuṭaṉ kalantu urukki ulōkam piritteṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu piritteṭukkappaṭum irumpil kari ciṟitaḷavu kalantirukkum. kariyiṉ vikitamum atu kalantuḷḷa vakiyum irumpiṉ paṇpukaḷi niruṇayikkiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṟkēṟpa (1) vārppu irumpu (Cast iron) (2) eḵku (Steel) (3) tēṉirumpu (Wrought Iron) eṉa mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa irumpukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
irumpu vakikaḷ
irumpu iṉṟi nākarikam vaḷarntirukka muṭiyātu. puki vaṇṭi, pēruntu vaṇṭikaḷ, kappalkaḷ, pālaṅkaḷ, pattu irupatu māṭik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi eḵku iṉṟi amikka muṭiyātu. cuttamāṉa irumpu kiṭittaṟkariya poruḷākum. irumpu eppoḻutum ciṟitaḷavu karip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ kalantēyirukkum. intak karitāṉ irumpiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi māṟṟi viṭukiṟatu. kariyē irumpiṉ valimikkuk kāraṇamāka irukkiṟatu. kariyiṉ kalappu aḷavē vārppirumpu, tēṉirumpu, eḵku eṉṟu māṟṟi viṭukiṟatu.
vārppirumpil 4 catavikitam kari kalantuḷḷatu. itu kaṭiṉa māṉatāka irukkātu. eḷitil uṭikkak kūṭiyatu. cūṭēṟṟic cammaṭṭiyāl aṭittu nīṭṭa muṭiyātu. urukki accukkaḷil vārkkalām. itaṉ payaṉ ciṟitēyākum.
tēṉirumpil kari mikak kuṟivāka uḷḷatu. paṇṭiya tamiḻakattil tēṉirumpē atikamāka uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. itu palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷil kāyccit taṭṭa ēṟṟatu. ciṟitu kūṭak kari illāta cuttamāṉa irumpu uṟutiyāṉatāka irukka muṭiyātu. iḵtu eḷitil turuppiṭikkātu. tēṉ irumput tuṇṭukaḷ cūṭēṟṟiṉāl oṉṟoṭoṉṟu iṇiyum; eṉavē itu nalla payaṉuḷḷatāka irukkiṟatu.
eḵku irumpum kariyum oru kalavip poruḷ. itil kariyiṉ aḷavu 1-7% vari irukkum. iṉṉum māṅkaṉicu kantakam, pācuvaram, cilikkaṉ ākiyaviyum itil uḷḷaṉa. itic civanta cūṭṭil kāyccit taṭṭalām. cātāraṇa cūṭṭil ōraḷavu uruvākkalām. naṉṟākak kaṭiṉamāvatu itaṉ mukkiyamāṉa paṇpu. karipporuḷē itaṉ kaṭiṉat taṉmikku mukkiya kāraṇamākum. valimi itaṉ maṟṟac ciṟappiyalkaḷiṉ oṉṟākum.2
irumpiṉ varalāṟu
irumpu tamiḻnāṭṭil cumār pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa ōr ariya tātup poruḷākum itu. poṉṉiviṭa atikappayaṉuḷḷatāyum arumiyāṉatāyum matikkappeṟṟiruntatu. mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻarkaḷ, ekiptu, āppirikkā, cumēriyā, kirīcu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceytu vantaṉar. ekiptiyarkaḷum, kirēkkarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṭa miruntē irumpi urukkip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷi aṟintaṉar. 1837ām āṇṭil irāyal ēciyāṭṭik cociṭṭi (Royal Asiatic Society) yil vācikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭuri oṉṟil aṟiñar kīt (Heath) eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāvil ceyyappaṭṭa eḵkup poruḷkaḷē ekiptiṟkum airōppāk kaṇṭattiṟkum ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻnāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil ulikaḷil veppam kuṟivāka iruntatāl irumpu toṭakka niliyil ciṟiya aḷavil urukkap paṭṭatu. appāl tōliṉālāṉa turuttiyik kaṇṭupiṭittu, periya aḷavil irumpi urukki palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭutti vara muṟpaṭṭaṉar. paṇṭik kālattil kallālum maṇṇālum kaṭṭappeṟṟa ciṟiya ulikaḷil irumpuk kaṉimaṅ kaḷiṉ poṭiyi marakkariyuṭaṉ cērttu urukkic cumār 70,80 iṟāttal eṭiyuḷḷa irumpuk kaṭṭikaḷi uṟpatti ceytu vantaṉar. mūṅkil kuḻāyiṉālum, tōliṉālum marap palakiyiṉā lum ceyyappaṭṭa turuttikaḷāl ūtappaṭṭa kāṟṟiṉāl uli mukattil kariyi erittu 10, 12 maṇi nērattiṟkup piṟku uliyi uṭittu irumpuk kaṭṭiyi eṭuppar. akkaṭṭi urukiṉa karic cāmpalōṭum piṟa acuttap poruḷkaḷōṭum kalantiruntatu. appāl atik kari aṭuppil kāycci cammaṭṭiyāl aṭittu irumpup pāḷaṅkaḷākac ceytārkaḷ.
tilliyiṉ (Delhi) aṇmiyil uḷḷa kuttupmiṉārukku arukil uḷḷa irumput tūṇ intiyāvilē ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. atu ki.pi. 300il ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ittuṇi āṇṭukaḷāka anta irumput tūṇ kāṟṟilum maḻiyilum veyililum aṭipaṭṭum turuppiṭikkātu iruntu varuvatu kaṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ viyappaṭikiṉṟārkaḷ. intūrukku 30 kal tolivil uḷḷa tār eṉṉum irumput tūṇ kuptarkaḷ kālattil uḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. irumpu kaḷic ciṟu ciṟu tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭi, karittūḷip pakkaṅkaḷil nirappic ciṟukaḷimaṇ pāttiraṅkaḷil vittu ataṉ vāyik kaḷi maṇ cāntiṉāl pūci, aṭittuk kariyaṭuppukaḷil veṇcūṭākum variyil vittuk kāyccic cila maṇi nēram ceṉṟatum pāttiraṅ kaḷi eṭuttu āṟa vittu uṟutiyāṉatum, kaṭiṉamāṉatumāṉa eḵkit tayārikkum muṟiyit tamiḻarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē aṟintiruntaṉar. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷum, vīṭṭu upayōkattiṟku uriya arivāḷmaṇi, tēṅkāyt turuvi, veṭṭarivāḷ, tēṅkāyk kīṟi, karaṇṭi, caṭṭi, akappi pōṉṟaviyum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
intiyāvil ki.pi. 400ām āṇṭil mutaṉmutalāka uṭcu (wootz) eṉṟa peyaril eḵku ceyyappaṭṭatu. ērāḷamāka airōppā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭatu. uṭcu eṉṉum eḵku mūlam ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟa ṭamāciṉ eṉṉum kattikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. eḵkik kāyccit taṇṇīril avittu eḵkik kaṭiṉa mākkum muṟiyip piṟa nāṭṭiṉar intiyariṭamiruntē aṟintaṉar. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil irumpiyum eḵkiyum tayārikkac cila muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭup piṉ kinaḻuva viṭap paṭṭaṉa. piriṭṭicu intiyāvil vaṭintiyap pakutikaḷil iru irumput toḻiṟcālikaḷ niṟuvap peṟṟaṉa. cutantira intiyāvil pilāy, rūrkēlā, turkkāpūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ayalnāṭṭār ātaravuṭaṉ periya irumput toḻiṟcālikaḷ niṟuvappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. cēlattilum oru ciṟu irumput toḻiṟcāli arumpi yuḷḷatu. cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ peṅkaḷūr vicākappaṭṭiṉam parakīp mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil putu urukkālikaḷ niṟuvat tiṭṭam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Iron Ores)
irumpu, taṉiyāka nilattil veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭum poru ḷaṉṟu. irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavi kemaṭiṭ (Hematite) (Fe2O), mākṉaṭiṭ (Magnettite Fe3 O4), nīruṭiya kārpaṉēṭṭāṉa ciṭariṭṭu (Fe CO3 x H2O) ākiyaviyākum.
ikkaṉimaṅkaḷ, amerikkā, kaṉaṭā, cōviyat uruciyā, intiyā ākiya nāṭukaḷil pēraḷavilum airōppāvilum āttirēyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟiya aḷavilum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ perum aḷavil uḷḷaṉa. pīkār, oricāp pakutikaḷil 8,000 miliyaṉ ṭaṉkaḷukkuk kuṟivillāmal kuṟintatu 60 catavikitam irumpiyuṭiya kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kāṣmīrilum tamiḻnāṭu, āntirā, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya mānilaṅkaḷilum irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. intiyā vil 9647 ṭaṉ uyartarak kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu aikkiya nāṭukaḷiṉ niṟuvaṉam matippiṭṭuḷḷatu. iṉṉum maṭṭavakik kaṉimaṅkaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ nilattiṉ mēṟparappil malikkuṉṟukaḷāka uḷḷaṉa. malikaḷil ciṟu toḷikaḷiṭṭuk kuṭintu avaṟṟiṉuḷ veṭimaruntic celutti veṭikkac ceytu atil kiṭikkum kaṉimaṅkaḷic cēkarittup pukivaṇṭikaḷil ēṟṟi irumput toḻiṟcālikaḷukku aṉuppu kiṟārkaḷ. nilattiṉ aṭiyil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ kāṇum iṭaṅkaḷil curaṅka vēlikaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭum kaṉimattil kalantirukkum kalliyum maṇaliyum akaṟṟa ati nīril alaciyō valivāṉa kāntaṅkaḷip payaṉ paṭuttiyō tuppuravu ceykiṟārkaḷ. kaṉimattik kariyuṭaṉ cuṭṭup pañcu pōṉṟa poruḷākavō ciṟiya uruṇṭikaḷākavō ceytu avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu irumpip piritteṭuppatil cila vacatikaḷ uṇṭu.
kaṉimattik kariyuṭaṉ kalantu cūṭēṟṟiṉāl irumpu pirikiṟatu. kaṉimattuṭaṉ cuṇṇāmpik kalappatāl atil uḷḷa cilikā alumiṉā mutaliya acuttaṅkaḷ ataṉuṭaṉ kūṭi mūlak kacaṭākap pirikiṉṟaṉa.
aṟiviyal vaḷarcci peṟāta toṉmiyāṉa kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuc ciṟiya kaḷimaṇ ulikaḷil tōl turuttikaḷiṉ mūlam irumpi urukki vantaṉar. marakkariyip payaṉpaṭutti irumpi urukkiṉar. iṉṟuḷḷa māperum iyantiraṅkaḷum, nilakkariyum kāṟṟūtum iyantiraṅ kaḷum miṉ ulikaḷum illāta kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ mukkiyamāṉa irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷāṉa mākaṉaṭip kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Ores) kemaṭiṭ mutaliyavikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu vārppirumpu, tēṉirumpu, urukku mutaliyavikaḷi uruvākkum muṟikaḷit tam paṭṭaṟi vāl pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṟintu āyiram āyiramākap paṭikkaruvikaḷiyum, vīṭṭukkuriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷi yum, payirt toḻil, taccuttoḻil, veṇkalap pāttiram ceyyum toḻil, kollar toḻil mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta uḷi, cuttiyal, vāḷ, kuṟaṭu, iṭukki, aram mutaliya poruḷkaḷi ellām ceytu kuvittuḷḷatik kaṇṭu ulakam perum viyappaṭintuḷḷatu.
teṉṉintiyā putiya kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyil aṭiyeṭuttu vittu viṭṭatu oru viyattaku ceytiyākum. atu putuk kallūḻiyiṉiṉṟu veṇkala ūḻiyi aṭiyavilli. atu putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyi aṭintu viṭṭatu eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ aṉantappūr, kaṭappi, karnūl māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum aṅkumiṅkum citaṟuṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṉṟit teṉṉintiyāviṉ ēṉiya pala pakuti kaḷilumkūṭap putiya kallūḻiyiṉ kalappuḷḷa cāṉṟaṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷum irumpup poruḷkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eṅku irumpu ulakil mutaṉmutalākak kiṭittuḷḷatu eṉṟu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu kāṇappaṭavilli. vaṭintiyāvil mutaṉ mutalil irumpiṉ mūlap poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭu urukkappeṟṟup piṉ teṉ intiyāviṟku vantatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. atil aṇuvaḷavum uṇmiyilli. teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta putiya kallūḻi makkaḷ taṟceyalāka irumpik kaṇṭu atu paṭikakaṟ pāṟiyiviṭa nīṭittu uḻikkak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar. teṉ ṉintiyāvil irumpi urukki atip palvēṟu karuvikaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttum pāṅku airōppāviviṭa mikat toṉmi yāṉa kālattiliruntu nilavi vantuḷḷatu. atu piṉṉar ayalnāṭu kaḷilum - ciṟappākak kirīṭ, kirīcuvari paraviyuḷḷatu - teṉ ṉintiyāvippōl aṅkum pala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷākavum, niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷākavum eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.3
irumpu kiṭitta iṭam
ātittanallūril irumpup poruḷkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. āṉāl, aṅku irumpu kiṭikkavilli. teṉ intiyāvil irumpup poruḷkaḷ kiṭittatāl aṅku irumput tātukkaḷ kiṭittirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvatil tavaṟirukkamuṭiyātu. teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi arumpuvataṟku, ataṉ puviyiyal amippē mukkiya kāraṇamākum. aṅkuḷḷa pāṟikaḷ karuṅkaṟ pāṟikaḷāka amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē appāṟikaḷil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Iron ores), māṅkaṉīcu (Manganese), poṉ vaṇṭal (Gold Deposits) īyam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅki iruk kiṉṟaṉa. cēlam māvaṭṭattilum, nelli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum nīlakiri, paḻaṉi, āṉimali, ēlakkāy kuṉṟu (Cardaman Hills) mutaliyavaṟṟilum nellūriṉiṉṟu makānati (Mahanadhi) vari kiḻakku malit toṭarkaḷilum irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ iruppatu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅku kār ṉōkkiṭc (Charnockites) eṉṟu iṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭuvarum karuṅkal (Granite) peritum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippāṟikaḷil uḷḷa kāṭipporuḷ (Acid) kaḷiliruntu kaṭum kuṉippāṉa irumpuc ciṭṭaṅkaḷ (Altra beik) variyum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu maṇṇūlkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
āntira nāṭṭil mayilkuṉṟil uḷḷa kaṭikānūrp pakutiyil varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉar vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kōṭari pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ ippoḻutu kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iḵtu antap pakutikku mikapperumi aḷippatākum eṉṟu varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. atōṭu, aṅku nīrāvit tuḷikaḷuḷḷa erimalik kuḻamputtuṇṭukaḷ niṟinta maṇmēṭukaḷ iruppatum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu pellāriyiṉ kuṉṟumukam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu. itu putiya kaṟkālam nilaviya iṭamāka eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum irumpu urukkiya ulōkak kacaṭukaḷiṉ uḷḷārnta taṉmi yiliruntu iḵtu irumpu ūḻi nilaviya iṭam eṉṟu eḷitil muṭiviṟku varamuṭiyum. mēlum iṅkuḷḷa kaṟutta allatu civanta irumpu uyirakik kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ ati naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, oruvēḷi putiya kal ūḻiyiṉiṉṟu piṉ toṭarntu vanta irumpu ūḻiyil maṉitaṉuṭiya mēmpāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcci paṭip paṭiyāy vaḷarntu varukiṟatu. putiya kallūḻi irumpu ūḻikku mika amitiyāṉa muṟiyil kaṭantu ceṉṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu teḷivāyk kuṟippiṭalām. pellāriyaṉ kuṉṟumukam, itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu aḷikkiṟatu. cappāṉiya irumpu āyvuttuṟi vallunar, teṉṉintiyāvil irumpuk kaṉikaḷ iruntaṉa; oru kālattil aṅkiruntu irumpu ayalnāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumatiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu namakku ākkam aḷippatākum.4
teṉ intiyāvil, cempu ūḻiyō veṇkala ūḻiyō tōṉṟiyatilli; putiya kallūḻiyit toṭarntu ataṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻi arumpiviṭṭatu. muntuṟa eḻunta irumpu ūḻiyiṉ eñciya kūṟukaḷuṭaṉ putiya kallūḻiyiṉ eñciya poruḷkaḷi arukarukē vittu āyntāl, aṉantappūr, kaṭappi, karnūl, nīlakiri māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil periya aḷavil irumpukkaṉimaṅkaḷ aṅkum iṅkum citaṟik kiṭappatu teriya varum. tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattila uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷi lum (Shevroy Hills) teṉ intiyāviṉ ēṉiya pakutikaḷilum irumpuk karuvikaḷum putiya kaṟkālak kalaviyiṉ taṭaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. irumpu eṅku mutalil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu urukkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu iṉṉum muṭivākat tīrmāṉikkappaṭa villi. irumpu vaṭa intiyāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu aṅkiruntu teṉ intiyāviṟku aṟimukam ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatil uṇmi yilli. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa putiya kallūḻiyic cērnta makkaḷ taṟceyalāka irumpik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar. atu paṭikap pāṟiyiviṭa valivuḷḷatāy nīṇṭa kālamāyiṉum keṭṭup pōkātatāyk kāṇappaṭṭatu. airōppāvi viṭa mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil teṉ intiyāvil irumput toḻil arumpi ēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟukūṭak kūṟalām. atu teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟik kirīṭ, kirīcu  mutaliya piṟa nāṭukaḷuk kellām paravat toṭaṅkiyatu. aṅkut teṉ intiyāvippōl pala vaḻikaḷilum irumpup poruḷkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu an nāṭṭiluḷḷa araṇmaṉikaḷum araṇkaḷum niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷum, kōyilkaḷum aḻakum uṟutiyum nalla amippum uṭiyaṉa vāy viḷaṅkiṉa eṉṟu tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcatar avarkaḷ tam cīriya nūlāṉa varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya teṉ intiyā eṉṟa nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.5
ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil 32 vakiyāṉa irumpup paṭik kalaṉkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum muyaṉṟu akaḻntu pārttāl 50kkuk kuṟiyāta eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa paṭikkala vakikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭikkalām - tamiḻarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻi arumpuvataṟkumuṉ 30kku mēṟpaṭṭa vakiyāṉa irumpup paṭik kalaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpatē tamiḻ ulakam perumip paṭuvataṟkuriya ceytiyākum. inta viyattaku ceytiyi aṟintu nāṉē perumitam aṭikiṉṟēṉ; eṉ uḷḷam eṉṟumillāp pūrippeytiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻaṉ ulaka araṅkil, taṉ mārpil kiyi vittukkoṇṭu pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ irumpu ūḻiyik kaṇṭu ulakilē oppaṟṟa eḵkup paṇpāṭṭi vaḷartta, naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam vāynta paḻamperum makkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavaṉ nāṉ eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟamuṭiyum.
irumpiṉ payaṉkaṇṭa makkaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uyarnta paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarttu nūṟāyirakkaṇakkāṉa pōrkkaruvikaḷi yum toḻiṟ karuvikaḷiyum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar. eṉṟālum ayalnāṭṭu makkaḷiyō, tam aṇṭi nāṭṭu uṟaviṉarkaḷiyōveṉṟu koṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ uḻippik kavarntu, uṇṭu koḻukka eṇṇiyatum illi; ceyaṟpaṭavum illi.
tamiḻar
taṉṉuyir nīppiṉum ceyyaṟka tāṉpiṟitu
iṉṉuyir nīkkum viṉi
eṉṉum aṟaneṟiyi maṟimoḻiyāka matittu vāḻntavarkaḷ.
niṟka, tamiḻarkaḷ āṭci, kālakkoṭumiyāl vīḻntatu. 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil innāṭṭik kavara vanta, pōrccukīciyarkaḷō pirañcu, āṅkilēya veḷḷikkārak koḷḷiyarkaḷō, piṟarō (āriyarkaḷō mokalāyarkaḷō) tamiḻar kaṇṭa irumpuk karuvi kaḷic cēkarittup paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccāliyil vikka villi tamiḻarkaḷ paṭikkalaṉkaḷippaṟṟi evvita nūlum eḻuti vikkavilli.
nāṉ eṉ iḷamip paruva mutal tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṭikkalaṉ kaḷic cēkarikkavum avarkaḷiṉ pōr muṟikaḷ pōrkkaruvikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷip paṭikkavum muyaṉṟuḷḷēṉ. nāṉ 1960ām āṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattilē varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōrmuṟikaḷum pōrkkaruvikaḷum eṉṟa nūli eḻutattoṭaṅkip pallāṇṭu kaḻittu muṭittuḷḷēṉ. annūl iṉṉum accēṟavilli.
1962 ām āṇṭu āṇṭaki ekarṭṭaṉ (Lord Egerton) 1896il eḻutiya intiyāvilum kīḻi nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa paṭikkalaṉ kaḷiṉ viḷakkam eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlam tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷa āṅkila aracō, tolporuḷ ārāyccittuṟiyō, paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyō, taṉippaṭṭa tolporuḷ cēkarippāḷar kaḷō, paḻaṅkālap paṭikkalaṉmītu akkari celuttavilli eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu. intiyap paṭikkalaṉkaḷiṉ cēkarippum puṟakkaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatu teḷivākak kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉi, tañci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paḻaṅ kālap paṭikkalaṉkaḷ, pāmaramakkaḷālum, pōrk karuvikaḷip paṟṟiya ārāycci vallunarkaḷālum aṟiviyal nipuṇarkaḷālum puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatāl avi paḻiya ulōkap poruḷ kaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭu uṭikkappaṭṭum uli. mukattiliṭṭu urukkappaṭṭum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. iṉṟu muṟkāla makkaḷ evvitamāṉa paṭikkalaṉkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpataṟku nammiṭam evvitac cāṉṟum illi. itippaṟṟi 1896 ām āṇṭil aṇṇal ekarṭṭaṉ namakku aṟivuṟuttiṉār. āṉāl 1978ām āṇṭu vari tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷō, tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷō immi aḷavum akkari kāṭṭavilli.6
namatu nāṭṭil palvēṟu vakiyāṉa vāḷkaḷum, vēlkaḷum, kikkōṭarikaḷum, kattikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpati namatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa cilikaḷ, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ, acentā ōviyam, aṇṇal vāyil ōviyam, maṟṟum pala kuki ōviyaṅkaḷ mūlamum tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa puti kuḻikaḷil kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭa irumpup poruḷkaḷ mūlamāyum naṉkaṟiyalām eṉṟu paṇṭikkāla intiyāvum pōrkkaliyum eṉṟa nūlil muṉivar pi.ci. cakkaravartti eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
(a) paṇṭik kālattut tirāviṭa intiyāvil payaṉ paṭuttiya irumpālāṉa īṭṭi, eṟivēl, kuttuvāḷ, uṭivāḷ irupuṟamum kūrmiyuḷḷa vaḷinta katti (kirīcu) mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟi āriyarkaḷukkut teriyātu. māpāratap pōril īṭṭiyum vēlum mukkiya pōrk karuvikaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉa. avi palvēṟu peyarkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. mūvili vēl teṉṉin tiyarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya taṉipperum paṭikkaruvi. atu civa perumāṉ karattil ēntappeṟṟa cīriya karuviyākum. intak karuvi, eṅkaḷuṭiyatu eṉṟu āriyarō, cīriyarō, mokalāyarkaḷō urimi koṇṭāṭa muṭiyātu eṉṟu tiru.e.pi. cantira cēkaraṉ pampāy pavāṉi tiṅkaḷ itaḻil virivāka paṇṭik kāla intiyap pōrk karuvikaḷ eṉṟa kaṭṭuriyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pōrk karuvikaḷ
nāṉ paṇṭiya tamiḻ makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pōrk karuvi kaḷip paṟṟi eḻutiya varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōr muṟikaḷum pōrk karuvikaḷum eṉṟa nūlil virivākap pala pōrk karuvikaḷippaṟṟi viḷakkan tantuḷḷēṉ. namatu varuṅkāla iḷam talimuṟiyāḷarkaḷ paṇṭik kālap paṭik kalaṉkaḷip pārkka muṭiyāmal pōṉālum peyarkaḷiyāvatu aṟintiruk kaṭṭum eṉṟu avaṟṟi vakippaṭutti oru paṭṭiyal aḷit tuḷḷēṉ. eṉatu paṭṭiyalil tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya ellā irumpup paṭikkaruvikaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu eṉṉāl uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu. ettaṉiyō pōrkkaruvikaḷ viṭupaṭ ṭirukkalām eṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalām. cintu veḷiyip pōl putiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ oṉṟikkūṭa akaḻntu āyum vāypput tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku ituvari kiṭṭavilli. attakiya vāyppu eḻuntālaṉṟip paṇṭit tamiḻar payaṉpaṭuttiya oru niṟivāṉa paṭikkalaṉkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyali nām pārkka muṭiyātu.
iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pōrk karuvi kaḷi maṉattiṟkoṇṭu nām ulakil oppaṟṟa nākarikattip peṟṟu - vāḻipaṭi vāḻiyākat teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntu vanta vīrarkaḷiṉ marapu vaḻiyil vanta makkaḷ eṉṟu perumitattōṭu nimirntu niṉṟu ulakil evariṭamum pēcamuṭiyum. ulaka varalāṟṟil en nāṭṭilum, tamiḻakattippōla pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ittuṇi vaki vakiyāṉa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa irumpuk karuvikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu evarum meyppikka muṭiyumā? muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, tamiḻakattil mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyum irumpup paṇpāṭum nilaviyatu oṉṟu. maṟṟoṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷōṭu puti kalaṉkaḷil avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭikkalaṉkaḷiyum piṟa karuvikaḷiyum vittup putikkum marapu vaḻakkam ākum. ātitta nallūr īmak kāṭṭi akaḻntu āyum vāyppu eḻāmal iruntāl nām intap pēṟṟi eyti irukka muṭiyātu.
nām nam tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nalaṉik karuti ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum piṟa ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kaṇṭa peyarkaḷiyum vakuttut tokuttu itaṉ aṭiyil tantuḷḷōm. ataṉik kaṇṭu teḷika.

niṟka, mēlē eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟavi tavira, namatu ciṟpa nūlkaḷ, ilakkiya nūlkaḷ, ōviya nūlkaḷ, purāṇa nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu 500kku mēṟpaṭṭa paṭikkalaṉkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷic cēkarittu akara varicippaṭutti eṉṉāl eḻuti veḷiyiṭa irukkum varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōr muṟikaḷum, pōrk karuvi kaḷum eṉṟa nūlil oru paṭṭiyal iṇittuḷḷēṉ. virivañci avi aṉittiyum iṅkuk kuṟippiṭātu viṭuttuḷḷēṉ.
vil, ulakiluḷḷa palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa malivāḻ makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. aḵtu iṉṟu pala nākarikam peṟṟa nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭiya tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuṉṟak kuṟavarkaḷiṭamum pōr maṟavarkaḷiṭamum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa vilkaḷ iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ cila villēnti iruntaṉa eṉṟu paḻaṅkatikaḷ collukiṉṟaṉa. villip payaṉpaṭuttuvatil paṇṭiya tamiḻarkaḷ taṉittiṟaṉ vāyntavarkaḷāy iruntu vantaṉar. pōrk kaḷattil mikka cakti vāynta paṭikkalaṉkaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻarkaḷ villip payaṉ paṭutti vantaṉar paḻaṅkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ ampukaḷiṉ nuṉiyil eḵkuk kavacam pūṭṭi ati naṉku payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. avi maṉita uṭalkaḷi maṭṭum aṉṟi vilakkukaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷiyum, maraṅkaḷiyum tuḷittuc cellattakkaṉavāy iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āntira nāṭṭil acantā ōviyaṅkaḷilum maturi mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar kōyiṟ ciṟpaṅkaḷilum vil oppaṟṟa paṭik karuviyāy viḷaṅkiyatu eṉṟu tiru cantiracēkarar pavāṇi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
(a) ātittanallūril kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paṭikkalaṉ kaḷil (Arms) pakivarkaḷmītu pāyccuvataṟku ēṟṟa eṭi kuṟintavi ēṟivēl (Javelin) īṭṭi (Spear) pōṉṟaviyākum. ivi tirāviṭamakkaḷ pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttum mukkiya māṉa karuvikaḷākum. ittakiya paṭikkalaṉkaḷic cila vētakāla āriyarkaḷum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ intiyā viṟkuḷ aṭiyeṭuttu vippataṟku muṉ ittakiya karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutaṟku eḷḷaḷavum cāṉṟu illi. pāratap pōril āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭa aracaṉāṉa kiruṭṭiṇaṉ tuṇiyip peṟṟaṉar. tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṇṭa ciṟanta paṭikkalaṉ kaḷāṉa vāḷ, vēl, īṭṭi, kuttuvāḷ pōṉṟa pōrkkaruvikaḷit tāṅkip pōr naṭattiṉar. pala, tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ pōrkkaruvikaḷ cakti, kuṇṭā, kuṇapā, cāliyam pōṉṟa vaṭa moḻippeyarkaḷ tāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūvilivēl, tiricūlam eṉṟa peyaril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. inta mūvilivēl muttalivēl eṉṉum oruvaki īṭṭi. itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ taṉipperum kaṭavu ḷāṉa civaṉār karattil uḷḷatu eṉṟu paḻaṅkatikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
civaperumāṉ tirukkarattil uḷḷa pōrk karuviyāka avar vīṟṟirukkum kayilimaliyil uḷḷa mūṉṟu cikaraṅkaḷāṉa nantākuṉṟi, nantakōṭu, nantātēvi eṉpavi (mūvili vēlākak) kaṟpaṉi ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. civaperumāṉ aintu talikaḷi yum pattuk karaṅkaḷiyumuṭiya mātēvaṉ (makēcuvaraṉ) āka ēṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷār. avaruṭiya pattuk kikaḷilum pācam, paracu, muttali vēl, cūlam, īṭṭi mutaliya pala karuvikaḷ iruppatākak kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ maṟiyāṉa ākamaṅkaḷilum maṟi nūlāṉa tirukkuṟaḷ, tirumantiram, tēvāram, tiruvācakam pōṉṟavaṟṟilum pala pōrkkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tarappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. itaṉi muṉṉāḷ pampāy mānila araciṉ amiccarum ciṟanta civapaktarumāṉa kālañceṉṟa tiru. kē.em. muṉci avarkaḷi āciriyarākak koṇṭu veḷivanta pavāṉi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ ēṭṭil aṟiñar cantiracēkaraṉ avarkaḷ mīṇṭum tiṟampaṭa naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
intiyāviṉ tātupporuṭ celvam
irumpuk kaṉic celvam ciṟappumikka mukkiyattuvam vāyntatuvām. intiyāviṉ irumpukkaṉic celvam 300 kōṭi ṭaṉkaḷ irukkum eṉpatu kuṟinta aḷavuk kaṇippu. piriṭṭaṉil 225 kōṭi 40 laṭcam ṭaṉkaḷ, cermaṉiyil 137 kōṭi 40 laṭcam ṭaṉkaḷ. amerikkāvilum pirāṉcilum māttiram intiyāvi viṭa atikamāṉa irumpukkaṉikaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu matippiṭap paṭṭaṉa. intiya pū vētiyal paricīlaṉit tuṟi cīcil cōṉ mūlataṉam (Capital) eṉṟa itaḻil 1929ām āṇṭu eḻutiya kaṭṭuri.
intiyā urimi peṟumuṉ 1933ām āṇṭil irāyal empayar cociṭṭi (Royal empire society)Æ‹ ātaravil car alpiraṭvāṭcaṉ eṉpavar aṭiyil varum uṇmikaḷi ulakiṟku uṇarttiyuḷḷār.
toḻil tuṟiyil intiyā pala vāyppukaḷi iḻantu viṭṭatu. itaṟku (aṉṟu) āṭcippīṭattik kavarntu atil amarntirunta āṅkila āṭciyē poṟuppākum ... oru periya iyantirattoḻil nāṭāka oḷirvataṟkēṟṟa cūḻnilikaḷ aṉittum mikutiyāka iruntum poruḷātārat tuṟiyilum, toḻil tuṟiyilum intiyā iṉṟu ulakiṉ piṟpōkkāṉa nāṭu kaḷil oṉṟāy irukkiṟatu ... intiyāviṉ toḻil vaḷarcciyiṉ piṟpōkkiṉi nām oru poḻutum akkaṟiyuṭaṉ tīrkka muṉ varavilli.8
intiyā, taṉ nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ tēvikaḷ perukuvatiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aṭuttuvarum āṇṭukaḷil ituvari kaṇṭirāta vēkattil iyantirat toḻil vaḷarcci aṭiyāviṭṭāl iṉṟē añcattakka aḷavukkut tāḻntirukkum. nāṭṭiṉ vāḻkkittaram paṭṭiṉikiṭakkum niliyit tāṇṭiviṭum -(eṉṟu ilaṇṭaṉ ṭimcu pattiriki 4-1-1933il eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṉum intiya maṇṇiṉ aṭiyil putintu kiṭakkum kaṉi vaḷaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttakkāṭṭiyatōṭu intiya uṇavu uṟpattiyilum, iyantira necavilum nām ceyya vēṇṭiya tuṇi uṟpattiyum, puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷiyum intiyāvit tanti nāṭākavum, iṅkilāntit tāy nāṭākavum eṇṇip paṇiyāṟṟiya aṉittulakap potu vuṭimi iyakka māperun talivar tōḻar iraciṉi pāmipattatar iṉṟiya intiyā eṉṟa nūlil (4-11-1947il) tamiḻil moḻi yākkam peṟṟa nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. avar irumpip paṟṟi maṭṭumaṉṟi, intiyāviṉ kaṉipporuḷ, viḷiporuḷ, cey poruḷ aṉittiyum paṟṟiya puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi intiya makkaḷiṉ naṉmatippip peṟṟār. piriṭṭicu aracu āṟṟiya koṭumikaḷi ovvoṉṟāyt tam nūlil viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.5
cappāṉ nāṭṭil vāḻnta tātupporuḷ ārāycci vallunar kaḷil (Metallurgist) pērum perumiyum peṟṟavar pērāciriyar kōvalāntu eṉpavarāvar. ivar cappāṉ tīvukaḷil palvēṟu pakutikaḷil irumpu kiṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷi aṟiyac cuṟṟuppayaṇam ceytu pala irumpuc curaṅkaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuc cappāṉ nāṭṭiṉ irumpu vaḷattiṟku ākkam aḷitta aṟiñar āvar. avar, irumpik kaṇṭu piṭittatum ati urukkum muṟikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittatum etirpārāta orunikaḻcciyāka iruntirukkalām. āṉāl, airōppāviviṭa mika mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil intiyat tīvakattil toḻil nuṭpam uṇarnta pēraṟiñar uḷḷat til irumput toḻiltuṟi etirpārāta naṟpēṟāka arumpi yirukka vēṇṭum. irumpip payaṉpaṭuttiyapiṉ teṉ intiyāvil tārāḷamāka eṅkum kiṭikkum nili eḻuntapiṉ ayalnāṭu kaḷukkum ciṟappāka kirīṭ, kirīcu nāṭukaḷukkum parava ārampittiruk kalām.
kirīṭ, kirīcu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāy eḻunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum irumpu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu teṉ intiyāvippōl palvēṟu vakikaḷil ciṟappuṟṟu ārampittuḷḷatu. kirēkka nāṭum kirīṭ tīvum ēṉiya mēṟkattiya nāṭukaḷum taṅkaḷuṭiya putiya kal ūḻiyik kaṭantu irumpup paṇpāṭṭiṉik kūṭa aṭiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. kal ūḻi maṉitaṉ mutaṉmutalāka malikaḷil vāḻntuvantāṉ. avaṉ uṇaviṉṟi aṅkumiṅkum alintu tirinta nilimiyum aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉ puṟa elliyik kaṭakka eṇṇum ārvamum avaṉ vāḻkkiyil oru putumiyik kāṇacceytatu. irumpu ulōkak fU(Iron ore)it¡ kaṇṭupiṭitta piṉṉartāṉ, paḻaṅkāla, kuṉṟil vāḻnta mutirāp paṇpuṭiya maṉitaṉ maliyiṉiṉṟu iḻintuvarum āṟṟiṉ karikaḷ vaḻiyāka mali aṭivārattiṟku iṟaṅkivantu aṭarnta kāṭukaḷi aṭiya muṟpaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kāṭukaḷiṉ vāḻum muṟiyip payiṉṟāṉ. ākiyāl irumpup paṇpāṭu tāṉ kuṉṟukaḷil vāḻnta kuṟiñci nila makkaḷi mali aṭivārat til amintuḷḷa kāṭārnta mulli nila vāḻkkikku mēva vaḻi kāṭṭiyatu. itutāṉ mutirāp paṇpuṭiya mutumakkaḷāṉa kallūḻi makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil oru putiya niliyākum. ituvē paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa mutaṟpaṭi yākum.8
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭum - ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭum
cintuveḷiyil mokañcatārōvi mutaṉmutalāka 1922 ām āṇṭu vaṅkap pērāciriyar ār.ṭi. pāṉarcci. muṉṉiṉṟu akaḻāyvu naṭattiṉār. akaḻntu āyntu oru periya nakar 70 aṭi āḻattiṉ kīḻ iruppatik kaṇṭu piṭittu ataṟku iṟantārkaḷiṉ nakaram eṉṉum poruḷ paṭa mokañcatārō eṉṉum peyari aḷittār. avartām mutaṉmutalil cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu, cempum maṇṇum kalanta cālkōlitik (Chalcolithic age) kālam eṉṉum ūḻiyil eḻunta tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉṟu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār.
ivar ēṟkeṉavē ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvip paṟṟi nuṇuki uṇarntiruntār. eṉavē tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu teṉ intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyiṉiṉṟu vaṭintiyā muḻuvatilum paravip paluccicuttāṉ vaḻiyākap pāracīkam īrāṉ pakriṉtīvu mutaliyavikaḷilellām paravi kirīṭtīvu vari paraviyuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu ātittanallūril arumpi arappā vari aṟuntu pōkāta caṅkilit toṭarpōl tikaḻkiṟatu eṉṟu car jāṉ mārcal avarkaḷukkut tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭiṉ toṉmiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. 1927ām āṇṭu kalkattāvi ṉiṉṟu vanta māṭarṉ reviyū eṉṟa tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭil tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉṉum talippil mūṉṟu toṭar kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmiyāṉa nākarikac ciṟap piṟku ākkan tēṭiṉār. ātittanallūril arumpiyatu irumpu ūḻiyil kaṇṭa irumpup paṇpāṭu eṉṟu ulakatira moḻintār.9
ataṉ piṉṉarc ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka varalāṟṟu āciriyarum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟip pērāciriyarumāṉa tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcatar ātittanallūril arumpiyatu ulakil oppaṟṟa irumpup paṇpāṭu eṉṟum, atu ki.mu. eṭṭā yiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟum, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ uyaravum vāṇikam vaḷaravum vāḻvu malaravum āḻ kaṭal kaṭantu kaṭal vaṇikam perukavum mūlakāraṇamāka iruntatu avarkaḷ kaṇṭa irumpup paṇpāṭē eṉṟu 1951ām āṇṭil avar āṟṟiya ulliyam meyyar coṟpoḻivil mika virivākat tiṟampaṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. ātittanallūr akaḻvāyvil āḻnta pulamivāynta tiru. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar tam coṟ poḻiviṉ mūlam ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭiṟku ākkam aḷitta nallārāka matikkappaṭṭuḷḷār.10

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
V
1.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) (Madras) 1915.
2.	kalik kaḷañciyam tokuti iraṇṭu, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam, ceṉṉi. mutaṟpatippu irumpu (ceṉṉi) 1955. pakkam: 82-84.
3.	“According to a mighty authority the civilization of the Indus valley was one that had become already age old and stereotyped on the Indian soil. How old it is we cannot definitely say that this stretch of time but we can certinly say that it was in the later stage of the iron culture of South India; for in our opinion the iron culture of the South India may be dated from eight to Ten Thousand B.C. Therefore it is not strange that the Indian Culture had many millions of human endeavour behind it” -Pre-historic South India by V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 243.
	(a)	The period of the Indus Valley Civilization is regarded as to be from 3250-2750 B.C. by Dr.R.K. Mukerji on the basis of the remins traced, in the excavation of the only one of the seven layers. Below these layers also there are other layers. These excavations would give the Indus Valley Civilization a much earlier date than 3270 B.C. Anyway since the existing remins of the Indus Valley gave to the period of 3250 and since its information is avilable in the literature of the Aryan the Rigveda of 3250 to 2500 is regarded as Proto-Historic period in theAnalysis of the history of Ancient India. Due to this Consideration the Indus Valley Civilization is placed in the Proto-Historic period of the Ancient Indian History” - Ancient Indian History Civilization and Culture by P.S.Joseph M.A. & others.
4.	Pre-historic South India-by Prof. V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 75.
5.	In South India there was no Bronze age or Copper age as such but it was the Iron age that succeeded the Neolithic age. If we study the Neolithic remins side by side with the relics of the early Iron age, a large quantity seems to be scattered as is found in the districts of Anantapur Cudappa and Kurnool also. The shevroy hills, the district of Tinnevelly and other parts of South India contin vestiges of the mixture of Neolithic and iron implements. It is not yet settled where iron was first found and smelted. There is no truth in the saying that it was introduced from North India to the South. Our belief is that the Neolithic people of South India should have come across iron accidentally and found it more durable and together than the trappoid rock. It may also be sid that the iron industry in South India is more ancient than that of Europe. It began to speread to other countries especially to Crete and Greece where architectural monuments and shrines resemble in several respects the South Indian one” - Pre-historic South India - Prof. V.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. pp. 95-96.
6.	“Neither Collection is rich in the South Indian arms and it is a matter of regret that the Government in India while professing to watch over the preservation of archeological remins has neglected to take advantage of the opportunity of acquiring old weapons from the armouries at Tanjore and Madras and allowed them to be broken up and the contents sold as old metal” - A Description of Indian and oriental Armour Rt. Hon. Lord Egerton of Tatton, M.A. (London 1896).
7.	It is noteworthy that all these three types of Sword, are represented in the Frescoes and Sculpture, at Ajanta while kirich and pattisa blades have been found in the Tinnevelly urn burials” - Art of war in ancient India - Dr.P.C. Chakravarti, M.A., Ph.D. 1941. p. 14.
8.	“The Knowledge and practices of this mighty weapon was not confined to North India. South India knew this weapon well from the beginning of its written History. The huge figure of warriors particularly that the Arjuna carved out in the Meenakshi Temple of Madura well might prove its existence in South India as an efficient instrument of fighting” - Apparatus of war in ancient India - A.P. Chandrasegaran (an article in Bhavans Journal) Bombay. 1959.
	(a)	Javelin and spear which came next in importance were seldom used by Vedic Aryans although these weapons were not known to them. It was during the Mahabarata war that Javelin and spear attined importance and they were known by different names Sakti, Kunta, Kunapa, Salyan Sanka etc. The trident which was a kind of spear was popular in South India and its was believed that it was the weapon of Lord Siva who of all the three gods Creation, preservation, destruction commands the highest veneration and popularity among the South Indians. Thirukkural, the Tamil Bible mentions the lance in two places. Apparatus of war in ancient India - A.P. Chandrasegaran (Bhavans Journal. 27.12.1959) Bombay p.60.
	(b)	It was Prof. Gowland the well-known metallurgist and explorer of the Japanese islands who expressed the opinion that the smelting of iron may have been hit upon by sheeraccident. The accident might have happened in peninsular India were according to the best minds the iron Industry is much more ancient than in Europe. After the use of iron became familiar in South India it began to spread in other countries specially Crete and Greece whose architectural monuments and shrines resemble in several respects those of South India. This is how Crete, Greece and other Western countries passed on also to iron culture from their Neolithic age. The stone age men were primarily living on hills and mountin fastnesses and also on the frings of rich forests ... Only after the discovery of iron ore it is resonable to suppose that primeval man took to the forest and made it his habits. Therefore it is only iron Culture that permitted the people of the hills to pass on to forest life” - Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. Madras. 1951. p. 97.
9.	Dravidian in South India such tombs vults and cemetrles belong exclusively to the age of Iron. Iron implements, weapons and other objects are to be found in large numbers in the tombs vults, coffins and urns. But this Iron age is not far distant from the end of the copper age, as along with Iron implements are to be found beautiful pots and other objects made of Bronze.
	Copper Age burials discovered in Northern India are very few in number but they prove the Iron age burials of South India to be a continuation and extension of the copper age methods of disposal of the Dead. It is perhaps well known that in India the remins of the copper age have not been discovered South of the Godavari but in that country copper and bronze were used for the manufacture of Art-ware, in discovered Iron age coffins. The Similarity of methods for dispose of the dead prove that the Iron age culture of Southern India is the lineal descendant of the Copper Age Culture of Northern India” - Dravidian Civilization by R.D. Banerji (Modern Review Calcutta) 1927, pp. 305, 553.
10.	These graves at Adichanallur are to be dated in the early Iron Age which succeeded the Neolithic Age in South India” - Origin and Spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1947. p. 21.
	“We can certinly say that it was in the later stage of the iron Culture of South India for in our opinion the iron Culture of South India may be dated from eight to 10,000 B.C. Therefore it is not strange that the Indian Culture had many millienia of human endeavour behind it” - Pre-historic South India by, Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951, p. 243.
