6. veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ
(BRONZE OBJECTS)
irumpu ūḻiyil veṇkalam
ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu ērāḷamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum oruvaki. veṇkalap poruḷkaḷil, pūmpaṭikkaṅ kaḷum caṭṭikaḷum vaḷikaḷum ciṟappāṉaviyākum. makkaḷ ātiyil nīr aruntum pāttiraṅkaḷākac curik kuṭukkikaḷiyum, tēṅkāyc ciraṭṭikaḷiyum, ōlippaṭṭikaḷiyum, ili kaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa toṉṉikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. pallāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟa piṉṉartāṉ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiyum piṉṉarc cuṭumaṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷiyum ceytaṉar. nīr aruntavum cōṟu camikkavum, kaṟivikkavum kiṇṇaṅkaḷiyum kalayaṅkaḷiyum caṭṭikaḷiyum pāṉikaḷiyum kuṭaṅkaḷiyum uruvākkiṉar. appāl avi eḷitil uṭintu pōvati aṟintu veṇkalat tālum, cempālum irumpālum kiṇṇam, caṭṭi, pāṉi, kuṭam, akappi, kuvaḷi, pūkkiṇṇam pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceyya muṟ paṭṭaṉar.
maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷip pōl allatu kaṟcaṭṭiyip pōl cempuk kuṭaṅkaḷum, pittaḷic caṭṭikaḷum veṇkalap pāṉi kaḷum eḷitil ceyyak kūṭiyavi alla. pāṭupaṭṭuc ceytāl nīṇṭa kālam uṭiyātu. aḻiyātu payaṉpaṭum. tamiḻakattil putuk kalūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil irumpu ūḻi eḻuntatiṉ payaṉāka irumpuc caṭṭikaḷum irumpu uḷi, cuttiyal, kuṟaṭu, cammaṭṭi, veṭṭirumpu, iṭukki pōṉṟaviyum ceyyum vāyppu eḻuntatu. eṉavē, tamiḻakattil kollar, kaṉṉār pōṉṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷ tōṉṟi irumpuk karuvikaḷāl veṇkalam ēṉaṅkaḷic ceyya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. kaṉṉārkaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivālum toḻil tiṟami yālum ērāḷamāṉa veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ ceytu kuvikkappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷil iṅkup paṭamiṭṭuk kāṭṭappeṟṟavi kuvaḷi, cāṭi, tōcikkal, kiṇṇam, kuṭam, keṇṭi, callaṭik kuṭikalam, ciṟu kuvaḷikaḷ pōṉṟaviyum piṟavumām. kuvaḷikaḷ perumpālum koṟuvāykaḷi yuṭiyaṉavāyum, uṭintaṉavāyum iruk kiṉṟaṉa.
muṟkālattil, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ viliyēṟap peṟṟaṉa vāyum kiṭittaṟkariya poruḷkaḷāyum iruntaṉa. eṉavē, vīṭṭil payaṉpaṭuttutaṟkuriya ariya poruḷkaḷiyellām iṟantavarkaḷōṭu putikkāmal iruntirukkalām. iṟantavarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya mukkiyamāṉa poruḷkaḷi maṭṭum avarkaḷōṭu putittirukkalām. atōṭu periya aṇṭā, kuttup pōṇi, tavali, vārppu, pōṇiccaṭṭi, kuṇṭāccaṭṭi pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷic cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyta tāḻikkuḷ vikka muṭiyātu. eṭi mikunta anta veṇkalac caṭṭikaḷiṉ cumiyit tāḻikaḷ tāṅka muṭiyātu. atu maṭṭumaṉṟip periya veṇkalac cāṭikaḷ, periya veṇkalak kuṭaṅkaḷ, cempup pāṉikaḷ, cempuc caṭṭikaḷ, pittaḷic caṭṭikaḷ, veṇkalap pāvi viḷakkukaḷ, veṇkalak kuttu viḷakkukaḷ, tūkku viḷakkukaḷ, nantā viḷakkukaḷ pōṉṟavi yum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭikkavilli eṉpatu niṉikkattakkatākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa paṭam 11l eṇ 1 mutal 15 variyuḷḷa pāttiraṅkaḷ ellām veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭaviyākam. 16 mutal 30 vari eṇṇuḷḷa veṇkalap poruḷ pūkkiṇṇam, paralkaḷ, pātac cilampu, kāṟ cataṅki, vaḷiyal, nāy, cēval, maṟimāṉ, āṭu puli pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiyuṭiya pūntaṭṭukaḷum, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷum, kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭiyaṉa. meyyākavē ceyviṉit tiṟam vāyntavi eṉa evarum oppuvar.
pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷ
pūk kiṇṇaṅkaḷ pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakat til nilaviya oppaṟṟa - uyariya ariya viyattakum nākarikattiṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. ulakilēyuḷḷa enta nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuciriyarkaḷum, tolporuḷ āyvu vallunarkaḷum ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭiṟku piṉṉaṉṟi aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar kūṭa ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta pāṅkāṉa pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷukku oppāṉa etiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟum itaṉ mūlam, tamiḻakattil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē nilaviya naṉiciṟanta nākarikattil eḻunta veṇkalap pāṉi caṭṭikaḷiyum pūk kiṇṇaṅkaḷiyum uruvākkum kaṉṉārkaḷip pōṉṟu toḻil nuṭpam vāynta toḻilāḷarkaḷi eṅkum kaṇṭirukka muṭiyātu eṉṟum uṟutiyuṭaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
veṇkala ūḻi
teṉ intiyāvil, irumpu ūḻi eḻuntataṉ piṉṉar ati aṭuttu, cempum veṇkalamum payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam vantuḷḷatu. oru cila tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷ. cempu ūḻiyum veṇkala ūḻiyum, irumpu ūḻikku muṉ vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟi yuḷḷaṉar. micūr aitarāpāttu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ aṭi nilattaṭak kāṭciḷic cāṉṟāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. atil cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷum maṟṟum taṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷkaḷum aṭaṅki iruntaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. intac cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi nām kavaṉamākap paṭittup paricōtaṉi ceyya vēṇṭum. irumpu eṉṉum tātup poru ḷukku muṉṉōṭiyākac cempum veṇkalamum iruntaṉa eṉṟa koḷkiyip pēcuvatu ēṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. cāṉṟukaḷ cila kāṭṭalām; avi puṟakkaṇikkak kūṭiyaviyē. āṉāl, teṉ intiyāvil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ toṭarntu irumpu ūḻiyik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟa koḷkiyi uṟutippaṭuttu vataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.1 teṉ intiyamakkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya aṟivi aṭintataṟku vaṭintiyāviṟkuc ciṟitākavō allatu peri tākavō kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām. iḵtu oru ciṟitum mikappaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭataṉṟu.
vaṭintiyāvil veṇkala ūḻi
vaṭintiyāviṉ paṇpāṭṭippaṟṟiya muṟiyāṉa āyvu naṭattiyavarkaḷ aṅku mutalil irumpiṉ upayōkam paṟṟi makkaḷ aṟiyātavarkaḷāy iruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa intiyāvil vāḻnta makkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttuvatil muṉṉēṟṟam eyti iruntaṉar. ataṉ piṉṉartāṉ avarkaḷ irumpip paṟṟiya aṟivip peṟṟu, atip payaṉpaṭutta muṉvantaṉar eṉpatu ulakaṟinta uṇmiyāka irukkiṟatu. teṉintiyāvil iruntatip pōṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil irumpup paṇpāṭu iruntatē illi eṉpatu tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāṉa uṇmi yākum. vaṭakkiṟkum teṟkiṟkum iṭiyē mika noymmi yāṉa toṭarpu iruntatu; ataṉ viḷivāka aṭut taṭuttu naṭipeṟṟu vanta kuṭiyēṟṟattiṉālum veḷiyēṟṟat tiṉālum, cempu, veṇkalam ākiya tātup poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu ēṉaṅkaḷum, vīṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum eppaṭic ceyvatu eṉṟa aṟivi virivākat teṉ intiyā peṟṟatu. ciṟitu kālañ ceṉṟa piṉṉarē, irumpu etaṟkup payaṉpaṭum? ati evvāṟu payaṉpaṭuttavatu? eṉpati vaṭa intiyā aṟiya muṉ vantatu.
kiṭṭattaṭṭa aimpattāṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭiṉ paricōtaṉiyāl inta nilimi naṉku viḷakkap paṭṭatu. cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa ciṟappāṉa pakutikaḷāṉa arappā vilum, mokañcatārōvilum lokuñcutārōvilumuḷḷa niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ aḻivupaṭāk kūṟukaḷum akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu, uḷḷārnta paṇpu, kālakkaṇippu, eḻuttup pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷi iṉṉum teḷḷat teḷiya āyavēṇṭum cintuveḷi nākarikatti uruvākkiya mutalmakkaḷip paṟṟip palvēṟu koḷkikaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. eyc. ār. hāl cintuveḷi akaḻ āyviṟkup pallāṇṭiṟku muṉ cintuveḷi makkaḷ cumēriyāviliruntu vantavarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu tamatu aṇmik kiḻakkiṉ paḻimiyāṉa varalāṟu eṉṟa nūlil oru karuttit terivittār.2 cintuveḷiyil ceyta akaḻvāyvuttuṟi iyakkunar taḷapati (Director general of Archelogy) car cāṉ mārcal avarkaḷ aṅku akaḻntu kaṇṭa nakaram, terukkaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ, kuḷaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, māṭivīṭukaḷ, naṭaṉamāṭum naṅkiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimam, aracaṉ allatu kuruveṉak kāṭci aḷikkum neṟṟippaṭṭam kaṭṭiya cuṇṇaccilikaḷ, naṭaṉamāṭum teyvac cili, tāyt teyvaccuṭumaṇcili muttiriyil kāṇappaṭum pacupati uruvam. civaliṅkaṅkaḷ, muttiriyil kāṇappaṭum teyva uruvamāṉa yāṉi, mīṉ, kāḷi, viyaṉciṉimā, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, pācipavaḻaṅkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, veṇkalappāttiraṅkaḷ, veṇkalattil ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, veṇ kalattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa paṭikkalaṅkaḷ, tantattiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, paṭakukaḷ pōṉṟa eṇṇilāp poruḷkaḷ ovvoṉṟiyum eṭuttu uṉṉippāka uṟṟunōkki viññāṉap pāṅkāka alaci, āyntu cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭip paṭitta varkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku vantār.3 avari aṭuttu akaḻvāyvu ceyta mekkē, car-mārṭṭimar uyilar, ār.ṭi. pāṉarji mutaliyavarkaḷum mārcal muṭipi nalla muṭipāka ēṟṟuk koṇ ṭaṉar.4 inta akaḻ āyvuttuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷil perumpāṉmiyiṉar cintuveḷinākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟu uṟutip paṭuttiṉar. cintuveḷi tirāviṭa nākarikam cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷil oppuyarvaṟṟatu; eṉṟālum iṅkuḷḷōr irumpu nākarikatti aṟiyātavarkaḷ; veṇkala nākarikattiṉāl uyarvaṭintārkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa tamiḻmakkaḷ, irumpunākarikattip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ; vaṭintiyat tirāviṭa makkaḷ veṇkala nākarikattip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatākum.
teṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ civa vaḻipāṭum, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭum, cakti vaḻipāṭum, kāḷivaḻipāṭum, āṟṟaṅ karip paṇpāṭum, civaneṟiyum, payirttoḻil payiṟciyum, kaṭalnīril kalamōṭṭum aṟivum, vāṉanūl aṟivum, eḻuttaṟivum, aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyum marapum piṟavum cintu veḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ peṟṟiruntālum irumpu āyutaṅkaḷiyō irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyō peṟṟirukka villiyeṉpatu akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa uyariya uṇmiyākum. itil evarukkum karuttu vēṟṟumi illi. mēlum irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻum uṟuti ūkkam veṇkalap paṇpāṭṭil peṟa muṭiyātu.
teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi
teṉintiyāvil putiya kaṟkālattiṉ toṭakkattil irumpu ūḻi arumpiviṭṭatu. teṉintiya makkaḷ akkālattil cempiyō veṇkalattiyō aṟiyārkaḷ. vaṭintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷiṭittiliruntē cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātupporuḷkaḷiṉ payaṉi aṟintu, avaṟṟip pāṉi caṭṭikaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttap piṟkālattil aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa uṇmi.
ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil atikamāṉa veṇkalap pāṉikaḷō, veṇkalaccaṭṭikaḷō, veṇkalak kuṭaṅkaḷō, cempuk kuṭaṅkaḷō kāṇappaṭātiruppatu varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa uṇmiyi uṟutippaṭuttuvatāka irukkiṟatu.
vaṭa intiyāviṭamiruntu teṉ intiyā cempu veṇkalam mutaliya tātupporuḷkaḷiyum avaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟi kaḷiyum aṟintiruntālum, teṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷāṉa tamiḻar iṉattavarkaḷ vaṭa intiyarkaḷiviṭa ulakil uḷḷa piṟa enta iṉattavarkaḷiyum viṭa cempu, veṇkalam, nākam, īyam pōṉṟa tātup porukkaḷi aṟintu avaṟṟit tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu varalāṟṟu uṇmiyākum.
teṉ intiyāvil veṇkalam, cempu ākiya kālaṅkaḷ eḻāviṭiṉum, vaṭa intiyāviviṭap piṟkālattil ērāḷamāṉa aḷavil cempum veṇkalamum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. itaṟku evvitamāṉa ilakkiyaccāṉṟukaḷum tēvi illi. tamiḻnāṭṭil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varum cempu, veṇkalam poruḷkaḷē cāṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil ovvoru vīṭṭilum mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku kuṭaṅkaḷ tavaṟātu uḷḷaṉa. vayatu mutirnta peṇpāl oruvar iruntāl avaḷukku oru kuṭamum, avaḷ makaḷukku oru kuṭamum avaḷ marumakaḷukku oru kuṭamum avaḷuṭiya ciṟu pēttikku oru ciṟu kuṭamum āka nāṉku kuṭamāvatu oru vīṭṭil irukkum. avaṟṟōṭu pittaḷik kuṭamum, cempukkuṭamum, veḷḷikkuṭamum cila vīṭukaḷil irukkum. mūṉṟu nāṉku marumakaḷ vantāl cītaṉamāka mēṟ koṇṭum varum. ivvāṟē ovvoru vīṭṭukkum veṇkala viḷakkum, pittaḷi viḷakkum, ki viḷakkum, kuttu viḷakkum, nantā viḷakkum, toṅku viḷakkumākap pala viḷakkukaḷ irukkum. iviyaṉṟit tamiḻakattil ovvōr illattilum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu varum cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu uḷḷaṉa :
1. kiṇṇam, 2. aritaṭṭuk kuvaḷi, 3. periya kuvaḷi, 
4. tāmpāḷam, 5. pūccaṭṭi, 6. kuttuppōṇi, 7. kuṭam, 8. koppari, 9. caṭṭi, 10. pāṉi, 11. tavali, 12. pōṇiccaṭṭi, 13. keṇṭi, 
14. koṭṭukkaṭāvu, 15. vaṭṭil, 16. kumpā, 17. akappi, 18. caruvaccaṭṭi, 19. paṭikkam, 20. neyccaṭṭi, 21. veṇṇeyccaṭṭi, 22. pākku veṭṭi, 23. cuṇṇāmpuk karaṇṭakam, 24. veṟṟili iṭikkum ural, 25. karaṇṭi, 26. curaṇṭi, 27. pittaḷic cimiḻ, 28. pūmpaṭikkam, 29. eccil paṭikkam, 30. ki kaḻuvum taṭṭup pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa vakiyāṉa cempu, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. cila vīṭukaḷil ovvoru poruḷilum 4, 5, 10 vari irukkum. iviyaṉṟip periya aḷavil veṇkalappāṉi, cempuppāṉi, pōṇic caṭṭi, aṇṭā, iṭṭaliccaṭṭi, vārppu eṉṉum peyaril palavita māṉa veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
cempu
ivi maṭṭumaṉṟip paḻaṅkālattil eḻutappeṟṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ceppēṭukaḷum nāṭṭil uḷa. ovvoru kōyililum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa viḷakkukaḷum camippataṟkuriya veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷum, kuṭaṅkaḷum, cempu aṇṭākkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. 3 aṭi mutal 9 aṭi vari uyaramāṉa cempup paṭimaṅkaḷum, aimpoṉ paṭimaṅ kaḷum eṇṇaṟṟavi tamiḻakattil maṭṭumaṉṟik kēraḷam, āntiram mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. ivi pukalāka uḷḷē tuḷi uḷḷaṉavāka iruppatilli; kaṭṭiyāka irukkum. koṭi maraṅkaḷum, tūṇ viḷakkukaḷum, kōpuraṅkaḷ mītum vimāṉaṅkaḷ mītumuḷḷa kalayaṅkaḷum cempālāṉavi. poṟṟakaṭu pōrttiyō, poṉ mulām pūciyō irukkum. cempu, pittaḷi, veḷḷic capparaṅkaḷum, tērkaḷum, pallakkukaḷum pala kōyilkaḷil uṇṭu. cōṭaca pūcikkuriya palvēṟu vakiyāṉa pūcā viḷakkukaḷum pittaḷiyilum veṇkalattilum uṇṭu. iviyaṉṟik karppūra viḷakkukaḷum, cāmpirāṇi pukikkum tūpat taṭṭukaḷum, aṭukkut tīpaṅkaḷumāka āyirakkaṇakkil tamiḻnāṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil uṇṭu. aracikaḷum, celvac cīmāṭṭikaḷum cempu, pittaḷi aimpoṉ pōṉṟa ulōkaṅkaḷil taṅkaḷ uruvaṅkaḷi, viḷak kēnti niṟkum pāvaṉiyil - pāvi viḷakkākac ceytu teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷiṉ muṉ vikkac ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. avaṟṟil neyvārttu viḷakkerippataṟku māṉiyaṅkaḷ aḷittiruppataṟkup pala ceppēṭukaḷum kalveṭṭu kaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭum cumār 10 āyiram kōyilkaḷiruk kalām eṉṟāl kēraḷam, karṉāṭakam, āntiram pōṉṟa tirāviṭa nāṭṭu mānilaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil ettaṉi veṇkalac cempup poruḷkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu cintittup pārkka vēṇṭum.
tamiḻnāṭṭil putiya kaṟkālat toṭakkattilē irumpu ūḻi eḻuntuviṭṭatāl, palvēṟu vakiyāṉa ulōkat toḻilkaḷ eḻuntuviṭṭaṉa. toḻil ceyyumtoḻilāḷarkaḷ, irumpuk kollarkaḷ, poṟkollarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, marat taccarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷukkuriya valuvāṉa kūriya kal uḷi, vāḷ, cuttiyal, vāycci, iṭukki, kuṟaṭu, poṭi veṭṭi, mara uḷi, iḻip puḷi, mūlaccaṭṭam, cammaṭṭi pōṉṟavikaḷic ceytu kuvit tuḷḷārkaḷ. nāṭṭil irumpuvēli ceyyum kollarkaḷ, poṟ kollarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, marattaccarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, tapatikaḷ pōṉṟa pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ perukiṉārkaḷ. ov voru toḻilukkum ovvoru iṉam ēṟpaṭṭatāl toḻilkaḷ ciṟappuṟṟeḻuntaṉa.
paṭimam vārkkum tapatikaḷ
teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa tapatikaḷukku īṭākak kōyil kaṭṭuvatilum, cilikaḷ camippatilum, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ vārppatilum tiṟamiyāṉavarkaḷ ulakil eṅkumē illi eṉṟu kūṟalām. tamiḻ nāṭṭup pāvi viḷakkiṟku īṭāka intiyāvil eṅkum aḻaku vāynta viḷakkukaḷip pārkka muṭiyātu. vaṅkan tanta tuṅkap periyār ō.ci.kaṅkūli, teṉ intiya viḷakkukaḷ5 eṉṟu 80 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻutiya kaṭṭuri oṉṟil kuṟippiṭ ṭiruppati ulakam eṉṟum maṟappataṟkilli. tamiḻnāṭṭil ceyyappaṭṭa veṇkalap paṭimam - civapuram naṭarācar paṭimam oṉṟu amerikkāvil 75 laṭca rūpāy matippuḷḷatāyk karutap paṭuvatu tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpattiṉ perumikku nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
ātitta nallūril akaḻ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭa iṭam iṭu kāṭāy iruntamiyāl atikamāṉa irumpup poruḷkaḷiyō veṇkalap poruḷkaḷiyō nām kāṇa muṭiyavilli. tamiḻakat til uḷḷa araṇmaṉikaḷiyum, nakaraṅkaḷiyum akaḻvāyvu ceyyum vāyppu nērntiruntāl, paṇṭiya tamiḻaka kammiyar kaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa kivaṇṇam vāynta - ciṟpa nuṭpam vāynta cempu, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷiṉ vakikaḷiyum, eṇṇikki kaḷiyum aḻakiyum uṟutiyiyum nām kāṇa muṭiyum.
paṇṭikkālat teṉ intiya varalāṟṟiṉi, namatu āyvukkup poruttamāṉa kōṇattil maṭṭum nām paricōtaṉi ceytu varukiṟōm. perumpālum vaṇika nōkkattōṭu makkaḷiṉ pulam peyarvu kāraṇamāṉa neruṅkiya toṭarpukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu nampikkoṇṭiruppōr palar uḷar. teṉ intiyar, cempu, veṇkalam, īyam mutaliya tātupporuḷkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷa muṉvantu, vaṭa pakutiyil payaṉuḷḷa paṇikaḷil amarn tiruntaṉar. atēvaḻiyil cintu veḷiyil kuṭiyēṟiya palar tārāḷa mākap piṟkālattil irumpuk karuvikaḷiyum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷiyum teṟkiṉiṉṟu vāṅki taṅkaḷiṉ tēvikaḷiyum avaciyattiyum niṟivu ceytirukkalām. itellām piṟkālat til eḻuntavi eṉpatil ciṟitum aiyamilli.
kālam
ātitta nallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mikap paḻami vāyntaṉa aḻaku vāyntaṉa. cintu veḷiyil cempum maṇṉum kalanta oru vaki ulōka (cālkōlitikap) paṇpāṭṭil uruppeṟṟa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷiviṭa irumpup paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭak kali kālattāl mikattoṉmi vāyntatu eṉpatil evarukkum aiyappāṭu eḻamuṭiyātu. potu nōkkōṭu intiya varalāṟṟi āynta āciriyarkaḷ cempum maṇṇum kalanta oru vaki ulōka ūḻiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 4000 mutal ki.mu. 2750 vari irukkalām eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.3 teṉ intiya varalāṟṟip potuvāka āynta āciriyarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 5000-m āṇṭil nikaḻntirukkalām eṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟip paṭippil īṭupaṭṭa orucilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl teṉ intiya varalāṟṟip palvēṟu uyarnta nōkkōṭu āynta aṟiñarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi ki.mu.8000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ nilavi irukkalām eṉṟu matippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
ātitta nallūr irumpu ūḻi, arappāviṉ cempu maṇṇum kalanta oruvaki ulōka (cālkōlitikap) paṇpāṭṭiviṭa evvāṟṟāṉum paḻami mikkatu - uyariyatu eṉpatil aiyamilli.
ātittanallūr paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kālattāl 3000 ām āṇṭiṟku muntiyaviyāyiṉum cey nērtti yil arappā paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiviṭac ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, pūvēlippāṭukaḷ atikam kāṇappaṭavilli. koṟki maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiviṭa aḻaku vāyntaṉa eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptu, kirīṭ, kirī mutaliya mēli nāṭukaḷil kiṭitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ēṟattāḻa arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi ottavikaḷākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēlippāṭukaḷi oppiṭṭup pārttāl arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭilum ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭilum arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, cumēriyā, mecapottāmiyā nāṭukaḷilum ēṉiya piṟa nāṭukaḷilum naṭipeṟṟa akaḻvu āyvil kaṇṭupiṭitta palvēṟu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu nalla orumippāṭuṭiyavi kaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu nām oru muṭiviṟku vara muṭikiṟatu - aḵtāvatu ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭi uruvākki ati vaḷartta ātittamiḻ makkaḷ uruvākkiya kalikaḷum, kip paṇikaḷum (Crafts) ayalnāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu paravic celvākkip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; mēlum mikat tolivil uḷḷa pala nāṭukaḷōṭum nēraṭiyākakkūṭa vaṇikat toṭarpum koṇ ṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatēyākum. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum nāvāykaḷum, marakkalaṅkaḷum, vaṅkaṅkaḷum, tamiḻnāṭu ki.mu.4000 ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē kaṭalmūlam mēṟku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kappal vaṇikam naṭattivantati naṉku cuṭṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.4
ātittanallūr irumpup paṇpāṭṭik kaṇṭa ātit tamiḻarkaḷ, nilattiṉ mūlam poti māṭukaḷil viḷiporuḷ kaḷiyum, ceyporuḷkaḷiyum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu cintu veḷiyiṉ vaḻiyākac cumēriyā, ellam ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam naṭattiyataṟkuk kaṭal vaṇikam evvitattilum taṭiyāka irukkavilli - ātittanallūr paṇpāṭṭil uruppeṟṟa poruḷkaḷukkum ēṉiya paḻiya nāṭukaḷil ceyyappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷukkum iṭiyē uḷḷa orumippāṭṭik kāṇpataṉ mūlam paṇpāṭṭiṉ kālatti uṟutippaṭuttuvatil nām uṉṉippāka irukkavēṇṭum. inta orumippāṭukaḷ paḻamic ciṉṉattic cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvataṟkut tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar kuṟip piṭuvativiṭa mika muntiya ki.mu. 3200il eḻunta kālattik kuṟip piṭuvatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptiṉ kūrṅkōpurat tūṇkaḷiṉ kālattiviṭap paḻamiyāṉatāka illā viṭṭālum allatu kūr kōpurak kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatāka iruntālum, cintuveḷi nākarikam camakāla nikaḻcci eṉṟu icivu koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷi muttirikaḷ cārkōṉik kālattiṟku muṉ iruntaṉa eṉa uṟutiyākak kūṟuvataṟku viḷakkam tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ irumpup paṇpāṭu
nām inta attiyāyatti muṭikkumuṉ oru ceytiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭavēṇṭiya niliyil irukkiṉṟōm. ulakil periya nākarikamākap pōṟṟappaṭum cintuveḷi nākarikattil - irumpiṉ upayōkam oru ciṟitum illi eṉpatēyākum. teṉ intiyā viṟkum cintuveḷikkum iṭiyē aṭikkaṭi pōkkuvarattukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, cempu veṇkala ūḻikku neṭuṅkālattiṟkup piṉṉarē irumpiṉ upayōkattic cintuveḷi makkaḷ aṟintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.5 teṉ intiyāviliruntu irumpup paṇpāṭu muṉ ceṉṟu, varuki terivikkumuṉ, intap paṇpāṭu oru cakāptamākak kāṭci taruvatu oru kāraṇamāka irukkiṟatu; allatu iṉṉum cariyākac colva tāṉāl intap paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu camakāla nikaḻcciyāka iruntiruk kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. eṉavē, nām ēṉ cintu veḷip paṇpāṭṭiṉi mika uyarnta paḻamic ciṉṉamākak kuṟippiṭa muṭiyātirukkiṟatu eṉpataṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, oru ciṟanta tolporuḷ āyvu vallunariṉ ariya karuttukkaḷi itaṉ aṭiyil kuṟippiṭṭu inta attiyāyatti muṭikkiṉṟōm! aḵtāvatu: oru karuttu mikatteḷivākat tavaṟiṉṟiyum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu; mokañcatārō arappā ākiya iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum inta nākarikam mutircciyaṭiyāta nākarikamāka veḷippaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl oṉṟu, intiya maṇṇil māṟṟa muṭiyātatum ēṟkeṉavē paḻami mikkatumāṉatu. ataṉ piṉṉaṇiyil pala nūṟāyirakkaṇakkāṉa, maṉitap paṇpukaḷip peṟṟirukkiṟa muyaṟcikaḷ amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, intiyā itu mutaṟkoṇṭu pāracīkam, mecapottāmiyā, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaḷil maṉita camūkam vaḷarcciyum mutal niliyum peṟṟa camūkattiṉ ceyaṟ pāṅkuḷḷa - nākarikam vāynta - mika mukkiyamāṉa nāṭukaḷip pōṉṟatāka aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpati nām eṭuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākum eṉpatākum.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VI
1.	Bruce Foote; Indian Pre-Historic and Proto Historic Antiquities. p. 25.
	(a)	The Neolithic age in South India passed on to the Iron Age without any interval long or short. In fact, the last centuries of the Neolithic age witnessed the making of iron implements which the Neolithic people used on a large scale. The date of this transition or the length of its duration cannot be fixed, with any satisfaction, though one school of archeologists contends that 5,000 B.C. may be roughly taken as the chronological limit for the beginning of the Iron age in South India” - Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 
1951. p. 95.
2.	Ancient History of the Near East by H.R. Hall.
3.	“Mohenjo-Daro and the Indus valley civilization by Sir John Marshall Vol. 1-3 (London) 1931.
4.	On the whole the state of civilization and culture by the finds of Mohenjo-Daro alone prove that from five thousand B.C. to two thousand B.C. the culture and civilization of Mesopotamia was in no way superior to that of India and Baluchistan. I have called this civilization Dravidian. Becuse the earliest example of its peculiar burial customs were discovered in Dravidian countries, but it is quite possible that the people who brought from another land were quite different from the modern inhabitant of Dravidian” - Dravidian Civilization - R.D.Banerji (an article) Modern Review - Calcutta 1927. p. 559.
5.	South Indian Lamps O.C. Gancoly (London) 1916.
6.	Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951. p. 124.
	“We have only examined such of the aspects as relavant to our study early the South Indian history. Believing as we do intimate contacts due to migration of people mostly for purposes of trade and commerce, the Indians came to contact with metals like copper and bronze, tin and lead which are usefully employed in the northern area. In the same way the inhabitants of the Indus - region freely borrowed iron tools and implements from the South to supplement their needs and wants” - Pre-historic South India by V.R.Rama-chandra Dikshtar M.A., (Madras) 1951.
 p. 126.

7. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ
maṭkalaṅkaḷ
paṇṭikkāla, mutirāp paṇpuṭiya mutumakkaḷ nākarika ulakil mutal aṭiyeṭuttu vittatum avarkaḷ uruvākkiya kiviṉip poruḷkaḷ maṭkalaṉkaḷēyām. mutaṉ mutalākap pacci maṇṇāl pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ ceyyap payiṉṟaṉar. appāl anta maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāṇṭaṅkaḷic cūḷiyiliṭṭu, nāṉṟāka vēka vittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cūḷiyiliṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ maṇṇiṉ taṉmik kēṟpa, cila civappākavum cila kaṟuppākavum iruntaṉa. ivi eḷitil uṭiyātavikaḷāy valuvāyntavi kaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa kalaṅkaḷ
ātitta nallūrp paṟampil aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil aṇipeṟat tikaḻum maṭkalaṅkaḷ 600kku mēl uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 100kku mēṟpaṭṭavikaḷi avar vakuttut tokuttuc ceṉṉi araciṉariṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟac ceytuḷḷār. avi iṉṟum nām kaṇṭukaḷittu āyvu ceyvataṟku ēṟṟavikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ceṉṉi mānila araciṉ arumporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟṟirukkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil 110 kalaṉkaḷi niḻaṟpaṭam piṭittu iriyā avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya ātitta nallūrilum perumpāyūrilumiruntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa ariya nūlil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷār. (paṭam, 6,7,8, pārkka) paṭam 6il 41 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, paṭam 7il 39 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum paṭam 8il 30 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷumāka 110 kalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa aṭiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa peyarkaḷ uḷḷa kalaṉkaḷākum.
kalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ
1. kiṇṇam	2. pāṉi
3. caṭṭi	4. kuṇṭāppōṇi
5. pāṉimūṭi	6. tāḻi

9. maravi	10. cempu
11. pōṇi	12. kuṇṭā

37. paṇiyāraccaṭṭi	38. mukkuḷiccaṭṭi
39. curikkāyp pōṇi	40. vaṭṭil
41. porikkaṉ caṭṭi	42. veṇṇeyccaṭṭi

49. kuḻampuccaṭṭi	50. toṭṭi
irīyā avarkaḷ paṭam piṭittu namakkut tantuḷḷa 110 maṭ kalaṉkaḷil 50kkut tāṉ peyar kāṇa muṭintatu. maṟṟavikaḷukkup peyar kāṇa muṭiyavilli. pala maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻak koḻintu pōṉavikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām peyar kuṟip piṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻakkil illātavikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kaviṉuṟum kalaṅkaḷ
putiya kallūḻiyil, teṉ intiyāvil kalaṉkaḷ ākkum kali eḻuntuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattil irumpukkāla ūḻiyil kalaṉkaḷ ākkum kali, ataṉ ucca kaṭṭatti eṭṭiyuḷḷatu. cintuveḷi yil vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ uruvākkiya kalaṉkaḷi viṭa, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kalaṉkaḷ toṉmiyāṉavi, tiṇmiyāṉavi, ciṟap pāṉavi. avi tamiḻar nākarikattiṟku ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nāḷaṭivil tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṭpāṇṭak kali vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭivanili, vaṇṇap polivu, eḻiṟ ceṟivu eṉṟa mupperum paṇpukaḷiṉ uccap pāṅkāka uyarntu oḷirat toṭaṅ kiyatu. kalaṉkaḷukkuk kaviṉuruvam kāṇpatil intiyarkaḷ iṇi yaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya aḻaku vāynta maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. aḻakum, vaṇṇamum uṟuti yum vāynta maṭkalaṉkaḷ eṉpataṟku koṟkiyil akaḻntukaṇṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷ nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ātitta nallūr, perumpāyūr, kōvi mutaliya ūrkaḷil kiṭitta pāṉi caṭṭikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum vaṇṇamum tiṇṇamum, vaṉappum vāyntavikaḷāl uḷḷattik kavarvaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ātitta nallūr, perumpāyūr, kōvi, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, pallāvaram iṉṉōraṉṉa eṇṇilā iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu kaṇṭa kalaṉkaḷum, tāḻikaḷum, uṭinta maṭpāṇṭa ōṭukaḷum ciṟappumikkaṉavāy matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ toṉmi, tiṇmi, vaṭivaḻaku, vaṇṇappolivu, ceyaṟ ceṟivu mutaliyaṉa pōṟṟaṟkuriyaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ilaṇṭaṉ mānakariṉiṉṟu, 1858ām āṇṭil veḷivanta intiyak kali, toḻiltuṟi itaḻ eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil, ceṉṉi ōviyak kallūrik kaṇkāṇippāḷar, tiru. eṭviṉ hōlṭar, ceṉṉi (mānila) maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟa talippil oru ciṟappuk kaṭṭuri eḻutiyuḷḷār.2 atu mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷ paṇṭiyat tamiḻmakkaḷ kaṇṭa maṭpāṇṭak kaliyiṉ kaviṉuṟum ciṟappi naṉkaṟiyumāṟu amintuḷḷatu.
kalaṉ ceyyum toḻil vaḷarcci
putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ, eṅkuḷḷavarāyiruntālum avarkaḷ tam vāḻkkiyil oru putiya toḻilik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ eṉṟu evar kūṟiṉum atu kalaṉ ceyyum kippaṇiyit tavirap piṟitoṉṟāka irukkamuṭiyātu. tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻnta putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷē, mutal variciyil, mutal kittoḻilik kaṇṭa maṉita iṉamāka matikkappaṭuvar. teṉ intiyāvē, niṉivuk keṭṭāta kālattiliruntu kittoḻilkaḷilum kali vaḷarppi lum taṉic ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyum. teṉ intiyāvil iṉṟum poṉṟātu uyiruṭaṉ nilavum oru paḻiya kittoḻil pāṉi-caṭṭi ceyyum kittoḻilē yākum. putiya kaṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭa palvēṟu vitamāṉa vaṭivamum vaṇṇamum vāynta maṭkalaṅkaḷ, aṉantappūr, karṉūl, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, putukkōṭṭi, tirucci, cēlam, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭavikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ vāḻnta inta māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa toṉmiyāṉa kuṭiyiruppukaḷil palavitamāṉa paḻiya maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cila, cātāraṇa māṉavikaḷāyum, cila aḻaku vēlippāṭukaḷiyuṭiyavi kaḷākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṟiñar puruṣ puṭ paḻaṅkālak kuṭimakkaḷ vāḻnta intap pakutikaḷil āyvu naṭatti mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṅkāla intap pakutikaḷil āyvu naṭatti mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṅkāla maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittār. avar kaṇṭupiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cila cūḷiyil iṭappaṭātavi. avi aṭuppilēṟṟic cōṟu, kuḻampu camippataṟku ēṟṟaṉavāka illi. pacci maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷākum. pala kaṟuppāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ; ciṟappāka mēṟpakuti vēkātavi kaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. cila pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ ventum vēkāma lum iruntaṉa. appāṉi caṭṭikaḷil civappāṉa pakuti ventu iruntatu. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ toṭakka kālattil ceyta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ karaṭu muraṭākavum ventum vēkāmalum iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, vaṭivaṅkaḷum muḻu niṟivākak kāṇap paṭavilli; kavarcciyāṉa tōṟṟamum aḷikkavilli. ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭeṭutta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ putiya kal ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil ceyyappaṭṭaviyāka irunta pōtilum avi nalla aḻakuṭiyaṉavākavum naṉṟāka ventaṉavākavum, muṟṟilum civappu vaṇṇam vāyntaviyākavum eṇṇilā vakikaḷiyuṭiyaṉavākavum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa.3
ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, naṉṟāka ventu civappu, kaṟuppu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil aḻakum uṟutiyum uṭiyaṉavāy irunta taṟkuk kāraṇam, avi irumpu ūḻiyilē eḻuntavi eṉpatē yākum. anta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷi vaṉinta maṇṇīṭṭāḷar, nalla karuvikaḷip peṟṟavarkaḷāyum paṭṭaṟivu vāyntavarkaḷākavum iruntatēyākum. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil vaṉiyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷ ciṟappumikka vikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.4
ātittanallūr akaḻvāyvu 110 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ naṭi peṟṟatu. aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya paṭṭiyal veḷi vantu aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlākiviṭṭatu. antap paṭṭiyal iṉṟuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷukkuk kiṭippatu aritu. atu tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyātatu. āṉāl, atippaṟṟiya āyvu nūl ituvari evarum eḻuti veḷiyiṭātatāl, iṉṟuḷḷa pala aṟiñarkaḷukku ataṉ mukkiyattuvam teriyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu. aṇmiyil veḷivanta māperum kalikkaḷañciya akarāti (The Readers Digest Great encyclopedic Dictionary) eṉṟa ciṟappumikka āṅkila nūlippaṭittēṉ. atil, tolporuḷ āyvu (Archeology) eṉṟa talippil cila kuṟippukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. atil 1937il car mārṭṭimar uyilar avarkaḷāl akaḻ āyvu ceyyap peṟṟa, ki.mu. 27il nilaviya nākarikattik kāṭṭum arikkamēṭu eṉṉum iṭattip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ataṟku eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nilaviya ātitta nallūr putiporuḷ akaḻvukaḷip paṟṟiya vivaram etuvum tarappaṭa villi. ati aṟintu mikavum varuntiṉēṉ.
ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya āyvu tamiḻ nāṭṭut tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟikkum varalāṟṟu āyvut tuṟikkum mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatākum. ataṉuṭiya meymmiyāṉa, aḻikka muṭiyāta nilikaḷiyum, payaṉkaḷiyum paṇpāṭukaḷiyum oppiṭṭup pārppataṟkum, namatu nāṭṭiṟkum, piṟa nāṭukaḷukkumuḷḷa vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷik kāṇpataṟkum, ataṉ kālaṅkaḷik kaṇippataṟkum nayattaku nākarikattiṉ naṉiyuṟu nilikaḷi nirṇayippataṟkum ōviyac ciṟappi uṇarvataṟkum kit toḻil vaḷattik kāṭṭuvataṟkum ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷ potuvākat teṉ intiyāvil tuḷirttuḷḷaṉa putiya kallūḻiyip paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ ākum. āṉāl, putukkallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil arumpiya irumpu ūḻiyi aṭuttu eḻunta maṭpāṇṭattoḻil, vaḷarcci peṟṟuk kalinayan taḻuviyatāy vaḷarntatu. ataṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, cey nērttiyum kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavāyt tikaḻntaṉa.5
paṇṭikkālattil vāḻnta paḻaṅkaṟkāla maṉitaṉ vilaṅkukaḷ pōl vāḻntāṉ. avaṉukku atikamāṉa tēvip poruḷ kiṭi yātu. pacikku ētāvatu uṇavum, nīrvēṭki tīrkkat taṇṇīrum vēṇṭum. iyaṟkiyiṉ kōḷāṟukaḷāl cila kālaṅkaḷil uṇavum nīrum taṭṭuppaṭum. appoḻututāṉ avaṉ cintikkat talippaṭu vāṉ. uṇavittēṭi, kiṭittati uṇṭu niṟivu peṟṟa maṉitaṉ appāl uṇavic cēkarittu vikkamuṟpaṭṭāṉ. putukkaṟkālat til avaṉ vāḻkkiyil oru tiruppam ēṟpaṭṭatu. avaṉatu cintaṉi vaḷarattalippaṭṭatu. kallālāṉa karuvikaḷiyum villākiya paṭikkaruviyiyum payaṉpaṭutta muṉintāṉ. nalla kallāyutaṅkaḷik kāṇa, kuṭi peyarntu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kilōmīṭṭar toliviṟkum - ēṉ ataṟku appālum naṭantu pōkat talippaṭṭāṉ. āṭumāṭukaḷip paḻakka muṟpaṭṭāṉ. puṉceyttoḻili vaḷarttāṉ. pālikkaṟakkavum neymōr tayir ceyyavum payiṉṟāṉ.
appāl kāḷimāṭukaḷip paḻakki uḻuvataṟkum, potikaḷ cumakkavum payiṟṟiṉāṉ. payirttoḻili vaḷarttāṉ. naṉceyp payirkaḷi vaḷarttu nalam peṟṟāṉ. tiṭīreṉṟu irumpu ūḻiyik kaṇṭu ataṉāl ēṟṟam peṟṟāṉ.6
kalappik koḻu, maṇveṭṭi, kaṭappāṟi, kōṭari, katti arivāḷ, paṉṉarivāḷ, toraṭṭi īṭṭi, eṟivēl, cūlam, muttalivēl, paracu mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu vēḷāṇmi ceytu teyvampōl uyarntāṉ. viḷiporuḷkaḷi mali yeṉak kuvittāṉ. vayiṟāra uṇṭāṉ; piṟamakkaḷukkum aḷittu akamakiḻntāṉ. ēṉiya nilattiluḷḷa makkaḷukku vārivāri vaḻaṅki vaḷḷalāka uyarntāṉ. tamiḻakattil uṇavu illi eṉṉum pēccukku muṟṟuppuḷḷi vittāṉ. uṇavukkuriya nel, pul, eḷ, koḷ maṭṭumaṉṟip paruttiyiyum payiriṭṭup pañci uṟpatti ceytāṉ. nūl nūṟkavum, necavu neyyavum vaḻikaṇṭāṉ. parutti āṭiyiyum, paṭṭāṭiyum neytu ariyil aṇin tāṉ; uṭal mēlē āṭiyip pōrttiṉāṉ; taliyil talip pāki aṇintu perumitam eytiṉāṉ. kāṭṭit tirutti, vīṭṭik kaṭṭi caṭṭipāṉikaḷi vittu uṇavu camittāṉ. vīṭukaḷil kutirkaḷil uṇavukkuriya kūlap poruḷkaḷiyum, kuṭaṅkaḷil taṇṇīriyum piṭittu vikkat terintāṉ. avaṉatu vaḷamvāynta vāḻviṟku ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ tuṇiyāka iruntaṉa.
cintuveḷi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ
1920ām āṇṭil arappāvum 1922ām āṇṭil mokañcatārōvum akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cintu veḷi akaḻvāyvu intiya miya araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi iyakkunar taḷapati car. cāṉ avarkaḷ talimiyil toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. arappāvi mātō carūp vāṭ (M.S.Vats) avarkaḷum, mokañcatārōvi irākēl tā pāṉarji (R.D. Banerji) avarkaḷum akaḻvāyvu naṭattiṉar.
cintu veḷi akaḻvāyvil, pañcāpil uḷḷa arappā, cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa mokañcatārō nakaraṅkaḷ maṭṭumalla, iṉṉam 20kku mēṟpaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷ akaḻntu kāṇappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟil vīḻccikkālam ki.mu. 3500 āṇṭu mutal ki.mu. 2750 āṇṭukaḷ vari irukkalām eṉṟu kaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
iṅku naṭipeṟṟatu, āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭu. intap paṇ pāṭṭil eḻuntatu uḻavar nākarikamākum. iṅkuk kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭa ulōkam cempum, veṇkalamumēyākum. iṅkuk kiṭit tuḷḷa taliyāya poruḷkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum ōviya eḻuttuk kaḷik koṇṭa muttirikaḷumām. iṅkuk kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷi naṉku cōtittu, avi paluccitāṉattiluḷḷa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu oppuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kāṇap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 1924ām āṇṭu mokañcatārōviṉ meyyuru mella ulakiṟkut teriyat toṭaṅkiyatu.
cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa mokañcatārō, arappā, kulli mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. arappā, mokañcatārō, caṅkutārō, lokuñcutārō, urūpār, kul, nāl, amri kāḷipaṅkaṉ mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷ akaḻntu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. cintuveḷi nākarikamē ulakil akaḻntu kaṇṭa nākarikaṅkaḷil periya nākarikamāka matikkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭanākarikam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatum ulakiṟku oru pēratircciyāy iruntatu. cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭi viṭac ciṟantatākavum ativiṭap paḻamiyāṉatākavum uḷḷa paṇpāṭāka viḷaṅkiya ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu ulakil kavaṉippāraṟṟuk kiṭantatu. āṉāl, mokañcatārō nakari akaḻntukaṇṭa vaṅkap pēraṟiñar arappāviṟkum ātitta nallūrukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa toṭarkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, makkaḷiṉ kavaṉatti ātitta nallūr īrkkumpaṭi ceyya muyaṉṟuḷḷār.
maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ māṇpu
ātitta nallūril vāḻnta makkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. avarkaḷit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ (Proto Tamils) eṉṟō, tamiḻarkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil vāḻnta avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ (Pre-Tamilian) eṉṟō, kūṟa vēṇṭiya tilli vēṇṭumāṉāl tamiḻarkaḷit tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ (Pioneers of the dravidian race) eṉṟu kūṟalām. tamiḻarkaḷ, vāḻviṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭitta vakivaki yāṉa maṭkalaṉkaḷum, tāḻikaḷum, veṇ kalappāttiraṅkaḷum, poṉṉaṇikalaṉkaḷum, irumpup poruḷkaḷum paḻaṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nākarikattiyum kittoḻil ciṟappiyum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭu upayōkat tiṟkuriyaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷēyākum. eṉavē, avaṟṟip paṟṟi iṅku āyvōm.
tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar, eṅku āyvit toṭaṅkiṉum aṅkuk kiṭikkum pala tiṟappaṭṭa poruḷ kaḷukkuḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷukku oru ciṟappāṉa iṭam aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum oru nakaram, allatu nāṭṭiviṭṭu makkaḷ kuṭipeyarum poḻutu aṅku viṭṭuc cellum poruḷkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷēyākum. iṉṉum atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟapiṉpum kāṇappaṭuvaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ uṭinta ōṭṭuc cillukaḷēyākum. avaṟṟiyē avarkaḷ avviṭatti, viṭṭucceṉṟa paḻaṅkālamakkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kavaṉamāka eṭuttu āyvu ceykiṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ citivukaḷum toṉmiyāṉa kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ nākarikatti uṇarttum aḻiyāccāṉṟukaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyāṉa maṇkaḷik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭavikaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. karical maṇṇikkoṇṭu kalaṉkaḷic ceytu cūḷiyi liṭṭu vēkumpaṭi ceytāl kalaṉkaḷ kaṟuppu vaṇṇam uṭiyavi kaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. mañcaḷ niṟamāṉa maṇṇum āṟṟu maṇalum kalantu ceyyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷ cūḷiyiliṭṭu ventatum civappu niṟamāka irukkum. iḵtaṉṟip palavaṟṟiṟku vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. cila maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷic cuṭuvataṟku muṉ ataṉ kaḻuttuppakutiyil vaṭṭamākac ciṟu kuccukaḷ allatu kampikaḷ koṇṭu pū vēlippāṭukaḷ ceytu cūḷiyiliṭṭu vēkavittup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟārkaḷ. intap pū vēlippāṭu kaḷil aḻakiya mayilum kuyilum, māṉum māṭum, palvēṟu vitamāṉa ilikaḷum pūkkaḷum kāykaḷum kaṉikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītu merukiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. cila, mēlē kaṟuppu, nīlam, ūtā pōṉṟa vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, uṭpuṟam veḷḷi niṟamum tīṭṭappaṭṭavikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil poṅkalpaṭi koṭuppataṟkup periya pāṉikaḷ mītu veḷḷic cuṇṇattiṉāl pūṅkoṭikaḷ tīṭṭi ataṉuḷ poṅkal viḻāviṟku uriya poruḷkaḷi vittu vaḻaṅkuvatum uṇṭu.
maṭkalaṉkaḷ, avi ceyyappaṭṭakālam, makkaḷiṉ aṟivu, kivaṇṇam, ceyvaṇṇam iṉṉōraṉṉa palavaṟṟi uṇarttu vaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. ittakiya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷi eṇṇi ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvic ceytavarkaḷ aṅkup pala iṭaṅkaḷit tōṇṭi, tāḻikaḷiyum avaṟṟuḷ irunta kalaṉkaḷiyum, piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum oṉṟiyum viṭātu cōtittu, cēkarittu, iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷic cuttam ceytu arumporuḷ kāṭciccāliyil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar.
palavakip pāṇṭaṅkaḷ
ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu kaṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum āyntāl avi palatiṟappaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷ vāyntavikaḷākak kāṇappaṭum. pala naṉṉiliyil uḷḷaṉa. pala citintum cīrkulintum uṭintum pōṉaviyākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. koṟkiyil aṇmiyil naṭattappaṭṭa akaḻvāyvil uṭiyāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭippatu aritāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl, aṭukkaṭukkāyp palvēṟuvakiyāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ uṭintupōṉa tuṇṭukaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭil maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu vaṇṇan tīṭṭiya maṭkalaṅkaḷ vaṇika muṟiyil ayal nāṭukaḷukku aṉuppappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. eñciyavi aṅkut tīṇṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭantu maṟintum uṭintum pōyuḷḷaṉa. cempuk kālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi makkaḷukkup putikkum vaḻakkam putitāka iruntatu. irumpuk kālattil vāḻnta teṉ intiya makkaḷ vakivakiyāṉa vaṇṇam vāynta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷic ceytuḷḷaṉar. avikaḷi ērāḷa māka aṅkuḷḷa putikuḻikaḷil vittum putittuḷḷaṉar. cintu veḷiyil cempu ūḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ irumpu ūḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭa ēṟṟamiku teṉṉintiya maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷukku īṭāka irukka muṭiyātu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟuvar.7 atu iṅku meyyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷukku iṇi yākap piṟa enta ūḻiyilum aḻakiya - nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ aminta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷik kāṇamuṭiyātu. maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷi vaṉiyum vēṭkōvarkaḷiṉ iyantiraṅkaḷum tikirikaḷum, ūcikaḷum piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷum irumpiṉāl iruntatāl, avar kaḷāl vaṉiyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ taṉic ciṟappuṭiyaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar ṭākṭar kōrṭaṉ cilṭu camūkap pariṇāma vaḷarcci eṉṟa nūlil virivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷār.8 ivar ātittanallūril arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil tikaḻum ariya, nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷip pārttuttāṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉārō eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. āṉāl avar ātitta nallūril irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷip pārttatē illi.
aṟiñar ār.ṭi.pāṉarcci avarkaḷ, tirunelvēli māvaṭ ṭattil uḷḷa ātittanallūril ceyyappaṭṭa akaḻāyvukaḷum avaṟṟil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭam, teyva uruvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷum, cintu, pañcāp, paluccittāṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅ kaḷil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ uyariya paṇpāṭukaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyi uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. avi vaṭa intiyāviṉiṉṟu teṉ intiyā vari caṅkilit toṭarpōl toṭarntu nilavukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.9

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VII
1.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir - by A Rea (Madras) 1915 pp. 21-40 Plate VI-IX.
2.	Madras pottery-Edwin Holder (The journal of Indian Art and Industry (London) 1858.
3.	In the shevaroy hills in the district of Tinnevelly the ceded districts, Hyderabad and Baroda, we meet with plenty of neolithic pottery. Bruce Foot mentions 127 place (Including Adichanalloor) where he discovered Pre-historic pottery 56 places yielded neolithic types, two of the transitional formation between the Neolithic and iron Ages 60 Iron Age types and the rest specimens of the later Iron Age.
4.	We came upon another aspect of the ancient culture of the Iron age and this is the development of the Neolithic pottery (at Adicha-nalloor)...
	“The vessels of this period are not rough but have a better polish. Gradually also potter began to use varieties of colours. Though the necessary pigments for producing the greenish hue and blue and blue varieties are still later, we can credit the potter of the Iron Age with some varieties ranging from yellow brown and grey. This shows that the potter had considerably improved his knowledge of firing the vessels which was rather limited inscope and extend till then. The pottery produced in this age is therefore not dull in colour but lustrous enough. In addition to this the potter of the Iron Age made his ware more and more an object of beauty by artistically decorating some of his vessels. Originally continued to the decoration of the objects with the figures of leaves and flowers now we see more and more of human and animal figures” - Pre-Historic South India - V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951 pp. 107-108.
5.	Pre-Historic South India - V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951 p. 107.
	“We come upon another aspect of the ancient culture of the iron age and this is the development of the Neolithic pottery. We must examine this question with some circumspection. No doubt the Neolithians knew the art of pottery and had to some extent a knowledge of the wheel which the potter skillfully used. But speaking generally about pottery it has been concluded that the vessels were more or less crude and presented a rough exterior. The majority of the vessels show that they were black in colour though red varieties were also known. But we come to the age of the iron culture we see a surprising varieties of vessels made of clay”.
6.	Ibid p. 97.
7.	The absence of pinted pottery in the parts of India and in all other periods of Indian History is extormely significant. I probably indicate the first period after the immigration of the people into Baluchistan and Indus vally in which habit, manners and customs were almost the same with faience-using copper age people who had burial customs peculiar to themselves. They with the near approach of the iron age the use of pinted pottery suddenly dies out the best forms of South Indian pottery though carefully wheel turned cannot compare with copper age product of the Indus valley and Baluchistan” -Dravidian Civilization Prof R.D. Banerji (Modern Review, Calcutta) Nov. 1927. p. 558
8.	Social evolution by Gordon Child (London) 1951.
9.	The Recent Discoveries in Sindh and Baluchistan prove that the cultural affinities of the Dravididan extended in an unbroken line from the Tinnevelly district in the extreme South of the Indian peninsula through Sindh and Baluchistan the island Bahrein in the Persian gulf South Persia, Mescpotamia into crete and some of the islands of the Eastern Mediterranean” - Dravidian Civilization by Prof.R.D.Banerji (Modern Review) Sep. 1927 p. 308.

8. tāḻikaḷ
(Earthernware Sarcophagi)
mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ
ātitta nallūrp paṟampil uḷḷa iṭukāṭṭil akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa piṇaṅkaḷi vittup putikkum tāḻikaḷ (Sarcophagi) ākum. ittāḻiyi, ulaka vaḻakkil nāṭṭu makkaḷ matamattaṉ tāḻi eṉpar. aṟiñarkaḷ ati mutu makkaḷ tāḻi eṉpar. mutumakkaḷ tāḻi eṉṉum coṟṟoṭar tiripuṟṟu matamattaṉ tāḻi āyiṟṟu. itic cilar, piṇañ cuṭu cāmpaṟ koḷkalam (Urn) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻittu varukiṉṟaṉar. iḵtaṉṟic cāṭi, kuṭuvi, maṇcavap peṭṭi eṉṟellām aḻippatum uṇṭu. iṉṟu it tāḻi eṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭālum ātitta nallūrttāḻi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. it tāḻi ātitta nallūriṉ ciṉṉamāka amintuḷḷatu.
tāḻiyiṉ ciṟappu
tolporuḷāyvuttuṟiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭil aimpatu iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu palatāḻikaḷik kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷē ciṟappiṟkuriyavikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭil uḷḷa putikuḻikaḷ 4 aṭimutal 9 aṭi vari akalamum, 6 aṭi mutal 15 aṭi vari āḻamum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi akaḻntu naṭuvē 4 mutal 11 aṭivari uyaramuḷḷa tāḻikaḷil iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭali vittup putittuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kuḻikaḷil nāṟpuṟamum peruṅkaṟkaḷi vittuc cuvarpōl uyaramāka eḻuppi naṭuvē cuṭu maṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa tāḻiyil cavattiṉi vittup putittuḷḷaṉar. iv vaḻakkam tol paḻaṅkālattil nilaviyatu. ātittanallūril akaḻnteṭutta tāḻi kaḷil maṉita elumpukaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil oru cila elumpukaḷ maṭṭumē uḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷ uṭkārn tirukkum niliyil vittu aṭakkam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻi kaḷil (puti kalaṉkaḷil), uyir nītta makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷ vikkap paṭuvataṟku muṉpāka uṭalil oruvaki maruntip pūci uṭal aḻiyātu irukkac ceyyum muṟikaḷi muṭitta piṉṉarē, tāḻiyi mūṭi, avaṟṟi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu kallaṟikaḷil aṭakkam ceyvar. āṉāl, avvāṟu aḻiyātirukkum uṭal etuvum tamiḻakattil illi. ekiptil 4000 āṇṭukaḷākiyum aḻiyā tirukkum piṇaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṟkaḷi variciyāka oṉṟaṉmēl oṉṟāka aṭukki naṭuvil piṇaṅkaḷi vittu mūṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iruntatu. anta ūḻiyip peruṅkallaṟip paṇpāṭṭu ūḻi (Magalithic age) eṉpar. teṉ intiyāvil varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil piṇakkuḻikaḷic cuṟṟip peruṅ kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amippatic ciṟanta vaḻakka mākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. intak kaṟkaṭṭup paṇpāṭu, palavakip paṭum. āṉāl, teṉ intiyap piṇakkuḻikaḷil irumpup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa intiyap piṇak kuḻikaḷilum miya nilak kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷilumuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum irumpu kāṇappaṭavilli. aitarāpāttilum micūr mānilattilum cilavakip peruṅkallaṟip paṇpāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ekiptunāṭṭil tamiḻnāṭṭip pōl, iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷukku maruntu pūci vittu uḷḷaṉar. avi keṭātiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avar kaḷiṉ kurutit toṭarpuṭiya tamiḻarkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷ aḻiyātu irukkak kiyāṇṭa muṟikaḷ veṟṟipeṟṟatākat teriyavilli.
ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭutta tāḻikaḷiṉ vāykaḷ akaṉ ṟuḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷmītu nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷmītu mūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷiṉ uḷḷē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭikkalaṅkaḷum veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum avarkaḷuṭaṉ putikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷil poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟil vēl, īṭṭi, vāḷ, mūvilivēl pōṉṟa irumpup paṭikkalaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil piṇaṅkaḷi naṉṉīril kuḷippāṭṭip puttāṭi puṉintu paṉṉīr teḷittuc cantaṉam pūci, naṟumaṇa mālikaḷ cūṭṭi, nalla cāmpirāṇip pukiyūṭṭi, pāṭiyil ēṟṟicceṉṟu iṭukāṭṭil putippatu iṉṟuvari iruntuvarum vaḻakkam ākum. āṉāl, inta marapu vaḻakkam cila tamiḻ iṉattavarkaḷiṭam maṭṭumēyuḷḷatu. pala iṉattavarkaḷiṭam illi; kāraṇam etuvum namakkut teriyavilli.
tāḻi vakikaḷ
iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷi vittup putitta tāḻikaḷ pala iṉṟu tamiḻakattil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ pala uḷa. avaṟṟil iru vēṟ ṟumiyāṉa amippukaḷ uḷa. oṉṟu vāyakaṉṟu aṭi kuvintu, nuṉi maṭṭum taṭṭiyāy uḷḷatu. (paṭam eṇ 1). ivi ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa ēṉiya maṭkalaṉkaḷōṭu oppiṭa muṭiyātaṉa. ataṉ ceynērtti ivaṟ ṟil pulappaṭavilli. ivi karaṭumura ṭākak kōra vaṭivil uḷḷaṉa. ivi iṟantārip putippataṟkeṉṟē taṉi muṟiyil ceyyappaṭṭavikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum it tāḻikaḷ ēṉiya kalaṉkaḷiviṭa mikak kaṉattaviyākavum kutirkaḷ pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(paṭam 2) itu ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉuṭiya tōṟṟam muṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟa eṇ 1il uḷḷa tāḻiyiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟu paṭṭatu. āṉāl, itaṉ tōṟṟamum vēlippāṭum ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. oru vēḷi uyar niliyil uḷḷa aṟivarkaḷ, camaya kurukkaḷ, cāṉṟōr, aracar, atikārikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ uṭal kaḷi vittup putikkum cāṭiyāka iruntālum irukkum. vāy ciṟitu, uṭal nīḷamāṉatu, kaḻuttu nīṇṭatu. puyam kaccitamāṉatu, aṭi ciṟuttup puyam vari akaṉṟuḷḷatu. eṇ oṉṟum, eṇ iraṇṭum aṭippakuti ciṟitu maṇṇiṟ kuḷ vittuc ceṅkuttāka niṟkumpaṭi putikkattakkaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa elumpukaḷi vittup putikkum putikalaṉ vaṭṭamāṉa ciṟiya taṭṭiyāṉa nuṉiyi yuṭiyatu. aṭippākam vaṭṭamāṉa niliccaṭṭattōṭu amin tuḷḷatu. kaḻuttu ciṟuttu, tōḷ pākam akaṉṟu vāy ciṟuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ aṭippakuti vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa nilic caṭṭattiṉ mītu, tāḻi cāyntu viṭātavāṟu poruttap peṟ ṟuḷḷatu. (paṭam 3) itu oru periya kūcā vaṭivil aḻakuṟac camik kappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. mēṟpakuti, karaṭu muraṭāka aṉṟi vaḻuvaḻuppāka merukūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa pallāvarattil akaḻntu kaṇ ṭeṭukkappeṟṟatu. itaṉ amippu, ātittanallūril akaḻntu, eṭukkap paṭṭa mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu muṟṟi lum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa perumpāyūrp pakutiyil pala puti kuḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ cila puti kalaṉkaḷi eṭuttu, āyntuḷḷār. (paṭam 4) avi pallā varattiluḷḷa putikalaṉkaḷiṉ vaṭi vatti ōraḷavu ottiruppiṉum aṭiyil amintuḷḷa pakutiyum mūṉṟu kāl kaḷum uṭalum ātitta nallūrt tāḻi kaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉavāka viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. kaḻuttum vāyum pallāvaram putikuḻikkalaṉi ottiruppiṉum atippōṉṟu nīṇṭirāmal kuṭṭiyāka uḷḷaṉa. ivi ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa mutumakkaḷiṉ tāḻikaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattiṟkup piṉ eḻuntaviyāka eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa intiyāvil cempu ūḻiyil eḻunta puti kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭutta tāḻikaḷ oruvitamāka uḷḷaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻiyil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ vēṟu vita māka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ : itu tavaṟu. tirāviṭa nāka rikam eṅku nilavi iruppiṉum orē aṭippaṭiyil amintirukkiṟatu eṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmi yākum.
perumpāyūril aṟiñar alek cāntar irīyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa putikalaṉkaḷil (tāḻikaḷil) cila, ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu cila vēṟupāṭukaḷi uṭiyaṉa. (paṭam 5 aip pārkka). aṭippaṭi oṉṟutāṉ eṉṟālum itu. paṭam 2aip pōṉṟu vāyum kaḻuttum, tōḷum aḻakāka amintuḷḷatu. āṉāl, aṭip pākam maṭṭum māṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivi, nilattil tāḻi ceṅkuttāka niṟkattakkatāka aṭi akaṉṟu ciṟiya mūṉṟu kuṭṭiyāṉa kāl kaḷiyuṭiyaṉavākat tiṟamiyuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. viḷakkamākak kūṟuva tāṉāl ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷtām ivaṟṟiviṭa amivuṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intap perumpāyūr tāḻi ceṅkuttāka, niṟka vittu iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu amin tuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. mēlum perum pāyūril itiviṭa akaṉṟu nīṇṭa periya kālkaḷiyuṭiya tāḻiyum (putikalaṉum) oru kāliyuṭiya nilip pēḻimītu, uruḷi vaṭivamāṉa pāṉi pōṉṟu vikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. āṉāl, ataṉ vāy ēṉiya putikalaṉkaḷiṉ vāykaḷi viṭa mikac ciṟiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṉṉāḷil uḷḷa ceṉṉi mānilattiṉ malapār māvaṭṭattil kaṟuppuvaṇṇam vāynta oruvakit tāḻikaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi ātitta nallūrt tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu atika vēṟṟumi yuṭiyaṉavalla. āṉāl, ciṟiya kaḻuttum akalamāṉa aṭippakutiyum uḷḷaṉa. vaṇṇamum kaṟup pāka amintuḷḷatu. kaḻuttu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭut tāḻikaḷip pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭālum aṭippakuti akaṉṟu nilattil vittāl ceṅkuttāy naṉku niṟpataṟku ēṟṟatāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uṭal arappāvil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷippōl akaṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. it tāḻikaḷ mika uṟutiyākac ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷip pōṉṟu pallāvaram, perumpāyūr, paḻiyakāyal, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭitta tāḻikaḷ aṉittum karuñcivappu vaṭivattil kāṇap paṭum poḻutu inta malapār māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tāḻikaḷ maṭṭum kaṟuppuvaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātittanallūrilē karumivāynta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟālum, tāḻikaḷ kaṟuppu vaṇṇattil kāṇappaṭuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ātittanallūril kāṇappaṭṭa pala maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟākak kaṟutta nalla paḷapaḷappuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum nilippēḻi mītu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pārppataṟku aḻakāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.
aṟiñar nallāyar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ paḻiyakāyal pakkattil akaḻntukaṇṭa cāṭi eṉṟu collap paṭum putikalaṉ oṉṟu 11 aṭi nīḷam iruntatu. itaṉi ēṟkaṉavē viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷ ḷōm. itaṉ uruvam tirunelvēli māvaṭṭat tiluḷḷa muṟkālap payirt toḻilāḷar illaṅ kaḷil nel, pul, eḷ, koḷ, ciṟupayaṟu, perum payaṟu pōṉṟa kūlapporuḷkaḷip pātukāppāka vikkappaṭum cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa periya kutirkaḷiṉ vaṭivil uḷḷatu. vāy caṟṟu akalamāyum uṭal nīḷamāyum uḷḷatu. kaḻuttu kaṭṭiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ piṇaṅkaḷi niṉṟa niliyil vittup putikkakkūṭiyatu. itu paṭukkum niliyil uṭali vittuc cāṭi vāyilil mūṭiyiṭṭup putikkum nalla kallaṟip peṭṭi (Sarcopagi) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu vantatu eṉat terikiṟatu. itaṉāl, irumpu ūḻiyil, tamiḻakattil palvēṟu vakiyāṉa putikalaṉkaḷil vittup piṇaṅkaḷip putittatākat terikiṟatu.
cintuveḷiyil cempu ūḻiyil eḻunta arappaṉ paṇpāṭum tamiḻakat til irumpu ūḻiyil taḷirtta ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭum ēṟattāḻac cama kālattil arumpiyatākac cilarāl karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. iru nākarikaṅ kaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōr kaḷic cērnta āṟṟaṅkari nākarikaṅ kaḷēyākum. iṉṉum teḷivākak kūṟuvatāṉāl civaṉi muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu, paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vāḻum vēḷāḷar paṇpā ṭākum. inta arappaṉ paṇpāṭum ātittanallūr āṟṟaṅkarip paṇ pāṭum mikat tolivil vevvēṟu ūḻikaḷil eḻunta paṇpāṭāka iruppiṉum kālattāl iṉat tāl paṇpāl, camayattāl nākarikattāl mika neruṅkiya orumippāṭu uṭiyaṉavākum. ātitta nallūrukkum, cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa arappā (Hara-ppa)É‰F« cumār 2400 ki.mī. iṭiveḷiyiruppiṉum, pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷ illāta kālattil aḵtāvatu 4000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉpē nāṭṭut toṭarpum iṉattoṭarpum paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpum camayattoṭarpum eḻun tuḷḷatu; neruṅkiya orumippāṭu malarntuḷḷatu. arappaṉ paṇpāṭum ātitta nallūr tirāviṭappaṇpāṭum caṅkilit toṭarpōl amintuḷḷatu eṉṟu ulakaṟiya urittār mokañcatārō akaḻ āyvi muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattiya nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam vāynta pērāciriyar tiru. ār.ṭi. pāṉarci avarkaḷ ātittanallūr tirāviṭa nākarikattip paṟṟi, car. cāṉ mārcal, muṉivar i.cē.eyc. mekkē, em.e.em. vāṭcu, muṉivar em.uyilar pōṉṟa pēraṟiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nūlkaḷil eṅkum kuṟippiṭātirukkum poḻutu tiru.ār.ṭi.pāṉarci mikattuṇivāka ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭiyum naṉkaṟintu tirāviṭa intiyā viṉ talinakarāṉa arappā nākarikattiyum teṉintiyāviṉ tamiḻar nākarikattiṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttakkāṭṭāka ilaṅkum mutu perum paṇpāṭākiya ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭi iṇikkum nākarikattiyum aṟintu eṭuttakkāṭṭiyataṟku nām viyappaṭi yātirukka muṭiyavilli.1 tamiḻarkaḷ enta nāṭṭilum, kaṇkāṇāt tīvukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi iruntālum, ettuṇi āṇṭiṟku muṉ kuṭipeyarntiruntālum, aṉṉiya iṉattavarkaḷōṭu kalantu pōyiruntālum evarum tamiḻarkaḷi eḷitil taṭam kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭa muṭiyum. avarkaḷiṉ camayam, teyvam, uṭi, naṭi, paṇpu, nākarikam, moḻi, marapu vaḻakkam ākiyavi māṟātu iruntu varuvatu avarkaḷi eḷitil kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku ētuvāy amintuḷḷatu.
ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷum arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta tāḻikaḷum ottaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. arappāvil kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷaṉavākak kaḻuttiṉṟi vāy akaṉṟu nuṉi kūmpiyatākak kāṇappaṭuvatu viyappākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivi ātitta nallūrt tāḻikaḷiṉ piratipalippu eṉpatil ciṟitum aiyam illi. itaṉi viḷakka ār.ṭi. pāṉarci mīṇṭum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
orumippāṭu
cempu ūḻimakkaḷ veṇkala ūḻimakkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅ kaḷi iṅkut teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. atōṭu mecapottāmiyā, miya āciyā, ciṉṉa āciyā (Asia Minor) kiḻakku miyanilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷiṉ tīvuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟappākak kiriṭ tīvukaḷilum uḷḷa veṇkala ūḻip paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu mikat teḷivāka iṇintiruppati mika virivāka vivarittuc cempu ūḻi, veṇkalūḻi makkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ aṅku nilaviyaṉa eṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. mokañcatārō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa mūṭappaṭṭa kallaṟip putiyalkaḷum (Cist burials) cāṭipputiyalkaḷum (Jar burials), kallaṟikaḷilum, putikalaṉkaḷilum tāḻikaḷilum vittup putikkum vaḻakkatti ottiruntatu. teṉ intiyā, teṉ kiḻakkup pakutikaḷōṭum mekrāṉ (Mekran) teṟkup pāracīkam mecapottāmiya, miyanilakkaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷuṭiya putikkum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷōṭu mikavum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. paḵriṉ tīvilum pāracīkattiluḷḷa puhir (Buhire) arukilum mātirip putiyalkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
car. ulliyam auli (Sir William Ouseley) eṉṉum aṟiñarāl puhir aṇmiyil uḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa irukāṭukaḷil uḷḷa putikuḻikaḷil maṉita elumpukaḷ cāṭikaḷi lum maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷilum vittup putikkappaṭṭiruntatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.3
maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷil vittō, allatu ativiṭac ciṟappākac ceyyappaṭṭa cāṭikaḷil vittō cavaṅ kaḷip putikkum vaḻakkamāṉatu mika nākarikamāṉatu. inta nākarika māṉa ceyal ekiptu, mecapottāmiyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭu kaḷilum, cintuveḷippakutikaḷilum paraviyuḷḷatu. āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil camakālattil alla itaṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē vakivakiyāṉa tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷi vittup putikkum vaḻakkam nilaviyuḷḷatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu.
veḷināṭṭut tāḻikaḷ
mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa puti kalaṉkaḷ aṉittum vaṭṭavaṭivilāṉa aṭippakutiyi uṭiyavi. arappā vil akaḻntu kaṇṭa putikalaṉkaḷākiya cāṭikaḷil oṉṟu nuṉi kūriyatāka uḷḷatu. puhiril kūṭa attakiya cāṭikaḷ kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil pirāmaṇapāttilum ittakiya cāṭikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu intiyāvil uḷḷa arumporuṭ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.4 āṉāl, iṅku 1882 ām āṇṭil etuvum kāṇappaṭavilli.5 ciṟitu kālattiṟku muṉṉar pahriṉ tīvil ceyyappeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa kallaṟikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa cāṭi kaḷum, tāḻikaḷum kūriya aṭippakutiyi uṭiyaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa.6 ṭirāy (Troy) nakaril cilimāṉ (Schleman) avarkaḷ itē pōṉṟa tāḻikaḷ palavaṟṟi akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. putikalaṉkaḷ (larnakes) mecapottāmiyāvilum, mokañcatārōvilum pirāmaṇapāttilum teṉṉintiyāvil palaviṭaṅkaḷilum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa piṇappēḻi yil (Coffin-š) mika nuṭpamākac ceyyappeṟṟa puti viṉiyil pāyiliṭṭu mūṭappaṭṭa elumpukaḷ iruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.7 kuḷippu-kuḻāyvaṭivāṉa (Bath-tube-shape) putikalaṉ kaḷum (Larnakes) nippūr cāṭipputiviṉiyum nīṅkaḷ aṟintirup pīrkaḷ. āṉāl, kirīṭ tīvil, cāṭip putiviṉikaḷil mika nuṭpa māṉa, putikalaṉkaḷum, ciṟitum peritumāṉa kallaṟip putikuḻi kaḷum (Cist graves), camāti maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, niṟivuṭiyaṉavāka, kālavarici muṟippaṭiyāṉa niyatiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa.8 aṅkup putiviṉikaḷ, pūccāṭikaḷ, cempu allatu pittaḷittaṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, katti kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa vāy iruntaṉa. naṭumiṉkaṇ (Middle mincan) kālattil, kirīṭtīvil tāḻipputi viṉikaḷ niṟivāṉa paḻakkamāy nilaviyiruntaṉa. avikaḷi nām ātittanallūril kāṇkiṟōm eṉṟa varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.9
tamiḻnāṭṭilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum piṇaṅkaḷip putikkum muṟikaḷi iṅku virivāka eṭuttakkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. vaṭa intiyā, pāracīkam, mecapottāmiyā, ciṉṉa āciyā, kirīṭ tīvu pōṉṟavikaḷil kāṇum muṟikaḷ, orē kāla veḷiyīṭṭut tokutiyil kāṇappaṭupavikaḷāy uḷḷaṉa. aḵtu ucca niliyil viṭāt toṭarpuḷḷatu. iḵtaṉṟi intiya varalāṟṟaṟiñar cuṉiti kumār caṭṭarjiyiṉ intiyap pataṅkaḷāṉa. tamiḻ, tammil, (Damil) tirāviṭa eṉpavikaḷum ulakiṉ mutal varalāṟṟāciriyarāṉa heraṭṭōṭṭa eṉpavar termilāy (Termili) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa atē poruḷilum, tirimamili (Drimmili) eṉṟu paḻiya iliciyaṉ cācaṉaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭupavi eṉṟu ōr aṟiñar kalkattāviliruntu veḷivarum mārṭaṉ reviyū eṉṟa tiṅkaḷ itaḻil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatākum.10
tamiḻarkaḷmoḻiyālum, paṇpāṭṭālum, nākarikat tālum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷālum mikavum toṉmiyum, ciṟappum, uṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum avarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻi eḻuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē, ulakil palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi ariya toḻilkaḷiyum uyariya camayattiyum nalla paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum parappiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum iṅku naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
puti kuḻikaḷ
maṉitaṉ āti kālattil vilaṅkukaḷōṭu vilaṅkāy vāḻn tāṉ. avaṉ vilaṅkiṉum koṭiya uyiriṉamāy iḻi ceyalkaḷ aṉittum peṟṟavaṉāy māṟiyatum uṇṭu. avaṉ ātiyil kāykaṉi, kiḻaṅku kīri mutaliyavikaḷit tiṉṟu uyir vāḻum pirāṇiyāyt tōṉṟiṉāṉ. appāl vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavi kaḷiyum koṉṟu tiṉṟu vāḻum vēṭar niliyi eytiṉāṉ. appāl avaṉ taṉṉip pōṉṟa maṉita iṉattavarkaḷik koṉṟu, utirattik kuṭittu ūṉit tiṉṟu uḷam makiḻnta katiyum ulakil uṇṭu. ittakiya koṭiya pēy maṉitarkaḷ iṉṟum cila tīvukaḷil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. ittakiya kuruti veṟi koṇṭa ūṉ cuvi kaṇṭa vilaṅku maṉitarkaḷi mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṉipāl (Cannibals) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya koṭiya maṉitap pēykaḷukku aṟivūṭṭi avarkaḷi maṉitarkaḷākkak karuti avarkaḷ vāḻum tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu camaya aṟivuri kūṟa muyaṉṟa veḷḷik kiṟittavap pātirimār palar avarkaḷukku nalla uṇavāṉatākac cila kiṟittavarkaḷ pēciyatik kēṭṭirukkiṉṟōm; eḻutiyuḷḷatip paṭittirukkiṉṟōm. intiya nāṭṭil kūṭa, cila malikaḷil vāḻum nākarikam aṟṟa makkaḷ, mali aṭivārattil uḷḷa ciṟṟūr kaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ pāl maṇam māṟāp pacciḷaṅ kuḻantikaḷik koṉṟu tiṉṟuḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷi nām ceytittāḷkaḷil paṭittatum uṇṭu. inta maṉita vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vayiṟē, makkaḷiṉ puti kuḻiyāy iruntatu eṉṟu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum puti kuḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi eḻutum āciriyarkaḷum vēṭikkiyāyk kuṟippiṭum niliyil oru kālattil namatu camūkam iruntatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
paṇṭikkāla maṉita iṉam tammōṭotta maṉitarkaḷ iṟantuviṭṭāl, anta uṭali nāyum nariyum, kaḻukum tiṉṉumāṟu aṅkēyē pōṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟa kālamum uṇṭu. nīṇṭa kālattiṟkup piṉpē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭali, maṇṇiṟ kuḻitōṇṭip putikkum vaḻakkatti maṉita camūkam peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. makkaḷ nākarikam peṟṟa kālattil piṇaṅkaḷi maṇṇāl ceyta puti kalaṉkaḷil vittup putikkum vaḻakkattiyum cuṭṭerikkum paḻakkattiyum peṟṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. mikap piṟkālattilētāṉ piṇaṅkaḷip peṭṭikaḷil vittu maṇṇil putikkum vaḻak kattiyum kallaṟi kaṭṭi atil piṇap peṭṭikaḷi vittu aṭakkam ceyyum muṟikaḷiyum maṉita camūkam aṭintatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iṉṟum pāracīka iṉattic cērnta corāṭar camaya makkaḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷi ōr iṭattil vittu, avi aḻuki nāṟṟam eṭuttuk kaḻuku pōṉṟa paṟavikaḷ tiṉṉum paṭi ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.
tāḻipputiyalil tamiḻar nākarikam
paṇṭikkālat tamiḻ iṉamum, avarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya uṟaviṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirāviṭa iṉamum patiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē, kal ūḻiyil vāḻntirukkumpoḻutē piṇaṅ kaḷik kuḷippāṭṭi nallāṭi puṉintu, naṟumaṇam ūṭṭi, aḻutu toḻutu, neyppantam kāṭṭi, oppāri pāṭi, māraṭittu, malar mālikaḷ cūṭṭi, malarmālikaḷ toṅkum capparattil vittut tūkkicceṉṟu putittum, tāḻikaḷil vittup putittum, kallaṟikaṭṭi avaṟṟil vittu mūṭiyum, vāḻntaṉar.
tamiḻiṉattār, putiya kaṟkālattilē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷit tāḻikaḷil vittup putittu vantaṉar eṉpataṟkut teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa puti kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭutta putikalaṉ kaḷē acikka muṭiyāta uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. aṉittiṉum putiya kaṟkālattiṉ toṭakkakāla irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷ ciṟanta cāṉṟu kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa makkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷip putippatil nalla nākarika muṟikaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉar. itaṟkut teṉintiyā peṟṟirunta aintu vitamāṉa putikkum muṟikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷāl pārāṭṭat takkaṉavāy ēṟṟukkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. tāḻiyiliṭṭup putikkum takimi
piṇaṅkaḷit tāḻiyiliṭṭup putikkum cīriya muṟi tamiḻakattil patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iruntu vanta muṟiyākum. itaṉi ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻi kaḷum, ataṉ uḷḷirukkum elumpukaḷum, poṉpaṭṭaṅkaḷum, irumpuk karuvikaḷum, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum, piṟavum uṟutip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. intap putikalaṉkaḷ uṭal muḻuvatum uḷḷaṭaṅ kum muṟiyil periyaṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa. āti kālattil piṇaṅkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷil oru maṉitaṉuṭiya ellā elumpukaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟum evarum kuṟippiṭavilli. cila tāḻikaḷil maṇṭiyōṭukaḷē kāṇappaṭavilli.
niṟka, tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷi vittup putikkum vaḻakkam caṅkakālattil iruntuvantatu. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattil tuñciyati aṟintu aiyūrmuṭavaṉār pāṭiya pāṭalē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ uyir nīttatu aiyūrmuṭavaṉārkkup perum varuttattit tantatu. akkālattil iṟantavarkaḷi oru periya tāḻiyil vittup putippatu vaḻakkam. eṉavē pulavar aiyūrmuṭavaṉār maṉakkaṇṇiṟkuk kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pūta uṭampu pulaṉākātu, pukaḻuṭampu pulaṉāyiṟṟu. tāḻiyāṟ kavippatāyiṉ ap pukaḻuṭampittāṉ kavikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. av vuṭampu nilulaku muḻutum parantu vāṉaḷāva uyarntu tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṟkēṟṟa tāḻi vēṇṭiṉ kuyavaṉ inta nila ulaki āḻiyāka vum mērumaliyi maṇtiraḷākavum koṇṭu periyatoru tāḻi ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. kuyavaṉi nōkki, ivvāṟu orutāḻi ceyya iyalumō eṉa oru pāṭṭip pāṭiyuḷḷār.11
naṉantali mūtūrk kalañcey kōvē
nilavari cūṭṭiya nīṇeṭun tāṉip
pulavar pukaḻnta poyyā nallici
virikatir ñāyiṟu vicumpivarn taṉṉa
cēṇviḷaṅku ciṟappiṟ cempiyar marukaṉ
koṭi nuṭaṅku yāṉi neṭumā vaḷavaṉ
tēva rulaka meytiṉa ṉātaliṉ
aṉṉōṟ kavikkuṅ kaṇṇakaṉ ṟāḻi
vaṉital vēṭṭaṉi yāyi ṉeṉiyatūum
irunilan tikiriyāp perumali
maṇṇā vaṉita lollumō niṉakkē	(puṟam 228. 4-15.)
puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷa maṟṟoru pāṭṭil
curampala vanta emakkum aruḷi
viyaṉmala rakaṉpoḻil īmat tāḻi
akalitāka vaṉimō	(puṟam 256-4-6)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 238, 364 ām pāṭalkaḷ tāḻiyip paṟṟik kūṟuvataṉ mūlam caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattilum tāḻiyil vittup piṇaṅkaḷip putittu varuvatu tamiḻar marapu eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.13
2. kālkaḷ uḷḷa putikalaṉkaḷ
kālkaḷiyuṭiya tāḻikaḷ ceṉṉiyilē pala kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala elumpukaḷuṭaṉum piṟa poruḷ kaḷuṭaṉum kāṇappeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil peritum ciṟitum uḷḷaṉa. mikapperiyatu āṟuaṭi allatu ataṟku mēlum uḷḷatāy nīḷa vaṭṭavaṭivil uḷḷatu. mikac ciṟiyatu nāṉku kālkaḷōṭu nāṟ kōṇa vaṭivil uḷḷatu. ivikaḷellām mūṭikaḷiyuṭiyaṉa. nām mīṇṭum, mukkālum, nāṟkālum uṭiya putikalaṉkaḷum uḷa eṉṟu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭṭatāka vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. ivi vaṭa āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭattiṟkē ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum.
3. akaḻnteṭutta kukic camāti
kuṭakkallu allatu kuṭikal, kallaṟikaḷ namatu nāṭṭil cila uḷa. ivi vaṭṭavaṭimāṉa aṟiyāka uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu nālu aṭi āḻamum āṟumutal 8 aṭi kuṟukkaḷavum uḷḷatāyp pāṟi yil ceṅkuttāyt tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mītu mūṭipōl maṟikkum pāvukaṟkaḷumuḷa. intak kallaṟip putiyalkaḷ, tāḻipputiyalkaḷ ākiyavikaḷukkum kalmēṭi (Dolmens) kaḷukkum iṭiyē toṭarpukaḷ kāṇamuṭiyum.
4. akaḻntu kaṇṭa ariya camāti aṟi
intac camātikaḷ malapāril uḷḷa tuḷi (Tholi) eṉpatōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉa. intac ceṅkōṇa vaṭivil veṭṭappaṭṭa pāṟiyil uḷḷa camātikaḷ, mēlē tiṟanta kūriyiyuṭiyaṉa. mēṟpakuti katavukaḷ pōl mūṭak kūṭiyatu. intac camātikaḷ paṇṭiya kirīṭṭil kūṭa iruntaṉa. kirīṭtīvil kaṟkaḷi aṭip paṭiyāka vittuc ceṅkalliṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷi yuṭiya nākarikamāṉa camātikkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. kēraḷa nāṭṭiluḷḷa tirāviṭamakkaḷ toḷi (tuvāram) eṉappaṭum puti kuḻiyil iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷiyiṭṭup putikki ṟārkaḷ. ivi tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟu akaḻ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum kirēttar (cret) makkaḷiṉ miṉōṉ kālattuc camātikaḷippōl uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa irumpu vāḷkaḷ, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷ, mutti rikaḷ pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kirēttar makkaḷ talip pakutiyil iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷāl virumpap peṟṟa poruḷkaḷi vittup putittu vantaṉar.
5. kallaṟip piṇakkuḻi
kallaṟip putiyalkaḷ : nilatti akaḻntu, aṭiyil vaṭṭa mākak kaṟkaḷi aṭukki vittu naṭuvil piṇaṅkaḷi vittup pala camayac caṭaṅkukaḷic ceytu putippatu tamiḻ marapu vaḻakkam. inta marapu vaḻakkam teṉ intiyāviviṭa takkāṇattil peritum paraviyuḷḷatu. aitarāpāttil uḷḷa mulā ali eṉṉum iṭattil uḷḷa putikuḻiyil eḻil peṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. intap piṇakkuḻiyil mikapperiyatu orē nērattil irupatu pēr nērāka niṟkak kūṭiyatāy uyarntatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa kaccalakoṇṭāvil uḷḷa oru putikuḻiyiṉ varipaṭam tarappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kāṇka.
piṇaṅkaḷip putik kum muṟiyil intiyā eṅkum orumippāṭu nilaviyatākak kāṇamuṭiyavilli. āṉāl, cuṭumuṟi pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ki.pi. iraṇ ṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟ paṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭum aim peruṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉ ṟāṉa maṇimēkaliyil piṇac caṭaṅku muṟi aintu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. anta aintil oṉṟu cuṭutal maṟṟoṉṟu piṇaṅ kaḷik kaḻivu nilaṅkaḷil vīci viṭuvatākum. itu putippati lum cuṭuvatilum aṭaṅkāta oru taṉimuṟiyākum. itaṉi iṭuvōrmuṟi yeṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. inta iraṇṭu muṟikaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷukkup putiya muṟiyeṉṟu terikiṟatu.
āriyarkaḷ inta nāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ innāṭṭil kaṇṭa putiya muṟi cuṭutal eṉpatākum. āriyarkaḷ innāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ eḻunta muṟikaḷ eñciya mūṉṟu muṟikaḷum ākum. ivi teṉ intiyat tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya muṟiyākum. āḻak kuḻiyil putippōr, toṭukuḻiyil iṭuvōr cātāraṇamāka kaṟpalakikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭa kallaṟikaḷil putippōr, kallaṟikkuḷ tāḻikaḷil uṭalivittup putippōr, tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tulakkamākak kūṟappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. itu paḻaṅkālap putiyal muṟiyit toṭarntu kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkakāla nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟimukap paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa putiya muṟikaḷ nilavum vari toṭarntu vantuḷḷatu eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṟatu.13
iṉṟu piṇaṅkaḷic cuṭukāṭṭil koṇṭupōy viṟaku kaḷi aṭiyil aṭukkivittu, mēlē cāṇa erumūṭṭikaḷi aṭukki ataṉmītu piṇatti vittu vikkōlip pōṭṭu maṇcānti ataṉmēṟ pōṭṭu meḻuki, mēlum vikkōlip pōṭṭu maṇcāntāl pūcit talippuṟam kālpuṟam iru vilāppuṟam mēṟpuṟam ākiyavaṟṟil tuḷiyiṭṭu neruppi (koḷḷik kaṭṭiyi) vippar. ippaṭic ceyvatāl uḷḷē irukkum piṇamum kaṭṭikaḷum erintu cāmpalāy viṭukiṉṟaṉa. elumpukaḷ maṭṭumē eñcikkiṭakkum. maṟunāḷ kāṭāṟṟa eṉṟa caṭaṅkum, patiṉāṟām nāḷ kal eṭuppu eṉṟa caṭaṅkum naṭi peṟum. iḵtu iṉṟum tamiḻarkaḷil maruta nilattavarkaḷāṉa vēḷāḷarkaḷiṭamum an nilattil tōṉṟiya toḻilāḷarkaḷāṉa taṭṭār (poṟ kollar) kollar, kaltaccar, marattaccar, kaṉṉār, eṇṇey vaṇikar, pāli nilamaṟavar, mullinila āyar pōṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṭamum vaḻakkil iruntu varukiṟatu.14

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VIII
1.	“The recent discoveries in Sindhu and Baluchistan prove that he cutural affinities of the Dravidians extend in an unbroken line from the Tinnevelly District (Adiththanalloor) in the extreme of Indian Peninsula through Sindhu and Baluchistan the island Bahrein in the persian gulf, South Persia, Mesopotamia and crete and some of the islands of the Eastern Mediterranean” -Dra vidian Civilization - R.D. Banerji, Calcutta, 1927. (Modern Review) p 308.
2.	These Lanarkes were found just above what Myres calls the Double-urn interments, what Rea called Pyriform tombs and Evans calls jar Burials in Crete are the same as the kettle burials of Mesopotamia. In Southern India these jars covered with a terra -cottah lid and in some cases with a stone - In the Northern India two of them were found with stone covers at Mohenjodaro in 1922-23 and in the chotta Nagpur Districts they are invariably placed under larger or small stone covers.
	“They therefore form a distinct part of the system of jar burial of the copper age and along with them were found some beautiful bronze chariot wheels similar to the pottery wheels discovered at Harappa and Mohenjodaro” Dravidian Civilization by Prof. Ragal Doss Banerji M.A., (2 articles in Modern Review Calcutta) 1927, p.555.
3.	The remins of the people of the copper and bronze Ages described above are very definitely connected with the bronze Age culture of Mesopotamia, central Asia, Asia Minor and the lands of the Eastern mediterranean archipelago specially that of crete. The cist Burial and Jar Burials of Mohenjo-daro, Nal and Harappa connected as they are with the cists, Larnakes jars etc., of South India, towards the South - east, are also intimately connected with the Burial customs of Mekran South Persia, Mesopotamin and the Mediterraneen people. The nearest specimens are to be found in the Isand of Bahrin and neer Bushire, in Persia, Sir William Ousely discovered a prehistoric cemetery of this type near Bashire consisting of jars with pointed ends contining human bones and covered with a shallow earthern vessel - Indian Antiquary Vol. VIII p.166 (Modern Review Nov. 1927 - p. 556).
4.	Indian Antiquary Vol. IV 1930. (London) 1930 - p.13.
5.	Anderson catalogue and Hand Book; p. 393-462.
6.	Annual Report of the Archelogical survey 1908-09 pp.60-78.
7.	Cambridge Ancient History (London) 1948-Vol.I p.377.
8.	Ibid p. 592.
9.	Ibid pp. 596-597.
10.	“Thus the Burial custom which we have found in all its different forms in Southern India and in isolated cases in Northern India, Persia, Mesopotamia, Asia Minor are to be found in a complete series in Crete. It is the continuety and final culmination which led me to accept the very brilliant suggestion of Dr.Suniti kumar Chatterji that the brilliant terms “Tamil, Damil, Dravida” were really the same as the Termli of Herodotus and the ”Trimili” of old Lycian inscriptions. Now the Archacological evidence is much stronger” - “Modern Review” (Calcutta) December 1924 Vol.XLII.
11.	puṟam. 228 : 4-15.
12.	puṟam. 256 : 4-6.
13.	Attention should consentrated on a view that the cremation was primarily a vedic rite but even after it was introduced into Southern India, the other methods were also practised. Another method of exposure of the body has thus been commended upon Atharvana Samhitha XVIII, 2-34 mentions among the pitrs invoked in the Pindapitry Ajina (offering of balls of rice to the Manes) the paroptas those abandened in distant places and the liddhitas (those exposed on elevated localities) Exposure of the dead persist to-day in a modified form among the Tebateans and parsis-Yuan Chwang, the chinese Pilgrime mentions this as one of the three recognizied methods of disposal of the dead in India.
	The Editor of Puducottah Manual says that the period to which any of the burials under consideration belongs can only be determined by the nature of the associated finds. In cases where only Neoliteic implements are found without any iron or bronze belong the prehistoric Age. Where urn and megalithic burials contin an abundance of iron implements and vessels and no neoliths, these may be sid to belong at the earliest to the Iron Age that succeeded the Neolithic Age and continued into historic times. See my paper on the disposal of the dead in S. India in the proceedings of Tenth All India Oriental Conference pp. 530-533 (Origin and spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar, M.A., (Madras) 1971, (Notes to lecture I p. 86 : 31).
