9. makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ
āyvut toṭakkam
1876ām āṇṭum ati aṭuttu, 1903 ām āṇṭum iṟuti yāka 1905ām āṇṭu ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil ṭākṭar cākōr, lāppikyū, iriyā pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ taliyōṭukaḷ palavaṟṟi kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷaṉar. appāl avi taliyōṭṭu āyvuttuṟi vallunarkaḷiṉ āyviṟku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷiṟ pala tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu ātirēliyap  paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭṭip pōṉ ṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
1962ām āṇṭil varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakāla maṉitaṉiṉ, makkippōṉa maṇṭiyōṭukaḷum elumpukaḷum pilippiṉic cērnta pālavāṉ tīvil oru kukiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu pilippiṉ tēcīyat tolporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil (Museum) cērkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
10 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa paḻaṅkālattil pōrṉiyō allatu malēciyat tīvukaḷiliruntu vanta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ cila pilippiṉil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatākap pilippiṉ arumporuṭkāṭciccālit talivar ṭākṭar irāparṭapāk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭik kālattil vāḻntu vanta makkaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷ cila cīṉā, intōṉēciyā pōṉṟa āciyāk kaṇṭattil ēṟkeṉavē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippoḻutu cappāṉi lum cila paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ taliyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
itit toṭarntu pala nāṭukaḷil paḻaṅkālattil vāḻn tiṟanta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭut tali yōṭṭārāycci vallunarkaḷāl (Craniologists) toṭarntu āyvu  ceyyappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.1 iṉṟu pala nāṭukaḷilum akaḻ āyvu ceyyappeṟṟu maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭu taliyōṭ ṭāyvu vallunarkaḷāl āyntu pala viyakkattaku uṇmikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟu ulakiṟku veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala nāṭukaḷilum pala tīvukaḷilum mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ taliyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatākap paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pala nāḷ itaḻkaḷil ceytikaḷ veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. aṭikkaṭi nūlkaḷilum pattirikikaḷilum taliyōṭu kaḷum atip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum veḷi varuvati aṟinta aṟiñar oruvar itu taliyōṭṭuk kālam (age of craniology) eṉṟār. atu poruttamāṉa ceytiyēyākum.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu eṭutta taliyōṭukaḷ cila ceṉṉi arumporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil (musuem) naṉṟākap pātukāttu vikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. V‹?- avikaḷil cila iṅkilāntiṟkāka āyviṉ poruṭṭuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭum tirumpi vantu cērntuḷḷaṉa. arum poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa taliyōṭukaḷ aḷavu aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
	taliyōṭṭiṉ	nīḷam	akalam	perukka 
		ce.mī.	ce.mī	aṭukkuk kuṟi
	1.	18.8	12.4	66.0
	2.	19.1	12.7	66.5
	3.	18.3	12.4	67.8
	4.	18	12.2	67.8
	5.	18	12.8	77.1
	6.	16.8	12.1	78.0
ivi tiru. a. iriyā avarkaḷ eḻutiya ātittanallūrilum perumpāyūrilumiruntu kiṭitta varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku mukkiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil viḷakkantarap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
taliyōṭṭu āyvu
1900ām āṇṭu alekcāntar iriyā avarkaḷ, ceṉṉi mānilat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟiyiṉ ātaravil ātitta nallūrp paṟampil āyvu naṭattiṉār. avaratu āyvil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa taliyōṭukaḷ ākum.
1909ām āṇṭu ceṉṉit tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyic cērnta aṟiñar tarṭaṉiṭam ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭitta taliyōṭukaḷ oppuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. avar āyntu veḷiyiṭṭa teṉ intiyak kulaṅkaḷum nākarika maṟṟa iṉak kuḻukkaḷum (Castes and tribes of Southern India) eṉṟa nūliṉ mutal tokutiyil oru viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār. atil avar, iraṇṭu taliyōṭukaḷ eṉatu karutti īrttuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu miyanilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷil (Mediterranean) uḷḷa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ pōṉṟatu - atu tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭu, maṟṟatu ātirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āṉāl, ciṟappumikka ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭu āyvil kaṇṭeṭutta taliyōṭukaḷ paṟṟi, avaratu taṉi nūlkaḷ varalām eṉṟu ulakam etirpārttatu; vara villi. 1903-1904ām āṇṭukaḷil pireñcu nāṭṭic cērnta pēraṟiñar, em. lūyi lāppikyū, mīṇṭum ōr akaḻ āyvu naṭatti ṉār allavā? ataṉ payaṉāka mēlum pala ariya poruḷkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟi avar naṉku āyntu ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil akappaṭṭa aṉittup poruḷkaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkku uriyaṉa eṉṟu ōr uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu koṇṭār.
1899ām āṇṭu mutal 1905ām āṇṭuvari ippakuti
kaḷi alekcāntar iriyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu cumār 2000kku mēṟpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi eṭuttu mika nuṇukkamākat turuvit turuvi āyvu naṭattiṉār. avaratu āyvil inta iṭukāṭu ōr arumperum paḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya uyariya karuvūlam eṉpati naṉku kaṇṭār. iṅkuk kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷ muḻuvatum kiṟittava ūḻi toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭavikaḷāy irukkavēṇṭum. avi pāṇṭiyaṉ kālat toṭakkatti uṭiyavikaḷāy kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum karutiṉār. ṭākṭar ci. maklīṉ (Dr.C.Maclean), ātittanallūr, iṭukāṭṭil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷum ataṉuḷ irunta poruḷ kaḷum paḻantirāviṭarkaḷ kālattil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām. allatu avarkaḷ kālattiṟkum muṉṉuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉār. pērāciriyar. ji.eliyaṭ mit (Prof. G. Elliot smith) eṉpavar, alekcāntar iriyā intap paḻamporuḷkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟiyatu, avaratu contak karuttukkaḷ eṉṟum atu naṉṟāka alaci āyvu ceyyātu koṇṭa muṭivu eṉṟum karutip piṉvarum karuttuk kaḷik kūṟiṉār. nammiṭam maṟukka muṭiyāta cāṉṟukaḷ illi; āṉāl, kālattip paṟṟi cūḻnilikkēṟṟa poruttaṅkāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ tām uḷa. avikaḷip palvēṟu vakiyākac ceyal paṭuttik kāṭṭamuṭiyum. uttēcamāka ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ kālam 400 āṇṭu mutal 4000-m āṇṭu vari irukkalām. evarum itit tavaṟu eṉṟu eṇpikkavō allatu vaṟpuṟuttavō muṭiyātu. āṉāl, inta iṭam paḻam pāṇṭiyar kālattic cērntatākak karutappaṭattakka cāttiyakkūṟu kaḷi uṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu kuṟintatu, kiṟittava ūḻi toṭaṅkuvataṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uḷḷatāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉār.2
pērāciriyar ji. eliyaṭ mit avarkaḷukku aṉuppap peṟṟa iraṇṭu taliyōṭukaḷiyum avar naṉku āyntu avaratu ciṟappumikka, maṉitaṉuṭiya pariṇāmattip paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷ (1927) eṉṟa nūliṉ iraṇṭāvatu patippil oru viḷakkam tantuḷ ḷār.3 atil ātittanallūril kaṇṭeṭutta taliyōṭukaḷ iraṇṭum, vevvēṟu maṉita iṉattic cērnta taliyōṭukaḷi ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu, ātirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkaḷiṉ taliyōṭṭi yottirukkiṟatu. maṟṟatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa miya nilakkaṭaṟkarip pakutiyil vāḻnta paḻiya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ taliyōṭṭi yottirukkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. aṭikkuṟippil miyanilak kaṭaṟkarip pakuti (Mediterranean) makkaḷ iṉam eṉpataṟkut tirāviṭa iṉam eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār.4
inta irumaṇṭiyōṭukaḷum vevvēṟu iṉattiṟkuriya taliyōṭukaḷāka iruntālum avi iraṇṭum orē kulattil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷiṉ taliyōṭukaḷ allaveṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉṟāl ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa vētakāla nūlkaḷil niṣātar, kirātar pōṉṟa āriyarkaḷallāta makkaḷ intiyāvil vāḻntaṉar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatiliruntu aṟintu koḷḷalām.
iṉappirivu
intiya makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi im makkaḷiṉam āṟu periya iṉattokutikaḷiṉiṉṟum avaṟṟiṉ uṭpirivukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantavikaḷākum. āṟu periya iṉattokutikaḷ eṉpatu aṭiyil varumāṟu :
(1) nekkiriṭṭō (2) āti ātirālāyṭu (3) maṅkōlāyṭu (4) miya nilakkaṭaṟkari iṉam (ataṉ uṭpirivāṉa ātimiya nilakkaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷiluḷḷa iṉamum kīḻnāṭṭu iṉamum) (5) akaṉṟa maṇṭiyōṭṭu iṉamum (ataṉ uṭpirivukaḷāṉa tiṉārik karmiṉāyṭup pirivukaḷum nārṭik iṉamum) ākum.
nekriṭṭō āti ātirālāyṭu, maṅkōlāyṭu mutaliya iṉaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ uṭpirivukaḷum intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātik kuṭimakkaḷēyāvar. nekriṭṭōkkaḷ kuḷḷa vaṭivamum ciṟiya taliyum, veḷippuṟam taḷḷiya neṟṟiyum vaḷarcciyuṟāta mukavāyum pammeṉṟa tali mayirum uṭiyavarkaḷ. intat tōṟṟam intiyāviluḷḷa kāṭarkaḷ, iruḷarkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ātirālāyṭu iṉavaki mēṟkūṟiya nekriṭṭōk kaḷip peritum ottirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷuṭiya uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟirukkum. talimayir pammeṉṟu curuṇṭirāmal vaḷintu curuṇṭuḷḷatu. teṉ intiyāvilum miya intiyāvi lum uḷḷa ātik kuṭimakkaḷuḷ perumpāṉmiyōr ivvaki yiṉarēyāvar. mikattoṉmiyāṉa kālattilē, ātirālāyṭu makkaḷum, miya nilak kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷum oṉṟittu vāḻntu utirak kalappu uṭiyavarkaḷāyviṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu ātirālāyṭu makkaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷum kalan tuḷḷaṉar eṉpataṟkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṟiñar, akli (Huxly) eṉpār muṟkālattilēyē āti rēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷōṭu uṟavu koṇṭirun taṉar eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkuriya cāttiyak kūṟukaḷ uḷaveṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar maṉita iṉavaki vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum, toṭarpu kaḷiyum, paṇpāṭukaḷiyum paṟṟiya āyviyal muṟikaḷum payaṉkaḷum (The Methods and Results of Ethnology) eṉṟu 1865 ām āṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭa kaṭṭuri oṉṟil, maṉita iṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikac ciṟanta - avaratu vakippaṭuttum muṟi, aṟimukap paṭuttappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. atil avar, ātirēliyarkaḷ oru kalappaṟṟa iṉam pōṉṟatākak kūṟuvatu, teṟku intutāṉiṉ (Hindustan) tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ kavaṉatti īrttuḷḷatu. eṉavē, avarkaḷ āttirēli yarkaḷiṭattil uṭal cārnta niliyilum kuṟiyā aḷavil, nila iyal cārnta niliyilum nammip piṉṉōkkic cella vaḻi kāṭṭi viṭṭārkaḷ eṉpati viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avar piṉṉar 1869 ām āṇṭil eḻutiya kaṭṭuri oṉṟil, intiyap putirkaḷi mika viḷakkamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atil takkāṇattu makkaḷip paṟṟi viḷakkik kāṭṭum poḻutu, avarkaḷ nīṇṭa taliyum karumiyāṉa tōlum kaṉṉaṅkariya viḻi kaḷum, kaṟuttu neḷivāṉa tali mayirum uḷḷavarkaḷ. evvitamāṉa viruppamum iṉṟit talimayiri maḻittuk koḷvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, tirāviṭarkaḷ, mutirāp paṇpuṭiya mutumakkaḷāy vāḻnta kālattil pēcappeṟṟa moḻiyip pēci vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ iṉṉum intutāṉattiṉ vaṭamēṟku, vaṭa kiḻakkup pakutikaḷil paḻantirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷ eṉṟu collat takka muṟikaḷil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum avar 1870 ām āṇṭil, nila iyaliṉ pirippēṭṭil maṉita iṉattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa tiruntiya vaṭivam (The Geographical Distribution of the chief Modification of mankind) eṉṟa kaṭṭuriyil entap putiya cāṉṟukaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭātu tammuṭiya paḻiya karuttukkaḷi mīṇṭum valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār. atōṭu avaratu kaṭṭuriyil, paṇṭikkāla ekiptiyarkaḷ kūṭa ātiralāyṭu iṉattavarkaḷiṉ kalappuṭiyavarkaḷē yākum eṉṟu melaṉō koray (Melonochroi) eṉṟa kaṟuppu airōpya iṉamum, ācuttira lāyṭu, cāntōkurōṭ (Australoid - xanthochrod) nōrṭi (Nordi) kalappil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē eṉṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. aṟiñar akliyiṉ kūṟṟuppaṭi āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakka ḷum, tirāviṭarkaḷum mikap paḻami vāynta puṉitamāṉa makkaḷiṉ cantatikaḷēyāvar. avarkaḷiṉ mayil vaṇṇa niṟattiyum, uṭal uyarvaḷavu pōṉṟavikaḷil mēleḻunta vāriyāṉa oppuṭimi kaḷiyum kavaṉikkumpaṭi kūṟiyuḷḷār. āttiralāyṭukaḷukkum tirāviṭarkaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa tōṟṟa orumippāṭukaḷ paṟṟi akliyiṉ karuttukkaḷ nalla poruḷ potinta naṉṉōkku ēṟpuṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
aṟiñar ṭōppiṉārṭu (Topinard) 1894 ām āṇṭil āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, nīkkirōvarkaḷum melliya mayirkaḷuṭiya, āṭṭōcṉōvā (Autochtonoes) iṉamum kalanta ōr iṉam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. akli, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa nīkrōvarkaḷuṭaṉ teṉṉintiyarkaḷāṉa kākkēciya melaṉōkurā (Caucasian Melanochro) miyanilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷ iṉamum kalantuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. aṟiñar āṭṭaṉ (Hotton) teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷil āttirēliya iṉamum piṟa iṉamum kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. itip paṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ palappala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi makkaḷik kuḻappiviṭṭālum uṟutiyāka ātittanallūril kiṭitta iraṇṭu maṇṭiyōṭukaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ eṉpatil aiyam eḻak kāraṇam etuvum illi.
aṟiñar cakkarmēṉ ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa taliyōṭukaḷil eṇ 1. teṉṉintiyāvil toṉṟu toṭṭu vāḻntuvarum paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ iṉṟiya ātirēliyat tiṇip piṟappurimi yāḷarkaḷiṉ paṇpottavarkaḷ. eṉavē aṟiñar akli ācuttirēliyar - tirāviṭar iṉa uṟavip paṟṟi aḷitta poruḷ viḷakkam ēṟpuṭiyatākum. eṉpatu aṟiñar palariṉ muṭipāka irukkiṟatu. atu iṉṟu teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil vāḻum vēṭar cakkāy (Saki) pōṉṟavarkaḷiyum, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkik koṇ ṭirukkum tirāviṭarkaḷukku muṉṉiruntatākak karutappaṭum ōr iṉattavar iruntaṉar eṉpatu oru kōṭpāṭu; antak kōṭpāṭṭi itu uṟutippaṭuttuvatāka irukkiṟatu. taliyōṭṭiṉ cāṉṟu māṟṟuvakiyāṉa karuttukkaḷ etiyum ātarikkavilli. ippoḻutuḷḷa āttirēliyak kuṭimakkaḷ ākkiramippāḷarka ḷāṉa intiyarkaḷukkum ācuttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukkum iṭiyē eḻunta kalappiṉ viḷivākavē irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu muṭivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār.5 mīṇṭum mīṇṭum pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi avaratu karuttu valiyuṟuttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṭṭil teṉ intiyāvē
taliyōṭukaḷi maṭṭum vittu iṉavēṟupāṭukaḷip paṟṟi iṟutiyāṉa muṭipu kāṇpatu aritu. taliyōṭṭu āyvu vallunarkaḷ, taliyōṭukaḷi mūṉṟu pirivākap pirittu āyn tuḷḷārkaḷ. oṉṟu nīṇṭa taliyōṭukaḷuḷḷa iyaṟkūṟukaḷi yuṭiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu maṭṭamāṉa uṭal uyarvuḷḷatu. iṉ ṉoṉṟu neṭṭiyāṉa uṭal uyarvuḷḷatōṭu iyaṟkūṟu muḷḷatu. atumaṭṭumiṉṟi uyarnta maṇṭiyōṭṭiṟkuriya kaviki mōṭupōṉṟa eṭuppāṉa tōṟṟamuḷḷa mūkkum uṭiyatu. ivi cārkōṉikku, mecapottāmiyā kālattiṟku muṉ irunta tākak karutappaṭukiṟa al upit (Al-ubid) kiṣ (Kish) mutaliya pakuti kaḷil uḷḷa iṉattoṭarpāṉa marapuk kūṟukaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya oppuṭiyaṉa. itumaṭṭumiṉṟi varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya taliyōṭṭu āyvuttuṟi (Craniology)Æ‹ nuṇ āyvu, mokañcatārō (cintuveḷi) āttirēliya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōr kaḷic cārnta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ mātiriyāka ātitta nallūriliruntu kaṇṭeṭutta maṇṭi yōṭukaḷ pōṉṟum iṉṟiya vēṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭukaḷ pōṉṟum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
eliyaṭ mit (Elliot Smith) kūṟṟuppaṭi, ātitta nallūril kaṇṭa maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ, paṇṭiya ekiptiya makkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇamuṭiyātatākum. teṉ intiyak kāṭukaḷil vāḻum ūrāḷikaḷ, kāṭar pōṉṟa iṉatta varkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa karuppuniṟa iṉattavarkaḷuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya kurutit toṭarpu koṇṭa varkaḷ eṉpatu iṉṟiya aṟiviyal kōṭpāṭākum avarkaḷ āttirēliya makkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷākat tōṉṟip pala talimuṟikaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. appāl avarkaḷ miya nilak kaṭal cārnta makkaḷāyt tōṉṟit tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itippaṟṟi namatu uḷamārnta koḷki, vēṟu vakiyāka irukkiṟatu. potuvāka intiyāviṉuḷ - ciṟappākat teṉ intiyāviṉuḷ - ēṉ? miya nilak kaṭal cārnta iṉattavarkaḷ uṭpukuntatāka ārāyap pukāmalē muṉṉatākavē meyyeṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum? maṇṇiyal kālaṅkaḷiliruntu teṉṉintiyāvil makkaḷ iṉam vāḻntiruntāl, paḻaṅkal ūḻi makkaḷum putuk kal ūḻi makkaḷum nīkrō mātiri uruvil vāḻntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyirukka vēṇṭum inta iṉattavarkaḷ putuk kal ūḻiyil ācuttirēliya makkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷuṭaṉ kalappuṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu vivātam ceyyalām. āttirēliya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭataṟkāka oru vēḷi intak kalappē poṟuppuṭiya tāka irukkalām eṉṟu kūṟalām. ivvāṟāka makkaḷiṉ oruṅku kalattal ūḻi ūḻiyākap pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē paraviyirukka vēṇṭum.
tamiḻarkaḷ, tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷallar
ātikālattil, teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟi aṅku vāḻntu varum makkaḷip paṟṟiya koḷki teḷivāṉatu; uṟutiyāṉatu. tamiḻarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷum teṉṉintiyāvil tōṉṟi, ūḻi ūḻikālam aṅkēyē uṟintu varupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ paḻaṅ kal ūḻi toṭṭu, putiya kal ūḻi, ulōka ūḻi pōṉṟa ūḻik kālaṅkaḷik kaṭantu varalāṟṟuk kālamvari - iṉṟuvari vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka, pala talimuṟikaḷāka vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ, avarkaḷiṟ cilar, tamiḻakattil paḻaṅkālattil eḻunta kaṭaṟkōḷkaḷukku añciyō piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālō miyanilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷukkō, ekiptu nāṭṭiṟkō, amerikkā, kirīṭ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkō ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi irukkalām. mīṇṭum, nila naṭukkattālō āṭcik koṭumiyālō cintuveḷikku vantiruk kalām. tāynāṭu tirumpiya tamiḻmakkaḷ, cintuveḷikku vantu tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyaric cūṭṭiyirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar palar karuttu. namatu muṭipum atuvē. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟi nipuṇarum varalāṟṟuttuṟi vallunarumāṉa tiru vi.ār. irāmac cantira tīṭcitar karuttum atuvē.6 iṅku ati mīṇṭum vali yuṟutti eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
intak karuttukkaḷip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ (Prof. Langdon), muṉivar ji.ār. aṇṭar, (Dr. G.R. Hunter) ec.ār.hāl (H.R. Hall) mutaliyavarkaḷ tam nūlkaḷil teḷivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. cintuveḷi nākarikam veḷivantapiṉ atip pōṉṟa nākarikam cumēriya nāṭṭilum nilaviyati aṟintu cumēriyarkaḷē cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟi nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amittu ulakil ōr uyariya paṇpāṭṭi uruvākkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭārkaḷ. atu avacarappaṭṭuc ceyta muṭipākum. tamiḻar teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟi, tamuḷar, timilar, tirāviṭar eṉṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉō allatu vēṟu peyarkaḷuṭaṉō ekiptu ellam, alupit, cumēriyā, kirēttā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭi yēṟi payirttoḻil ceytu, poruḷ īṭṭi, kittoḻil kaḷukkuk kālkōli, nāṭṭi vaḷampeṟac ceytu, paṇpāṭṭip paṭittu, camayamvaḷarttu vāḻviṟku vaḻikāṭṭiṉar. nāṭṭiṟkup poṉṟāy polivūṭṭiṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷum miyanilak kaṭaṟkarippakuti makkaḷum
makkaḷ iṉattik kuṟittu eḻunta āḻnta āyviṉ payaṉāka vaṭakkē vāḻnta iṉattavarkaḷum miyanilak kaṭaṟkarip pakuti kaḷil uṟinta makkaḷum teṉ intiyā viliruntu nīr vaḻiyākavum nilavaḻiyākavum alupitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷēyāvar. kiḻakkuk kari kaḷil paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi ettuṇi āyiram āṇṭuka ḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyavi eṉṟu collamuṭiyāta paḻami vāyntaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta periya iṉattavar tamatu uyarnta aṟiviyum ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷiyum cīriya tōṟṟattiyum vaccira vaṭivattiyum ulaka muḻuvatilum parappiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu uruciya aṟiñar kontaret tōv teḷivāka eṭuttakkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
cumēriyarkaḷ tōṟṟattilum aṟivilum paṇ pāṭṭilum nākarikattilum tirāviṭarkaḷi ottavarkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ allatu tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṭal vaḻiyākavum nilavaḻiyākavum upitiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi mella mella ellattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkut toḻil vaḷarttu nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭiyiṭṭu, appāl cumēriya nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu ṭikkiricu yūppiraṭṭicu āṟṟaṅkarikaḷil kuṭi yēṟi, teṉ intiyāvil taṇporuni āṟṟaṅkariyil aṭip paṭiyiṭṭa ākkantarum ariya paṇpāṭṭi, yūppiraṭṭicu, ṭik kiricu āṟṟaṅkarikaḷilum nīla āṟṟaṅkariyilum kaṭṭivaḷarttu varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa makkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷē aṅkiruntu mīṇṭum taṅkaḷiṉ tanti nāṭāṉa intiyāviṟku vantu vaḷañceṟinta cintu āṟṟal kariyil kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
ayalnāṭukaḷil tamiḻar kuṭiyēṟṟam
varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷum māṉiṭiyal vallunarkaḷum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi aṟiñarkaḷum teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷē. paḻaṅkallūḻi toṭṭu varalāṟṟuk kālam nikaḻum iṉṟuvari intiyā yamuṉi cintu āṟṟiṟku appālvari, vaṭamēṟku intiyā muḻuvatum imayamali aṭivāram vari ceṉṟu payirt toḻil vaḷartta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ eṉṟu muṭipukaṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. annāṭṭiṉiṉṟu cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit teṉ intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpataṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ aṇuvaḷavum illi. atu cila mēṉāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa kaṟpaṉik kati yākum. teṉintiyāvilum vaṭintiyāvilum vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ pāracīkam, īrākku, ekiptu, kirēttā, cippiracu pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi nākarikatti vaḷarttuḷḷa ṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar kāḷitācu nāk kūṟiyuḷḷār.8
cintuveḷiyil malarnta māperum nākarikattil vīṭukaḷiṉ amippukaḷiyum muttirikaḷiṉ ceynērttiyiyum maṇpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṉivuc ciṟappiyum nāṭṭiyamāṭum naṅkiyiṉ veṇ kalap paṭimattiṉ vārppu vaṉappiyum nōkkiṉāl nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ pāpilōṉi yarkaḷi viṭa uyarnta nili aṭintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. intiya nākarikattiṉ iṟutiyil pāpilōṉiya nākarikam tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭātā? intiya nākarikamē cumēriya nākarikamāka malarntuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṇṇuvatum eḷḷaḷavum iḻukkilli eṉṟu pēraṟiñar kōrṭaṉ cilṭu eṉpār viṉāvi cintuveḷi nākarikamē ulakil uḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷukkellām muntiya nākarikam. ulakiṉ eḻunta ellā nākarikaṅkaḷukkum tāy nākarikam; oppaṟṟa uyarnta nākarikam eṉṟu pukaḻmāli cūṭṭiyuḷḷār. ataṉi viḷakkit tamnūlil vi.ār.irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
IX
1.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallore and Perumbir by Alexandar Rea F.S.A. Scott (Madras 1915).
2.	The Adichanallur skulls (Bulletin of the Madras Govt. Museum) by Zuckerman, M.R.C.S., L.R.C.
3.	Essays on the evolution of Man by Prof. G. Elliot Smith (London) 1927.
	These two skulls conform to different racial types. One pe. i figs 1, 2 iii 7 of the present paper of them is clearly and unmistakably proto-Australian in type and the second one (pe ii figs 4,5 : iii 6) conforms more nearly to the racial type that is known as Mediterranean.
4.	“F.N.” It is not pure Mediterraneantype the breadth of the cranium and the flattening of the occiput suggesting the possibility that it may be an example of the type. I have called “Maritime Armenold” a branch of the Alphine Race that is found as one of the ingredients of the racial mixture known as Dravidian” - G. Elliot Smith.
5.	The Adichanallur skulls by S. Zuckerman M.A., M.R.C.S., L.R.C.P. London (Madras) 1930 p.19.
	“Regarded on its face value however Adichanallur No.1 provides support for the view that the oboriginal inhabitant of Southern India was similar to the present day Australian Native. It therefore upholds Huxleys’ interpretation on the Australian-Dravidian relationship which if embodied to-day in the conception of a Proto-Dravidian racial stock comprising the Australians the Saki, the veddahs and the jungle tribes of Southern India. The evidence of the skull does not support the alternative interpretation (see p.12) that the present Australian native  is the result of a cross between an oboriginal Australian and invading indians, if anything it is aginst such an interpretation.”
6.	Animated by a love of adventure the ancient Southern Indian left his shore by Sea to the Mediterranian regions and the Far East and colonised these regions feeling the necessity for new homes. Those who left for making the Mediterranian race, perhaps some of them might have come back to India and stayed in their original homes. We must not forget that there was also a land route which led them to the Indus velley, Baluchistan, Mesopotamia and Egypt. Thus there was contact perhaps intimate contact of people and culture. These we may call tentatively the makers of and the people responsible for the Dravidian culture and Civilization” Pre-historic South India-Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshistar M.A., (Madras) 1951 p. 134.
7.	(a)	“The Tamils who have an ancient culture speak a language allied to the language of India that from the Dravidian family spoken to-day by more than 100 million people. The Dravidian belong to one of the oldest ethnic groups in India. They lived there long before the belligerent nomad tribes of aryans mentioned in the Rig-Veda the sacred book of the Hindus came to the “land of marvels”. Today the Dravidian languages are spoken in Southern India upto 18’-20’s, but they once covered central and Northern India as well. Moreover facts show that several thousand years ago the Dravidian languages were also spoken in Baluchistan and Southern Iran. The Dravidians may have been the first settle in the Tigris and Euphraties area preceding the sumerians whose civilization is regarded as the oldest in the world” - The riddles of three oceans A Kondratov (Moscow) 1974. P. 132.
	(b)	The features which the proto Indian culture and the Mesopotamian culture have in common may quite possibly be explined by the fact that the people who created the oldest Indian civilization and the first men to develop the valley of the Tigris and Euphrates were cognate peoples speaking Dravidian languages. Or perhaps they were simply one and the same people. It is also possible that the Dravidian languages may have been common to other peoples besides the ubids and the Proto-Indians.
	(c)	The regions east of the Tigris in Iran called Khuzistan was once known as Elam. A civilization flourished there 5,000 years ago with citystates a distinctive culture and a written language scholars find that the culture of the Elamites had many features in common with that of Mesopotamia and even more so with the proto-Indian culture”. 
Ibid p. 143.
	(d)	It is fully possible that Elamite and the ubid languages branched off from the common Dravidian stock at an early date, and this explins the similarities and the differences between them. There might be another explanation. The Dravidian languages, the language of the ubids who preceded the sumerians, and Elamite might all go back to a more remote common language Ibid - 145.
	(e)	“... Dravidian ethnic type of India that the Sumerians bear most resemblance, so far as we can judge from his monuments. He was very like a Southern Hindu of the Dekkan (who still speaks Dravidian languages) and it is by no means impossible that the sumerians were an Indian race which passed certinly by land, perhaps also by sea through Persia to the valley of the two rivers. It was in the Indian Home (perhaps the Indus valley) that their writing may have been invented and progressed from a purely pictorial to a simplified and abbrevated form which  afterwards in Babylonia took on its peculiar uniform appearance owing to its being written with in a square ended stilus on soft clay. On the way they left the seeds of culture in Elam ..... there is little doubt that India must have been one of the earliest centres of civilization and it seems natural to suppose that the strange unsemitic, unaryan people who came from the east to civillse the west were of Indian origin specially when we see with our eyes how very Indian the Sumerian were in type. “F.N.” The Civilization was not Aryan. The culture of India is pre Aryan in origin as in Greece the conquered civilized conqerors. The Aryan India owed its civilization and its degeneration to the Dravidians as the Aryan Greek did to Myceneans” - An Ancient History of the Near East - R.H.Hall.
8.	The earliest stone age culture of India is represented by the hand axe technique of Madras and the old stone age people may have migrated from South India into Central India where in the Narbada valley, have been found middle pleistocene tools and a fauna gradually extended through the Ganges and Jamuna valleys to North Western India right upto Himalayan Hills” - India and the Pacific world - Kalidas Nag p. 279.
9.	(a)	The Ethnic and other affinities between South India and the Mediterranean basin must be due to the fact that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of northern and Mediterranean nations proceeded to the different parts of the globe which they now inhabit (Dr.E.Maclean) it is interesting that the Indians of North America and ancient Egyptians had a tradilon that they were immigrants and Herran was inclined to postulate the Indian orgin of Egiptions basing his theory on the form of the skull of the Egyptians. There is also a theory that the land of the Punt the original home of the Egyptians was perhaps the Pandian land including the Malabar coast. But more evidence, it is sid, is needed to confirm this”, - origin and Spread of the Tamils - Prof - V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A.. (Madras) 1947 pp.3-4.
	(b)	Hindu America-chaman Lal (Bombay) 10.94.

10. iṟuvāy
varalāṟṟut toṭakkam
 intiyāviṉ varalāṟu, intiyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟa villi. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattil intiya varalāṟṟi airōppiyar cilar eḻuta muṟpaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, avar kaḷil evarālum intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakālatti aṟuti yiṭṭurikka muṭiyavilli. cilar kirēkkavīraṉ alekcāntar intiyāviṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttu vantu intiya maṇṇil kālaṭi yeṭutta vitta ki.mu.330 ām āṇṭu mutal intiya varalāṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
varalāṟṟaṟiñar tārāciṅ intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc curukkam eṉṟa nūlil, ki.mu. 6, 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ toṭakka kālattiliruntu tāṉ intiya varalāṟu tuḷirkkiṟatu : intak kālattiltāṉ, intiyāviṉuḷ eṇṇaṟṟa putiya iṉattavarkaḷ nuḻintu intiyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu vāḻa muṟpaṭṭa ṉar; nāṭṭil putiya nākarikamum camayamum pukuntu vaḷarat talip paṭṭaṉa; avaṟṟōṭu paḻiya paṇpāṭum, camayamum puṟakkaṇikkap peṟṟaṉa; intiyāvil tali tūkkiya mutaṟpēraracu talitūkkic cāyntatu; itu nāṭṭiṉ mukkiyamāṉa varalāṟākum. intiya varalāṟu ayal nāṭṭup payaṇikaḷ (vaḻippōkkarkaḷ) eḻutiya eḻuttuc cāṉṟukaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu uruvākkap peṟṟatu. avarkaḷ aḷitta cācaṉaṅkaḷum, maṭalkaḷum kācukaḷum niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum intiya varalāṟṟi uruppeṟac ceyya utaviṉa eṉṟu eṭuttak kāṭṭiṉār.
varalāṟṟiñar cilar, ki.mu. 1500 ām āṇṭil, ayal nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu āṭumāṭukaḷi mēyttukkoṇṭu vanta nāṭōṭi makkaḷāṉa āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ puṉita pūmiyākiya intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntu intiya nāṭṭiṉ urimiyāḷarkaḷāṉa tirāviṭarkaḷi veṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷi aḻittu, avarkaḷiṟ perumpālōrit teṉṉintiyāviṟkuḷ nerukkit taḷḷiviṭṭu, vintiya malikku vaṭapakutiyil āriyā varttam, pirammavarttam eṉṉum peyaril taṉi nāṭukaḷi amittu intiyāvip piḷantaṉar. puṉitamāṉa tirāviṭa intiyāvil piriviṉi eṉṉum naccu vitti ūṉṟiṉar. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil pukuntu āriyā varttattiṟku aṭikōliya avala nāḷē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka nāḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ; eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa eḻuti yuḷḷārkaḷ.
cintu veḷiyil intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉ akaḻ āyvu arumpiyatu. 1930ām āṇṭu vari ōraḷavu muṭi vuṟṟatu. 1932ām āṇṭu car. cāṇ mārcal mokañcatārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa mūṉṟu tokutikaḷik koṇṭa arum perum nūlit tiṟampeṟa veḷiyiṭṭār. annūliṉ mūlam cintuveḷiyil tirāviṭap pēraracu cīruṭaṉum ciṟappuṭaṉum oḷi viṭṭōṅkiyatu. ki.mu. 1500kkum ki.mu. 2000kkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattil ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pōnta āriyarkaḷāl tirāviṭa intiyap pēraracu tākkappaṭṭu vīḻttappeṟṟatu. paḻiya tali nakaramāṉa arappāvum, putiya talinakaramāṉa mokañcatārōvum aḻikkap peṟṟaṉa. aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷil palar teṉ intiyā viṟkut taḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. cintuveḷiyil oru vakic cempu ūḻiyil (Chalcolishic age) āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭu nilaviyatu. aṅku vāḻnta tirāviṭamakkaḷ ulakilē oppaṟṟa nākarikatti uruvāk kiṉar. aṅku nilaviyatu vēḷāṇkuṭi makkaḷiṉ aracu. avvaracu uḻavut toḻilil uyariya veṟṟiyi īṭṭiyatu. annāṭṭiṉar kaḷiṉ camayam civam; teyvam civaperumāṉ. umātēvi, murukaṉ, āṭavallāṉ pōṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷum pala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. annāṭṭiṉar tam moḻiyip pēṇivantaṉar. ōviya eḻuttik kaṇṭu ēṭukaḷ pala eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar. ayalnāṭukaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki vaṇikam vaḷarttu vantaṉar. cintuveḷiyil, nantāp pēroḷi yāy nilaviya tirāviṭap pēraraci vīḻtta, āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil nuḻinta kālamē, intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka kālam eṉṟu cila varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟku varumuṉ avarkaḷukku eḻutat teriyātu. āṉāl, avarkaḷ intiyāvil nuḻivataṟku muṉpē cintuveḷit tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷ eḻutap payiṉṟirun taṉar. āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷiṭamiruntē eḻutak kaṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ moḻi tiruttappeṟṟatu. eṉavē atu cemmi peṟṟa moḻi (camakkirutam) eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟatu. camakkirutam eṉṟa collukkut tirutti amikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. eḻuttu arumpiyapiṉ varalāṟu arumpukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttukkēṟpa āriyarkaḷ vantu tirāviṭa intiyāvic cīr kulitta kālamākiya ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka kālam eṉṟu varalāṟṟu aṟiñar cilar kaṇṭaṉar. paṇṭiya intiya varalāṟum nākarikamum paṇpāṭum eṉṟa nūlil (pak. 54) tiru.pi.ecu.cōki. em.ē. avarkaḷum ēṉiya mūṉṟu kūṭṭu āciriyarkaḷum eḻutiya ciṟanta nūlil āriyarkaḷ intiyā viṉ kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟa kālattiliruntu intiya varalāṟu toṭaṅku kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟi, mēṟkaṇṭa kūṟṟi valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷaṉar.
ulakilē eḻuttut tōṉṟi vaḷarcci peṟṟa piṉṉarē vara lāṟṟi variya muṭiyum. cintuveḷiyil āriyar pukunta kālattil eḻuttukaḷ iruntaṉa. āṉāl, avi vaḷarcci peṟṟirukkavilli. varalāṟu variyattakka niliyi aṭiyavilli. eṉavē aṟiñarkaḷ akkālatti varalāṟṟukkālam eṉṟu kūṟātu varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakālam (Proto Historie Period) eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. mēlum cintuveḷiyil oru vaki maṇṇum cempum kalanta ūḻi (Chal-colithic age) eḻutuvataṟku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa ātitta nallūril ki.mu.8000-10,000 ām āṇṭaḷavil irumpu ūḻi tuḷirttu uyariya eḵkup paṇpāṭu malarntiruntatu. aṅku, cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ mūtātiyar tōṉṟi uruveḻuttu oliyeḻuttu mutaliya palavakikaḷik kaṇṭu, ēṭum eḻuttāṇiyum kikaḷil tāṅki, eḻutum vāyppip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. eṉavē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ mūlam taṇ poruni yāṟṟiṉ paṇpāṭṭil, teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa ātittanallūriliruntu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉalām. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl intiya varalāṟṟu, ki.mu. 8000ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ ātittanallūr irumpup paṇ pāṭṭil - atāvatu teṉṉintiyā vil tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum.
cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ
potuvāka airōppiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ, āriyarkaḷippōl ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu intiyāvil, āriyar kaḷukkumuṉ cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟip piṉ, āriyarkaḷāl teṉ ṉintiyāviṟku viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷē eṉṟu eṇṇi vantaṉar. cintuveḷiyiṉi akaḻntu aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi naṉku alaci ārāynta car-cāṇ mārcal avarkaḷ kūṭa nākarika mutircci peṟṟa cumēriyarkaḷ cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ik karutti vēṟu cila āciriyarkaḷum ātarittu vantuḷḷaṉar.
tirāviṭarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē
ulakam tōṉṟiya kālam mutal tamiḻarkaḷ teṉṉin tiyāvil tōṉṟip paravi vaḷarntu paṭippaṭiyāka nākarikam peṟṟu varupavar kaḷāvar. eṉavē tāṉ cermaṉ nāṭṭu nila nūṟ pulavar erṉacuṭ ekkal teṉ intiyā maṉitavarkkattiṉ toṭṭil eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
aṟiñar kāḷitācu nēk intiyāvum pacipik ulakamum eṉṟa nūlil, teṉṉintiyāvil paḻaṅkal ūḻiyic cērnta makkaḷ nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟut teṟkiṉiṉṟu vaṭakku nōkki vantu miya intiyā vari - ēṉ? kaṅki yamuṉi veḷi - vaṭamēṟku intiyā vari ceṉṟu imaya maliyiṉ aṭivārattilum vaṭakiḻakku intiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar. ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭil tirāviṭarkaḷ pāracīkam (īrāṉ), īrāk, ekiptu, kirīṭ, cippiracu nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
uruciya nāṭṭu aṟiñar alekcāntar kontārat tōḷ mūṉṟu mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil, tamiḻarkaḷ paḻam perum paṇpāṭṭip paṭittavarkaḷ; iṉṟum avarkaḷ paḻam perum tirāviṭa moḻiyip pēci varukiṉṟārkaḷ. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, avar teṉṉintiyāviṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭṭu naṭuvaṇ intiyāvilum vaṭintiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭintiyāvil tirāviṭa moḻi payilappeṟṟu vantuḷḷatu. tirāviṭa moḻi paluccicuttāṉam īrāṉ īrāk nāṭukaḷilellām paravi iruntatu. tirāviṭarkaḷ īrākkil kuṭiyēṟi, kaṉṉi nilaṅkaḷi uḻutu payiriṭṭu naṉiciṟanta nākarikaṅkaḷi vaḷarttuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ, īrākkil (cumēriyāvil) vittiṭṭu vaḷartta tamiḻ nākarikamē ulakiluḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ mikat toṉmi vāynta nākarikamākavum uyariya nākarikamāvum oppaṟṟa nākarikamākavum pativu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
muṉivar eyc ār. āl (Dr. H.R. Hall) eṉṉum pēraṟiñar, cintuveḷi akaḻāyvu toṭaṅku muṉpē tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ palvakip paṇpukaḷ niṟinta meymmic ceytikaḷi ulakiṟku uvantaḷittaṉar; cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷē īrākkil kuṭiyēṟi cumēriyar eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil iruntu tamiḻar cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit tirāviṭar āyiṉar eṉṟu kaṇ ṭār. tirāviṭarkaḷ nīrvaḻiyālum nilavaḻiyālum pāracīkattiṉ vaḻiyāka tikkirīcu, iyūp piraṭṭīcu āṟukaḷiṉ paḷḷattākkukaḷi aṭintaṉar. avarkaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyil ellā, nāṭṭil intiyap paṇpāṭṭiṉ vittukkaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭup pōyirukkalām. cumēriyarkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum teṉ intiya makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum oṉṟupōlavē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cumēriyarkaḷ cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷāyum cintuveḷi makkaḷ teṉ intiyat tamiḻarkaḷākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa karuttukkaḷi pērāciriyar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyā eṉṟa nūlil tiṟampaṭa viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
muṉivar mōrkaṉ pirañcu moḻiyil ekiptu nāṭṭiṉ ēṟṟamum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku mukkiya āciyāvum eṉṟu varinta nūlil, miya nilak kaṭaṟ pakutikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ mūlat tāyakamākavum, kaliyūṟṟākavum viḷaṅkuvatu teṉ inti yāvē eṉṟu nāṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭamākavum uṟutiyākavum kūṟa muṭiyum eṉṟu ciṟappuṭaṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam, tirāviṭarkaḷ vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟku vantaṉar eṉpatu tavaṟāṉa koḷkiyākum eṉpati aṟiyalām. teṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tari vaḻiyākavum kaṭal vaḻiyākavum cintuveḷi, ellam, (īrāṉ) cumēriyā (īrāṉ) ekiptu (micir) kirīṭ, amerikkā, āppirikkā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām kuṭiyēṟip payirttoḻil vaḷarttu, kittoḻilkaḷip perukki, moḻi, eḻuttup paṇ pāṭu, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟi uruvākki vaḷarttup pukaḻīṭṭiya tāy iṉam eṉṟu eṇṇaṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar.
āriyarkaḷ cumār ki.mu. 2003ām āṇṭaḷavil intiyāviṟ kuḷ vantavarākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. irāmāyaṇap pōr ki.mu.1500 ām āṇṭiyoṭṭi nikaḻntatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. namatu intiya nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉāḷ mutal amiccarum varalāṟṟu nūṟ pulavarumāṉa paṇṭitar cavakarlāl nēru avarkaḷ tam makaḷukku eḻutiya kaṭitam oṉṟil irāmāyaṇam pōr āriya - tirāviṭap pōr eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār. irāmaṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṟku varum poḻutu pāṇṭiyarkaḷ koṟkiyil kapāṭapurattil kōṭṭi eḻuppip poṟkatavukaḷ pūṭṭiyum iṭic caṅkam niṟuviyum tamiḻ vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kuraṅkuk kuṭumpattic cērnta vāliyum avaṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷum aracu niṟuvi araṇmaṉi kaṭṭic ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantaṉar. civaperumāṉik kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu aṟavaḻikaḷil pōr āṟṟi vantaṉar. irāvaṇaṉ puṭpaka vimāṉattiṉ mūlam pōkkuvarattu naṭatti vantāṉ. avaṉatu araṇmaṉi eḻunili māṭamāy eḻil peṟṟilaṅkiyatu. araṇ maṉi pavaḷat tūṇkaḷum paṭikak kalpaṭikaḷum uṭiyatāy eḻil peṟṟu ilaṅkiyatu. irāvaṇaṉ civapaktaṉāka iruntu poṉṉāl ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷi vittu vaḻipaṭṭu vantāṉ; māṟṟāṉ maṉiviyit teṉṉintiya maṉṉarkaḷ marapu muṟippaṭi ciṟi piṭittu vantālum, avaḷi araca kitiyāy acōka vaṉattil ōr araṇmaṉiyil vittu, taṉ tampi vipīṭaṇaṉ makaḷ tiricaṭiyit tuṇiyāka irukkac ceytu, teṉṉāṭṭup paṇpu tiṟampā vaṇṇam naṭantu koṇṭāṉ - irāvaṇaṉ ici vallōṉ - yāḻ eṉṉum teyvīka icik karuviyi mīṭṭuvatil iṇiyaṟṟava ṉāyt tikaḻntāṉ. irāvaṇaṉ maṉivi maṇṭōtari, maṇivācakap perumāṉāl civaperumāṉiṉ nallaruḷip peṟṟa kaṟpukkaraci eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭupavaḷ. mēlum tamiḻakattil nilaviya uyar nalaṅkaḷellām cintuveḷiyiṉiṉṟu āriyarkaḷāl viraṭṭap paṭṭa tirāviṭarkaḷ teṉ intiyā pōntu oru cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷil periya nāṭukaḷ nakaraṅkaḷ kaṭṭi, moḻi vaḷarttu uyarnta paṇpukaḷi nilavacceyya muṭiyumā? muṭiyavē muṭiyātu. tirāviṭarkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟkālam mutal tamiḻakattil vāḻntu putiyakaṟ kālattit tāṇṭi ulōka kālatti aṭintu ulakilē mutaṉ mutalākap poṉṉik kaṇṭu atit tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭutti vantatōṭu irumpik kaṇṭu ati naṉku payaṉpaṭutti ēṟṟam aṭintuḷḷaṉar. ataṉōṭu tamiḻarkaḷ putiya kaṟkālattilē mali toṭuttuk kaṭalvari paraviya nāṉilam amittu mēṉili eytiyum, irumpup paṇpāṭṭi naṉku payaṉpaṭuttip payir vaḷarttum toḻil vaḷarttum kappal kaṭṭik kaṭal vaṇikam vaḷarttum vantuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmi.
itukāṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷāṉa poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliya vaṟṟip paṟṟiyum maṭkalam, tāḻikaḷ, taliyōṭukaḷ ākiyavi kaḷōṭu kūlap poruḷkaḷ, pañcāṭikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟiyum nammuṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ mūlam oruvāṟu naṉkaṟintirup pīrkaḷ. iḵtivvāṟirukka ātittanallūrip paṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ciṟappāka ituvari eṭuttuk kūṟavilli.
tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattil paṇi yāṟṟi vanta nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār kūṭa makkaḷit tici tiruppi viṭum muṟiyil iruntālum ātitta nallūr eṉṟa peyari 3,4 iṭaṅkaḷil teṉ intiyāviṉ varalāṟu (A History of South India) eṉṟa tam nūlil ēṟattāḻa 6 pakkaṅkaḷukkumēl celaviṭṭut tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟu āyviṟku iṭar viḷittirukkiṟār. atiliruntu cāttiriyār ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil paḻamporuḷkaḷ oṉṟum illi eṉṟu collāmal viṭṭuviṭṭārē eṉṟu viyappaṭikiṉṟēṉ. āṉāl, oru cila āciriyar ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil kiṭitta oru maṇṭiyōṭu ācuttirēliya nīkrō yiṭ maṇṭiyōṭu pōl irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya karutti uṭkoṇṭu mīṇṭum ati eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. (See A. History of South India. pp. 53, 55, 57) ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṇṭiyōṭu ācuttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭāka irukka muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭṭil oṉṟākavē irukka vēṇṭum. pēraṟiñar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya teṉ intiyā eṉṟa nūlil mika virivāka āyntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. ivaratu karuttukkaḷi cōviyat nāṭṭiṉ uyiriyal tuṟi vallunar muṉivar pērāciriyar mikayil necutturk maṉita iṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil, maṇṭiyōṭṭi vittu maṉita iṉaṅkaḷit tirāviṭar, āriyar, nīkrōvar eṉṟu pirittaṟivatu muṭiyāta kāriyam eṉṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvatōṭu, iṉap pirivu eṉṟa tattuvattiṉ aṭip paṭiyē tavaṟu eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
cāttiriyār veḷiyiṭṭa tavaṟāṉa varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ
varalāṟṟu āciriyar oru nāṭṭippaṟṟiyō, ōr iṉattip paṟṟiyō eḻutumpoḻutu annāṭṭiṟkup pōy aṅkuḷḷa nakaram, vīṭu, iṉam, iṭipāṭukaḷ, akaḻāyvukaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, karuvikaḷ kācukaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cevivaḻi marapuc ceytikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi nēril pārttum, cevipaṭuttum naṉku āyntum muṭipiṟku varavēṇṭum. itaṟkut tiru.kē.ē. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār vitivilakku. ivar varalāṟṟiṟkuriya iṭattip pārppatō, akaḻ āyvupporuḷkaḷi nōkkuvatō kiṭiyātu. eṭuttukkāṭ ṭākat tamiḻk kalik kaḷañciyam āṟāvatu tokutiyiṉ mutaṟ patippil tirunelvēli eṉṟa talippil oru kaṭṭuri eḻuti yuḷḷār. ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvip paṟṟic cila varikaḷ eḻuti viṭṭu, itaṉ kālam ki.mu.mutalāyiram āṇṭu eṉṟum ivi kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakka nūṟṟāṇṭuvari nīṭittiruntaṉa veṉṟum oru tavaṟāṉa ceytiyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṟku avar evvitac cāṉṟum kāṭṭavilli. mēlum ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷi avar pārttatum illi. atip paṟṟi ārāycci ceyta tol āyvu vallunarkaḷ karutti avar eṉṟum nōkkiyatum illi. atōṭu avar piṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttiṟka matippuk koṭuppavarākavum teriyavilli. attuṭaṉ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya eḵkup paṇpāṭu eṉpatik kūṭak karuttuṭ koṇṭa tākat teriya villi.
mokañcatārōvi akaḻntu āyntavar vaṅkap pērā ciriyar irākēl tācup pāṉarcci (R.D. Banerji) eṉṟa nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam vāynta varalāṟṟup pulavar āvar. avarukku nalla akaḻāyvut tuṟip paṭippum, paṭṭaṟivum uṇṭu. avar, ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu irumpu ūḻiyil eḻunta irumpup paṇpāṭu; atu teṉ intiyāviluḷḷa kumarimuṉiyiṉiṉṟu toṭarntu, aṟupaṭāta caṅkilit toṭarpōl arappāvari nīṇṭu, paluc cicuttāṉ vaḻiyāy pakriṉ tīvakatti ūṭuruvi, teṟku īrāṉ, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiyellām kaṭantu kirīṭ tīvu vari ceṉṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu 1927il tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟa toṭar kaṭṭuriyil virivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattiṉ intiya varalāṟṟut tuṟi tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟi maṉita iṉa āyvuttuṟi ākiyavikaḷiṉ pērā ciriyar, vi.ār.irāmaccantira tīṭcitar 1950-51il nikaḻttiya car ulliyam meyyar coṟpoḻivukaḷil (Sir. William Meyer Lectures, 1950-1951) ātitta nallūr irumpup paṇpāṭu teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟip paravi vaṭintiyā muḻuvatiyum kavarnta ēṟṟamikum tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu ākum. itu ulakil utitta uyariya oppaṟṟa paṇpāṭu ākum. itaṉ kālam ki.mu.8,000 mutal 10,000 vari irukkalām eṉṟu aḻuttam tiruttamāka aṟintuḷḷār. ataṟkuppiṉ ātitta nallūrip paṟṟi eḻuta muṉvanta kayavar kē.ē.nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār, takka ātāram kāṭṭi ati maṟut tirukka vēṇṭum. allatu ati ēṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. aḵtiṉṟi ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu ki.mu. mutalāyiram āṇṭiliruntu kiṟittava cakāptattiṉ toṭakka nūṟṟāṇṭuvari nilavi irukka lām eṉṟu ātāramaṟṟa kayavāḷik karuttukkaḷit terivittatu ēṉ eṉṟu eṉakkup puriyavilli. ivarukkumuṉ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu mika mikat toṉmiyāṉatu eṉṟa karutti veḷiyiṭṭa pērāciriyar ār.ṭi.pāṉarcciyum, pērāciriyar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum ivariviṭa evvitattilum kuṟintavarkaḷallarē!. ivaratu poyk kūṟṟiṉik kalikkaḷañciyattil iṭam peṟac ceytatu tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakattiṉ kuṟṟamēyākum. aṭutta patippil ikkaruttukkaḷ akaṟṟappaṭṭu uṟutiyāṉa nalla karut tukkaḷ iṭam peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakattip paṇivuṭaṉ vēṇṭik koḷkiṟēṉ.
mēlum nīlakaṇṭacāttiriyār iṉṉum tamiḻar varalāṟṟut tuṟiyil pukuntu pala tavaṟukaḷi iḻittuḷḷār. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka aṭiyil varum oru ceytiyi maṭṭum iṅku kūṟi iti muṭittukkoḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ.
nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār kalikkaḷañciyattil tirunelvēli eṉṟa talippil oru kaṭṭuri eḻutiyuḷḷār. atil pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭi eṉṉum ūrippaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atil cila tavaṟāṉa takavalkaḷi aḷittirukkiṉṟār. atāvatu, nelli māvaṭṭattil patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi yil, oru araṇ iruntatu. atuvē maturikkut teṟkē mikunta vaṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntatu. āṉāl ati nirūpikkakkūṭiya citivukaḷ (cāṉṟukaḷ) etuvum kāṇpataṟ killi eṉṟa karuttup peṟa eḻutiyuḷḷār.
tiru. cāttiriyār nelli māvaṭṭattil piṟantavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ati vittē nelli māvaṭṭattiṉ ciṟanta ūrāka viḷaṅkum pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭiyippaṟṟi ivari eḻutu māṟu kalikkaḷañciyattiṉ patippu āciriyar kuḻu ivarik kēṭ ṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ mutaṟ perum palkalikkaḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟuttuṟip pērāciriyar pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭikku varāmalē, allatu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi yil vāḻum makkaḷi vicārikkāmalē iṅku kōṭṭiyiṉ citivukaḷ etuvum kāṇpataṟkilli eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppati aṟintu nellimāvaṭṭa makkaḷum, varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum - ēṉ? paḷḷic ciṟārkaḷum pāmaramakkaḷum nakikkiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷiṉ pulamikku iḵtu ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. ittakiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kallūrikaḷilum, palkalikkaḻakattilum iruntāl makkaḷukku varalāṟṟil nampikki uṇṭākumā? pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭiyil irunta māperum araṇi iṭittapiṉ ataṉ niṉivāka, (1) pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi pēruntu niliyattiṉ aṇmiyil tiruvaṉantapuram cāliyil oru citivuṇṭa pakuti uḷḷatu. ataṉmītu kaṭṭapommaṉ cili niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷatu. maṟṟōr araṇiṉ periya pakuti, nakar maṉṟattiṉ piṉpuṟam uḷḷatu. ataṉuḷ piḷḷiyār kōyil uḷḷatu. mēlē kāval niliyam uḷḷatu. itu kōṭṭippiḷḷiyār kōyil eṉappaṭum. maṟṟoru pakuti, kōṭṭip piḷḷiyār kōyilil iruntu nērākak kiḻakkunōkkic cellum cāliyil ēṟakkuṟiya 200 mīṭṭar tolivil nakarmaṉṟa nīrttoṭṭikku (Water tank) aṇmiyil oru periya citivu irukkiṟatu. itaṉ aṭiyilum mēṟpakutiyilum namatu araciṉ kāṭṭut tuṟi (Forest Department) iruntu varukiṟatu. itaṉ vaṭakkē ēṟattāḻa 120 mīṭṭar toli vil maṟṟoru citivu iruntu varukiṟatu. nāṉkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutikaḷ kuṉṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟip pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭiyil uḷḷa paḷḷicciṟārkaḷum aṟivar. iṅkuc citivu kaḷē illi eṉṟu eḻutum āciriyarip paṟṟi emmiṭam oru paḷḷi āciriyi piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. aiyarukku yāṉi pārkka veḷḷeḻuttā? eṉṟu kūṟum paḻamoḻikku nīlakaṇṭar ilak kākiyuḷḷār eṉṟu paṇpōṭu ivaratu tavaṟi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. paḻutuṟṟa kaṇkaḷāl, vayatu mutircci yāl oru viṭayatti eḻutumuṉ atippaṟṟi āyntō vicārittō eḻutātu viṭṭa, māṟupaṭṭa karuttuṭiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu eppaṭit tamiḻar uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟṟuc ceytiyi etirpārkka muṭiyum? mēlum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa varalāṟṟiyō, paṇpāṭṭiyō, nākarikattiyō nīlakaṇṭacāttiri pōṉṟa kayavarkaḷiṭam tamiḻarkaḷ etirpārppatē tavaṟākum eṉpatu namatu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipākum.
tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻar neṟi, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, tamiḻmoḻi ākiyavaṟṟi virumpātavarkaḷālum kāḻppuk koṇṭavarkaḷā lum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kuṟippāka, tiru.nīlakaṇṭacāttiriyār pōṉṟavarkaḷtamiḻarkaḷukkup perun teyvamē kiṭiyātu; murukaṉkūṭa āriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉākappeṟṟa teyvam eṉṟu pēciya pakuti tamiḻnāṭṭut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi veḷiyīṭukaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻarkaḷ, moḻiyiyō, paṇpāṭṭiyō, teyvattiyō, camayattiyō evarkaḷiṭa miruntum kaṭaṉākavō  iravalākavō eṉṟum peṟṟatilli. tamiḻarkaḷ tām taṅkaḷ moḻi, paṇpāṭu, teyvam, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟi ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu pēraṟiñarkaḷ muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itiyaṟiyātu tiru. cāttiriyār intiyāviṉ orumippāṭu, karuttu cutantiram eṉṟa peyarāl tamiḻarkaḷukku iḻittuḷḷa tīmikaḷi kālam kiṭṭumpōtu viḷakkik kāṭṭavōm.
putiya aṟivu oḷi
muṭipāka ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ eṇṇila eṉpati niṉivil koḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṅkuk kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ matippiṭaṟkariya viliyuyarnta karuvūlaṅkaḷ. aṅkuk kiṭitta poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷum veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum irumpup poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum tāḻikaḷum tamiḻar kaḷukkum, ēṉ? intiyāviṟkum perumi aḷippaṉa vākum. tamiḻarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uyarnta nākarikattit taṇporuniyāṟṟup paṇpāṭṭi ulakiṟku naṉkoṭiyāka aḷitta perumakkaḷ eṉpataṟku nalla cāṉṟākat tikaḻkiṟatu. ātittanallūril eḻunta viyattakum oppaṟṟa paṇpāṭṭi nūṟāṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ulakamum, ulakap pēraṟiñarkaḷum aṟiyaviṭātu ceytatupōl mīṇṭum mīṇṭum oru pulluruvik kūṭṭam, īṉat toḻilil īṭupaṭṭu varukiṟatu. inniliyil ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu mīṇṭum makkaḷ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvarap paṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpatu īṇṭu niṉivu kūṟattakkatu.
ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭiṉmītu kavintuḷḷa iruḷ paṭalaṅkaḷ aṉittum akaṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. ataṉmītu putiya aṟivoḷi vīcat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. ataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ulakam etirpārā vaṇṇam ātittanallūrum poruniveḷi nākarikamum (Adhitha Nallur and Poruni Valley Civilization) eṉṟa innūl veḷivantuḷḷatu.
cintuveḷic cempu nākarikam ki.mu.6000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ nilaviya tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭiṟku aḷaviṭaṟkariya matippi aḷit tuḷḷatu. atu tirāviṭa nākarikantāṉ ulakil oppaṟṟa paḻamperum nākarikam eṉpati ulakam ēṟṟuk koḷḷac ceytatu. āṉāl tavaṟākac cila aṟiñarkaḷāl cintuveḷi nākarikam miyanilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷ intiyāviṟku vantu tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟa peyarāl uruvākkappeṟṟa nākarikam eṉṟu kāṇappeṟṟatu. cintu veḷiyiṉiṉṟu āriyarkaḷāl viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷē teṉṉin tiyāvil iṉṟu vāḻum makkaḷ eṉṟu karuta iṭam aḷittatu.
ātittanallūr irumpu nākarikam ki.mu.8000 ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ ulakil nilaviya oppaṟṟa nākarikamākum. inta irumpup paṇpāṭṭil ulakil poruniveḷi nākarikattiṉ mukam tōṉ ṟiyatu. itaṉ payaṉākap payirttoḻilil periya muṉṉēṟṟam eḻuntatu. kit toḻilkaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi virintu kiḷittu muḷitteḻuntaṉa. nāṭṭil makkaḷiṉ tēvikku miñciya viḷi poruḷkaḷum, ceyporuḷkaḷum mali maliyāka eṅkum kuvintu kiṭantaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭut tuṟimukaṅkaḷil nāvāykaḷum, kalaṉkaḷum, vaṅkamum eṇṇilātu malintu kiṭantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ palaṉākak kaṭal vaṇikam piṟantatu. tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭal vaṇikar tam kappalkaḷil tam nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭu toli vil uḷḷa el, upit, ellam, cumēriyā, akkēṭiyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ, amerikkā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar. kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ ulakameṅ kum taṅkaḷ vaṇikam, kaṇitam, camayam, moḻi, teyvam, nākarikam, eḻuttu mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḷarttaṉar. paṇṭiyat tamiḻarkaḷ ulakam eṅkum paraviṉar.
intiya orumippāṭṭukku araṇceyyum nākarikam
teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟiya tamiḻarkaḷ paḻaṅkal ūḻi, putukkal ūḻi, irumpu ūḻiyil vāḻntu nākarikam peṟṟu, vaṭa intiyā muḻuvatilum paraviyatōṭu nila vaḻiyākavum, nīr vaḻiyākavum ulakameṅkum paraviṉārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ātitta nallūr nākarikattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vaṭintiyā īrāṉ, īrākku, ekiptu, kirīṭ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmi nilināṭṭap peṟukiṉṟatu.
itu intiya nāṭṭiṟkup perumi aḷippatōṭu intiya orumippāṭṭiṟku ātittanallūr nākarikam ōr araṇāka amintuḷḷatu eṉpatu teriyavarukiṉṟatu. intiyar maṭṭumalla ulaka makkaḷ yāvarum tāṅkaḷ aṉivarum ōriṉam, ōr kiṟi eṉṟa uyariya koḷki valuppaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.
viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ
ip pakutiyil ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭa poṉ, irumpu, veṇkalam ākiyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭap poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ; tāḻikaḷ, maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ ākiyaṉavum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟukkuriya paṭaṅkaḷ nūluḷ āṅkāṅkē iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ (Diadems)1
ivi potuvāka nīḷ uruṇṭi vaṭivam uḷḷavi. ov voṉṟiṉ iṟutivari nīṇṭa varikkōṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta varikaḷiṉ muṭivil tuḷikaḷ iṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. it tuḷikaḷ paṭṭattik kayiṟu allatu kampiyil kōttu neṟṟi yil kaṭṭuvataṟkāka iṭappaṭṭavi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perum pālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷil varikkōṭukaḷ iṟutiyil kāṇappaṭa villi. intap poṟpaṭaṅkaḷ mika melliyaṉavāy iruppatāl nīḷ uruṇṭi vaṭivil valappuṟamum iṭappuṟamum eḷitil kampiyiṉāl kuttit tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭu vantatākat terikiṟatu. cilavaṟṟil kōṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mukkōṇavaṭivil piṉpuṟamākap puḷḷipuḷḷiyāka aḻutti aṇiceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ivaṟṟuḷ āṟu paṭṭaṅkaḷ aṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭātaviyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ip poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya muḻuviḷakkamum paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭil poṉ eppaṭiyellām tamiḻarkaḷāl payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu eṉṟa varalāṟum innūluḷ 76ām pakkam mutal 94ām pakkam muṭiyak kāṇalām.
piṟa puti poruḷkaḷ
maṟṟum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa vaki vakiyāṉa 33 veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ, īṭṭi, koṭuvāḷ, katti pōṉṟa palvēṟu irumpuk karuvikaḷ 97 vakiyāṉa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ, maṇṭiyōṭukaḷ ākiyaṉa paṟṟiya viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷ nūluḷ āṅkāṅkē iṇikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.


1.	These are locally known as Pattam and the system of putting gold in some shapes or other over the dead body is prevalent in orthodox Hindu homes even now a days. Dr. Sir John Marshall makes the following note on these objects (Annual Report of the Archelogical Survey of India, 1902-3 Footnote on page 105):- “The gold frontlets are of thin gold leaf so flimsy that they could not possibly have been used as jewellery in real life. They are mere imitations of the genuine article subsituted by the relations of the dead who no doubt kept the more substancial jewellery for themselves. For similar instances of this economical piety towards the dead, see Frazer Pausanias, vol III page 107. Schleimann Myvenarl page 156. E. Gardner, New Chantere
